<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：主动视觉观察能力评估（17个任务，3大类）；应用场景：通用视觉-语言理解；传感器类型：自然图像；目标类别：无特定目标类别。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 评估对象为多模态大语言模型（MLLMs），包括GPT-5.5、Claude Fable 5等。论文本身不提出新模型架构，而是设计基准测试。摘要未说明具体模型结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等内容。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ActiveVision（新提出的基准测试，17个任务，3大类）；评价指标：任务解决率（GPT-5.5最高10.6%，Claude Fable 5仅3.5%，人类平均96.1%）。无遥感数据集。

Experiments: 主要结果：GPT-5.5在最高推理努力层级下仅解决10.6%的任务，在17个任务中有11个得零分；Claude Fable 5仅解决3.5%；人类参与者平均96.1%。即使模型编写并运行自己的视觉代码，差距仍然很大。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供遥感在轨目标检测轻量化的局限性分析。摘要未说明该基准测试在遥感场景下的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点：论文提出的"主动视觉观察"概念（闭环视觉感知）对遥感在轨场景有一定启发——遥感卫星在轨连续观测时，模型可能需要根据中间检测结果动态调整注意力或观测策略，而非单次前向推理。但这种借鉴非常间接，且论文本身不提供任何可复现的轻量化或检测技术。

Abstract: Human vision is a closed loop: gaze is continuously redirected by intermediate hypotheses rather than a single snapshot. Decades of psychophysics and cognitive science have argued that this active observation is essential for a wide range of tasks. Whether today's multimodal large language models (MLLMs) exercise active observation is an empirical question that current vision-language benchmarks do not answer. We introduce ActiveVision, a benchmark that makes active observation measurable for MLLMs, comprising 17 tasks across 3 categories. Tasks are designed to force repeated visual perception rather than a single static description. Frontier MLLMs collapse on ActiveVision: the highest-scoring model we evaluate, GPT-5.5 at the highest exposed reasoning-effort tier, solves only 10.6% of items and scores zero on 11 of the 17 tasks, and even Claude Fable 5, despite topping most reasoning and coding leaderboards, solves just 3.5%, far behind three human participants who average 96.1%. Furthermore, much of the gap persists even when models write and run their own vision code: such code is unreliable on realistic imagery, and catching its failures itself requires the active perception the models lack. Together, these results indicate that current MLLMs lack robust active visual observation, motivating architectures and training objectives that close the perception-reasoning loop.

</details>


### [70] [Toward Semantic Communication for Real-time Mobile 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16128)
*Fangzhou Zhao,Yao Sun,Xuesong Liu,Runze Cheng,Shang Kai,Yi Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向实时移动3D重建的语义通信框架，通过像素级置信度图引导几何估计，提升噪声信道下的位姿估计精度和3D结构一致性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于移动平台实时3D重建中的语义通信问题，不涉及遥感图像、卫星平台、目标检测任务或模型轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：实时移动3D重建（real-time mobile 3D reconstruction）；场景：自主导航（autonomous navigation）、数字孪生（digital twin construction）；传感器：图像流（image stream）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 语义通信框架（SemCom framework），包含语义收发器（semantic transceiver），输出重建图像及像素级置信度图（pixel-wise confidence map）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注通信可靠性而非边缘/星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：图像质量（image quality）、位姿估计精度（pose estimation accuracy）、3D结构一致性（3D structural consistency）。

Experiments: 与现有SemCom方法及传统分离信源信道编码（separate source and channel coding）对比，所提框架在保持高图像质量的同时显著提升位姿估计精度和3D结构一致性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 未涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 未涉及模型压缩或加速；4. 未讨论星上/边缘部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但语义通信（SemCom）中"像素级置信度图引导"的思路，若迁移到遥感在轨场景中，可考虑用于：在星地链路带宽受限时，对检测结果中置信度低的区域优先传输或重传，但与本课题核心方向（轻量化目标检测模型）关联较弱。

Abstract: Real-time mobile 3D reconstruction is fundamental to many emerging applications such as autonomous navigation and digital twin construction, where a moving platform continuously captures an image stream and transmit to a computing server for scene understanding. Unlike offline reconstruction, camera poses and scene geometry are estimated on-the-fly during acquisition, making multi-view consistency a real-time requirement and rendering geometric estimation highly sensitive to communication-induced distortions. Semantic communication (SemCom) transmits compact semantic information, offering a promising way to preserve task-critical data over unreliable links. However, existing designs are optimized at the image or single-view level and without providing explicit reliability information for geometric estimation, limiting their applicability to real-time mobile 3D reconstruction. In this context, we propose a SemCom framework for real-time mobile 3D reconstruction. The framework includes a semantic transceiver that outputs a reconstructed image alongside a pixel-wise confidence map, quantifying the reliability of each region. We further introduce a confidence-guided geometric estimation method, incorporating confidence into RANSAC-based pose initialization and bundle adjustment to reduce the influence of unreliable regions and enhance robustness under noisy channels. Simulations show that, compared to existing SemCom and traditional seperate source and channel coding, our framework maintains high image quality while significantly improving pose estimation accuracy and 3D structural consistency.

</details>


### [71] [CLIFE: Camera-LiDAR Fusion Framework for Edge-Deployable Roadside VRU Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16154)
*Tam Bang,Hoang H. Nguyen,Lei Cheng,Lihao Guo,Siyang Cao,Hussam Abubakr,Tianya Zhang,Austin Harris,Mina Sartipi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLIFE是一个面向路口弱势道路使用者感知的边缘端相机-激光雷达融合框架，在Jetson AGX Thor上实现53.2 FPS的实时推理，无需云端卸载。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。该论文聚焦于自动驾驶/智慧交通场景下的边缘端多传感器融合感知，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向弱相关。虽然涉及边缘部署、轻量化推理（Jetson嵌入式平台）、实时性（53.2 FPS），但场景是地面路口而非遥感/卫星/航空图像，传感器是相机+LiDAR而非遥感卫星常用的光学/SAR/多光谱传感器，检测对象是VRU（行人、骑行者等）而非遥感目标（舰船、飞机、车辆等）。轻量化方法（late-fusion、O(N log N)复杂度）有一定借鉴意义，但整体方向差异较大。

Reading priority: 2分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向弱相关，主要价值在于边缘部署的设计理念（全链路边缘化、late-fusion策略）有一定启发，但场景、传感器、目标类型、部署平台均不匹配，不建议作为重点阅读论文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多传感器融合感知与目标跟踪；应用场景：城市信号化路口（地面交通）；传感器类型：相机+LiDAR；目标类别：vulnerable road users (VRUs，弱势道路使用者)；与遥感无关。

Model architecture: CLIFE框架包含三个核心模块：(1) targetless online calibration（无目标在线标定），自适应精化相机-LiDAR对齐；(2) lightweight late-fusion tracking（轻量级后融合跟踪）；(3) 多传感器融合与轨迹关联，每帧计算复杂度为O(N log N)。融合策略为late-fusion（后融合），即在各传感器独立检测后再进行融合。

Lightweight method: 轻量化主要体现在：(1) 采用late-fusion策略而非early-fusion，降低计算开销；(2) 每帧计算复杂度为O(N log N)；(3) 完全在单块嵌入式设备（Jetson AGX Thor）上运行，无需云端卸载。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等经典模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 部署平台：Jetson AGX Thor（NVIDIA嵌入式AI计算平台）；推理速度：53.2 FPS（late-fusion核心），满足实时性要求；完全边缘端运行，无云端卸载；带宽开销低（减少传输带宽需求）；能耗、参数量、FLOPs、显存等指标摘要未说明。注意：该平台算力远高于星上典型处理器（如FPGA、ARM Cortex系列），直接迁移到卫星平台可行性有限。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：在Chattanooga市12个信号化路口采集的同步相机-LiDAR数据，涵盖白天、夜间及多种天气条件；评价指标：摘要未明确列出具体指标（如mAP、召回率等），仅提及感知范围（perceptual range）和鲁棒性（robustness）提升。

Experiments: 实验在12个路口部署，并在代表性路口进行深入评估；融合架构相比单传感器显著提升了感知范围和鲁棒性；late-fusion核心在Jetson AGX Thor上达到53.2 FPS。未提供与SOTA方法的定量对比、消融实验等详细信息。

Limitations: (1) 场景局限在地面交通路口，与遥感卫星场景差异大；(2) 依赖LiDAR传感器，遥感卫星通常不具备LiDAR；(3) 部署平台Jetson AGX Thor算力远高于星上可用算力；(4) 未提供mAP等标准检测精度指标；(5) 未涉及模型压缩（剪枝、量化等）具体技术；(6) 摘要未说明模型参数量、FLOPs等关键轻量化指标。

Ideas for my research: (1) 借鉴其"边缘端全流程部署"思路：CLIFE将标定、融合、跟踪全链路放在边缘设备上，类似地，在轨遥感检测也可考虑将预处理、检测、后处理全流程在星上完成；(2) late-fusion策略：对于多光谱/多模态遥感数据，可参考其轻量级后融合设计，避免早期融合带来的高计算开销；(3) O(N log N)复杂度设计理念：在轨模型设计时应关注算法复杂度阶数，确保随目标数量增长时计算可控；(4) 在线标定思想：星上传感器（如多光谱相机）可能因发射震动或热变形产生偏移，可借鉴其无目标在线标定方法。但需注意，该论文的传感器配置（相机+LiDAR）和部署平台（Jetson）与遥感卫星差异显著，直接技术迁移有限。

Abstract: Reliable roadside perception of vulnerable road users (VRUs) remains challenging under occlusions, variable lighting, and diverse weather conditions, particularly under strict edge-computing and latency constraints. Existing multi-sensor fusion systems rely on cloud or server-grade infrastructure, creating a deployment gap at real-world intersections. We present CLIFE, an edge-native camera-LiDAR fusion framework that integrates targetless online calibration and lightweight late-fusion tracking entirely on a single embedded device, without cloud offloading. CLIFE adaptively refines camera-LiDAR alignment on demand and performs multi-sensor fusion and track association with O(N log N) per-frame cost. We deploy CLIFE across 12 signalized intersections in Chattanooga and conduct an in-depth evaluation at a representative intersection using synchronized camera-LiDAR data that spans diverse daytime, nighttime, and weather conditions. Our experiments demonstrate that the fusion architecture substantially enhances the perceptual range and robustness of the individual sensors under varied environmental and traffic conditions. The late-fusion core operates at 53.2 FPS on the Jetson AGX Thor, ensuring high throughput for real-time intersection-scale applications. By centering perception at the edge, CLIFE provides a deployable foundation for downstream safety applications, while reducing bandwidth and calibration overhead for agencies operating multi-intersection corridors.

</details>


### [72] [Vision-Language Assistant for Emotional Reactions to Risky Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16181)
*Harine Choi,Eun Hak Lee,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个名为KYA的视觉-语言驾驶辅助系统，用YOLOv8检测危险驾驶行为，再用大语言模型生成情感化语音反馈。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文面向的是**自动驾驶/辅助驾驶场景**中的危险驾驶行为检测与情感交互，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关领域；不涉及在轨部署、星上推理、边缘部署等轻量化部署场景；虽然使用了YOLOv8（可迁移至遥感目标检测），但论文本身不讨论遥感任务。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：危险驾驶行为检测 + 情感化语音生成；应用场景：车载辅助驾驶系统；传感器类型：dashcam（行车记录仪视频）；目标类别：附近车辆（vehicle detection），危险行为如sudden cut-in。

Model architecture: 双模块架构：视觉模块使用YOLOv8s/YOLOv8n/YOLOv8x等变体检测车辆并提取行为指标（相对距离、速度、预计到达时间）；语言模块使用ChatGPT-4o、Claude 3、Gemini 2.5、Copilot等LLM生成情感化语音反馈。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。虽然使用了YOLOv8s（轻量版YOLOv8），但论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩技术，也未讨论实时推理的延迟、FLOPs、参数量等部署指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然YOLOv8s本身是轻量检测器，但论文未讨论在嵌入式平台或车规级芯片上的部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：dashcam视频（包含危险驾驶行为）；用户研究：108名参与者；评价指标：用户偏好评分（5分制），LLM的情感对齐评分。YOLOv8s+ChatGPT-4o组合最高得分4.29/5.00。

Experiments: 对比了YOLOv8不同变体（s/n/x）与不同LLM（ChatGPT-4o, Claude 3, Gemini 2.5, Copilot）的组合效果；进行了用户偏好研究；所有模型获得较好评分但不同用户角色偏好不同。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未讨论模型轻量化、部署效率；3. 未提供检测精度（mAP等）和推理速度指标；4. 情感生成部分依赖云端LLM，不适合边缘/星上部署。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴点有限：1. YOLOv8作为遥感目标检测的基线模型可参考，但该论文未提供YOLOv8在遥感场景下的具体配置；2. 视觉-语言多模态融合的思路在遥感领域（如遥感图像描述、视觉问答）有潜在应用，但与本课题的轻量化目标检测方向关联不大。

Abstract: This study introduces a vision-language pipeline that detects risky driving behaviors and generates emotionally expressive responses to support driver awareness and comfort. Although vision-language models have advanced perception and reasoning in autonomous driving, existing systems rarely consider the emotional dimension or real-world user experience. Keep Yelling Assistant (KYA) detects high-risk driving maneuvers in real time, such as sudden cut-ins. It then produces emotional responses through a large language model tailored to driver preferences. The framework comprises two core modules. The vision module uses YOLOv8 variants to detect nearby vehicles and identify risky behaviors such as sudden cut-ins. Key driving metrics, including relative distance, speed, and projected reach time, are extracted and normalized to produce a structured behavior log. The language module processes this log with user-defined emotional tone settings, such as neutral, humorous, and analytical, and generates verbal reactions using state-of-the-art large language models, including ChatGPT-4o, Claude 3, Gemini 2.5, and Copilot. We evaluated the proposed system using dashcam videos containing risky driving behaviors and a user study involving 108 participants. Participants selected preferred response styles, and the large language models were evaluated based on emotional alignment. All models received favorable ratings, although preferences varied across personas. Notably, the combination of YOLOv8s and ChatGPT-4o achieved the highest score of 4.29 out of 5.00. By integrating real-world perception with emotionally adaptive dialogue, KYA introduces a new paradigm for emotionally intelligent in-vehicle artificial intelligence. It offers promising directions for improving safety, trust, and emotional well-being in both conventional and autonomous vehicles.

</details>


### [73] [Searching Videos as Trees: Self-Correcting Agents for Grounded Long Video QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16189)
*Ce Zhang,Ziyang Wang,Yulu Pan,Oluwatumininu Oguntola,Pranav Wagh,Qiyu Wu,Hiromi Wakaki,Mohit Bansal,Gedas Bertasius*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VideoTreeSearch框架，将长视频问答建模为自适应时序树上的迭代自纠正搜索，通过zoom_in/zoom_out/shift/answer四种操作实现回溯与恢复。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于长视频问答（Grounded LVQA）任务，属于视频理解与多模态问答领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：Grounded long-video question answering（长视频问答+证据区间定位）；场景：通用视频理解；传感器类型：RGB视频；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: VideoTreeSearch框架：基于视觉场景边界构建非均匀时序树，每个节点对应语义连贯的视频片段；Agent通过四种离散操作（zoom_in, zoom_out, shift, answer）在树上导航。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向通用视频QA，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CG-Bench, Haystack-LVBench, Haystack-Ego4D（Grounded LVQA）；Video-MME, MLVU, LVBench（通用长视频QA）。指标：mIoU, T-F1, accuracy。

Experiments: 在三个Grounded LVQA基准上，VTS超越最强prior agentic方法：CG-Bench +12.5 mIoU，Haystack-Ego4D +7.4 T-F1；在通用长视频QA上超越所有prior agentic基线，最高+7.1 accuracy。消融实验证实自纠正分层搜索是核心机制。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或部署技术。该论文解决的是视频问答中的回溯与纠错问题，而非检测或压缩问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。如果强行关联，其"分层搜索+回溯"的思想在遥感大场景下的多尺度目标搜索（如先粗定位再细检测）可能有概念上的类比，但无实质技术贡献可迁移。

Abstract: Grounded long-video question answering (Grounded LVQA) requires answering a question about a long video while localizing the short evidence interval that supports the answer. Recent agentic methods frame this task as multi-turn exploration with a single crop_video(start, end) action, which supports coarse-to-fine narrowing but provides no primitive for fine-to-coarse backtracking. As a result, these agents typically converge prematurely and cannot recover from an early mistake. We propose VideoTreeSearch (VTS), a framework that casts grounded LVQA as iterative self-correcting search over an adaptive temporal tree. VTS constructs a non-uniform tree from visual scene boundaries so that each node corresponds to a semantically coherent segment, and trains an agent to navigate the tree through four discrete operations: zoom_in, zoom_out, shift, and answer. These operations expose backtracking and recovery as explicit, learnable primitives rather than implicit behaviors. To train this navigation, we introduce a trajectory synthesis pipeline that produces multi-step paths through the tree, including deliberate detours into incorrect branches followed by recovery. We use these trajectories for supervised fine-tuning, followed by reinforcement learning with grounding and answer-accuracy rewards. On three Grounded LVQA benchmarks (CG-Bench, Haystack-LVBench, Haystack-Ego4D), VTS outperforms the strongest prior agentic methods by +12.5 mIoU on CG-Bench and +7.4 T-F1 on Haystack-Ego4D. The learned policy also transfers to general long-video QA, surpassing all prior agentic baselines on Video-MME, MLVU, and LVBench by up to +7.1 accuracy points. Ablations confirm that self-correcting hierarchical search is the central mechanism behind these gains: removing either adaptive descent or explicit backtracking substantially degrades performance. Code is available at https://github.com/CeeZh/VTS.

</details>


### [74] [FVAttn: Adaptive Sparse Attention with Runtime Load Balancing for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16190)
*Hao Liu,Chenghuan Huang,Ye Huang,Zhiying Wen,Hao Liu,Mohan Zhang,Chen Li,Ziyang Ma,Jing Lyu,Jiangsu Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向视频扩散Transformer（Video Diffusion Transformers）的无训练稀疏注意力系统，通过运行时负载均衡和稀疏增强，在多GPU序列并行下加速高分辨率视频生成中的自注意力计算。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于视频生成（video generation）中的扩散Transformer（DiT）加速，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）。虽然涉及稀疏注意力（sparse attention）和推理加速，但应用场景是视频生成而非遥感在轨目标检测，且方法针对多GPU序列并行环境，与星上单卡/边缘部署场景差异巨大。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不推荐深入阅读。除非后续研究涉及星上多芯片并行架构下的稀疏注意力加速，否则优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：高分辨率视频生成（high-resolution video generation）；应用场景：视频扩散模型推理加速；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: Video Diffusion Transformers（视频扩散Transformer），核心瓶颈是自注意力（self-attention）机制。方法基于无训练稀疏注意力（training-free sparse attention），采用Top-p路由、Top-k安全下限、视频感知块组织作为稀疏路由前端，并在运行时修复掩码。包含运行时负载均衡（Runtime Load Balancing）和松弛感知稀疏增强（Slack-Aware Sparse Augmentation）两个核心模块。

Lightweight method: 无训练稀疏注意力（training-free sparse attention），属于推理加速方法，但并非传统意义上的模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。不涉及参数量压缩或模型结构轻量化，而是通过稀疏化注意力计算来降低计算开销。摘要未说明剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。方法针对多GPU序列并行（multi-GPU sequence parallelism）环境设计，与星上部署（单卡/低功耗/边缘设备）场景不匹配。虽然报告了4.41×注意力加速和2.02-2.11× DiT推理加速，但这些是在多GPU集群上的结果，不适用于在轨单芯片场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集，仅提及在step-distilled Wan2.2 I2V模型上评估。评价指标：负载不均衡度（load imbalance，从1.34降至1.08）、注意力加速比（4.41× over FlashAttention）、DiT推理加速比（2.02-2.11×）、视频质量（competitive video quality，未给出具体数值）。

Experiments: 在step-distilled Wan2.2 I2V模型上，平均负载不均衡度从1.34降至1.08，注意力计算相比FlashAttention加速4.41倍，DiT推理加速2.02-2.11倍，视频质量保持竞争力。摘要未说明与其他稀疏注意力方法的详细对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 方法针对多GPU序列并行，不适用于单卡/边缘/星上部署；3. 无训练稀疏注意力虽降低计算量，但未减少模型参数量或内存占用，对星上存储受限场景帮助有限；4. 仅验证了视频生成任务，未涉及目标检测；5. 摘要未报告实际延迟（毫秒级）、能耗等对在轨部署更关键的指标。

Ideas for my research: 1. 稀疏注意力思想可借鉴：在遥感目标检测Transformer中，可探索类似的无训练稀疏注意力机制来加速推理，但需适配检测任务而非生成任务；2. 负载均衡策略：如果未来在轨部署采用多芯片并行架构，运行时负载均衡和P2P通信迁移策略可能有参考价值；3. 松弛感知增强思路：在计算资源有剩余时填充高价值计算块，类似思路可用于星上动态调整检测精度与速度的权衡。但整体而言，该论文与课题方向匹配度低，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Transformers process long spatio-temporal sequences, making self-attention the main bottleneck in high-resolution video generation. Training-free sparse attention reduces this cost, but adaptive Top-$p$ routing creates uneven per-head workloads under multi-GPU sequence parallelism. The resulting workload heterogeneity turns sparse attention into a rank-level straggler problem. We present \method{}, a training-free sparse-attention system that improves the distributed execution efficiency of adaptive sparse attention under multi-GPU sequence parallelism. \method{} uses Top-$p$ routing, a Top-$k$ safety floor, and video-aware block organization as the sparse-routing frontend, then repairs the materialized mask at runtime. Runtime Load Balancing migrates a small number of heavy heads via P2P communication to shorten the current critical path. Slack-Aware Sparse Augmentation fills residual non-critical-rank slack with additional high-value blocks, while overlap hides scheduling and migration overhead behind existing computation. On step-distilled Wan2.2 I2V, \method{} reduces average load imbalance from 1.34 to 1.08 and delivers a $4.41\times$ attention speedup over FlashAttention, while achieving a $2.02$--$2.11\times$ DiT inference speedup with competitive video quality.

</details>


### [75] [MotionForesight: Re-purposing Video Models for Future 3D Scene-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16192)
*Homanga Bharadhwaj,Yash Jangir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionForesight利用预训练视频模型中的先验知识，通过轻量适配器将回顾式3D跟踪转化为前向预测，从单目视频中预测人-物交互中物体的未来3D轨迹。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是从日常单目视频中预测人-物交互场景下物体的未来3D运动轨迹，属于embodied intelligence / 物理场景理解 / 视频预测领域。完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星、Earth observation、SAR/高光谱/多光谱、航空图像、空间在轨智能等遥感核心要素；也不涉及目标检测（object detection）、小目标检测、旋转框检测等遥感目标检测任务；虽然文中提到"lightweight adapter"（轻量适配器），但这是针对视频预测模型的微调适配，并非面向边缘/星上部署的模型轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。因此与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的相关性极低。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：3D运动轨迹预测（3D motion forecasting）；应用场景：人-物交互（human-object interaction）的物理运动预测；传感器类型：单目RGB视频（monocular videos）；目标类别：日常操作物体（杯、抽屉、盖子等）。完全不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: 基于预训练视频模型（pretrained video model）构建密集3D跟踪器（dense 3D tracker），使用学习到的掩码潜变量（learned mask latents）替代未来RGB和几何信息，训练轻量适配器（lightweight adapter）将回顾式跟踪表示（retrospective tracking representation）转化为前向预测器（forward predictor），同时冻结大型视频和跟踪组件。

Lightweight method: 文中提到"lightweight adapter"和"freezing the large video and tracking components"，这是一种参数高效微调（parameter-efficient fine-tuning）策略，冻结大模型、仅训练轻量适配器。但这并非面向边缘/星上部署的模型轻量化方法（如剪枝pruning、量化quantization、知识蒸馏knowledge distillation、神经架构搜索NAS等），而是为了降低训练成本。摘要未说明推理时的参数量、FLOPs、延迟等部署指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然使用了轻量适配器，但底层依赖大型预训练视频模型和3D跟踪器，整体模型规模可能很大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 使用40k人类交互视频（human videos），无辅助输入（如语言）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 在多样化的分布外（out-of-distribution）物体、环境、视角和交互上泛化良好，性能优于使用超过百万训练视频的更大模型。摘要未提供定量对比结果。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 轻量化仅体现在训练阶段的适配器设计，而非推理阶段的模型压缩或加速；4. 底层依赖大型预训练模型，实际推理开销可能很大；5. 未提供任何部署相关的量化指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可参考的点是"冻结大模型+训练轻量适配器"的参数高效微调思路，如果未来在遥感在轨场景中需要利用预训练基础模型（如视觉大模型）进行微调，可考虑类似策略降低训练开销。但遥感在轨目标检测更关注推理阶段的轻量化（小模型、低FLOPs、低延迟），而非训练阶段的参数高效微调，因此借鉴价值不高。

Abstract: Humans can infer how objects are likely to move from passive observation: a cup may be lifted, a drawer may slide, and a lid may rotate shut. Such predictions expose the physical consequences of interaction needed to act in the real world. We study how to learn this anticipation from ordinary monocular videos of human-object interaction. Given a short observed video context, MotionForesight predicts future 3D trajectories for points on the manipulated object. This casts interaction prediction as object-centered 3D motion forecasting without any assumptions on the object properties. Our key insight is that video prediction models already encode rich priors about how objects move during human interactions. We redirect these priors from pixel prediction toward future 3D scene flow. We start from a dense 3D tracker built on a pretrained video model, generate pseudo-ground-truth tracks from complete clips, and train the forecaster using only the observed frames. We replace future RGB and geometry with learned mask latents and train a lightweight adapter to turn the retrospective tracking representation into a forward predictor, while freezing the large video and tracking components. Using just 40k human videos and no auxiliary inputs such as language, MotionForesight generalizes across diverse out-of-distribution objects, environments, viewpoints, and interactions. It also outperforms substantially larger models that use over a million training videos. These results show that we can efficiently re-purpose video priors into explicit geometric forecasts for embodied intelligence. https://motionforesight.github.io/

</details>


### [76] [Knowing the Self, Understanding the World: A Dual-Cognition Benchmark for UAV Spatio-temporal Reasoning with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16193)
*Like Liu,Zhengzheng Xu,Haitao He,Hongzhe Li,Shuchang Zhang,Dian Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了UAV-DualCog基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在无人机场景下的双认知推理能力（自身状态+外部环境），包含多视角时空推理任务。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。论文涉及UAV（无人机）场景，属于遥感/航空图像范畴，但核心任务是多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的视觉-语言推理能力评估（场景理解、事件识别、导航），而非目标检测。轻量化方面仅提及"lightweight optimization probe"作为微调实验，不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏等模型压缩方法。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向弱相关。

Reading priority: 1分（不推荐精读）。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向弱相关，不涉及目标检测和模型压缩核心问题。如果研究方向涉及MLLM在遥感中的应用可作泛读，否则可跳过。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态大语言模型在无人机场景下的双认知推理（self-state reasoning + environment-state reasoning），包含图像和视频任务。应用场景：UAV（无人机）航空场景。传感器类型：摘要未明确说明（基于场景级语义点云构建数据）。目标类别：场景理解、地标识别、事件识别、导航，非目标检测。

Model architecture: 评估对象为现有MLLMs（多模态大语言模型），如thinking/frontier models。摘要未提出新的模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要仅提及"lightweight optimization probe"（轻量优化探针实验），用于验证UAV-DualCog-Train数据集作为结构化监督信号的价值。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文聚焦于基准构建和模型评估，而非部署优化。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：UAV-DualCog（基准测试集，包含图像和视频任务，多视角时空推理QA样本），UAV-DualCog-Train（训练集，来自不重叠场景）。数据构建方式：从场景级语义点云自动生成。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标（涉及空间/时间定位精度、推理准确率等）。

Experiments: 评估了当前主流MLLMs在UAV双认知任务上的表现，发现：1）现有模型远未达到可靠水平；2）自身状态推理、视角变换、精确空间定位、时间区间定位是持续瓶颈；3）通过thinking/frontier模型和人类基线验证，基准对人类可理解但对模型有挑战性。

Limitations: 1. 与目标检测任务无关，不涉及检测框、旋转框、小目标检测等核心问题；2. 不涉及任何模型轻量化技术；3. 不涉及星上/边缘部署；4. 数据基于语义点云自动生成，与真实遥感卫星图像存在域差异；5. 未报告计算效率指标。

Ideas for my research: 1. 该论文的"双认知"视角（自身状态+外部环境）对遥感卫星在轨场景有启发意义——卫星也需要同时感知自身姿态/轨道状态和地面目标；2. 多视角时空推理任务设计思路可迁移到遥感视频目标检测中的时序建模；3. 自动化数据构建管线（从语义点云生成QA样本）的思路可用于遥感目标检测数据增强；4. 但核心任务（MLLM推理）与目标检测差异较大，直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have achieved strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, yet their capabilities in UAV scenarios remain insufficiently explored. Recent UAV-oriented benchmarks have begun to evaluate MLLMs in aerial scenarios, but they typically focus on scene understanding, event recognition, or navigation completion, rather than jointly assessing the dual-cognition capability required for UAV agents: reasoning about both the UAV's own state and the external environment in multiview spatio-temporal contexts. To address this gap, we present UAV-DualCog, a benchmark for aerial multiview spatio-temporal reasoning built on this dual-cognition perspective. UAV-DualCog includes both image and video tasks to jointly evaluate self-state and environment-state reasoning, while requiring spatial or temporal grounding beyond discrete answer prediction. We also develop an automated pipeline that constructs data from scene-level semantic point clouds, yielding a scalable benchmark with diverse scenes, hundreds of landmarks, and thousands of QA samples. Extensive evaluations show that current MLLMs remain far from reliable in UAV dual cognition. Self-state reasoning, viewpoint transformation, precise spatial grounding, and temporal interval localization are persistent bottlenecks, and additional validation with thinking/frontier models and a human baseline confirms that the benchmark is understandable to humans but challenging for existing models. We further construct UAV-DualCog-Train from disjoint scenes and show through a lightweight optimization probe that it provides useful structured supervision, suggesting its value not only as an evaluation benchmark but also as a data resource for advancing MLLM-based UAV agents. Project website and supplementary materials: https://uav-dualcog.lozumi.com

</details>

### [77] [Large Language Model-Enhanced Multi-hop Parallel Image Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15297)
*Bingyan Xie,Jihong Park,Rui Mao,Longyu Zhou,Tianhao Liang,Yongpeng Wu,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种大语言模型增强的多跳并行图像语义通信框架（LLM-MHPSC），通过残差补偿链路和粗到细残差压缩方案缓解多跳无线图像传输中的失真累积问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于无线图像语义通信中的多跳传输失真问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。论文主题为无线图像语义通信，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多跳无线图像语义通信；场景：通用无线图像传输；传感器类型：未说明；目标类别：未说明。完全不涉及遥感图像或目标检测。

Model architecture: LLM-MHPSC框架，包含：残差补偿链路、深度学习压缩器+自适应算术编码（AAC）的粗到细残差压缩方案、大语言模型残差传输优化器（LLM-RTO）、自适应跳数选择策略。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。仅提及通过自适应跳数选择策略平衡传输性能与计算成本，但未给出具体轻量化技术细节。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然涉及计算成本权衡，但未针对星上或边缘部署场景进行讨论。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体使用的数据集和评价指标，仅提及实验结果表明优于现有语义通信和传统方案。

Experiments: 摘要未提供详细的实验对比结果、消融实验数据或定量指标。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。从研究方向角度看，主要局限在于：完全不涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务或模型轻量化技术，与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化课题无直接关联。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发包括：(1) 粗到细（coarse-to-fine）的渐进式压缩思路可迁移到遥感模型轻量化中的特征压缩；(2) LLM辅助的残差优化思路可启发在轨场景下利用大模型进行自适应推理调度。但整体方向差异较大，不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: This paper proposes a large language model-enhanced multi-hop parallel image semantic communication (LLM-MHPSC) framework to mitigate distortion accumulation in multi-hop wireless image transmission. Unlike conventional single-hop semantic communication schemes, LLM-MHPSC deploys an extra residual compensation link at each hop to counteract accumulated distortions. To minimize additional bandwidth overhead, a coarse-to-fine residual compression scheme is designed by integrating a deep learning-based compressor with adaptive arithmetic coding (AAC). Furthermore, a large language model-based residual transmission optimizer (LLM-RTO) is developed to accurately estimate residual distributions and enable channel state and hop-aware rate adjustment, thereby improving residual compression efficiency under varying channel and hop conditions. An adaptive hop selection strategy is also proposed to activate the residual link on demand, striking a balance between transmission performance and computational cost. Experimental results show that LLM-MHPSC outperforms state-of-the-art semantic communication and traditional schemes, realizing robust image transmission with a marginal increase in bandwidth. This framework provides a flexible and effective solution for extending semantic communication to practical multi-hop application scenarios.

</details>


### [78] [Data-driven Video Codec with Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15298)
*Nishan Khanal,Saugat Neupane,Abhinav Chalise,Nimesh Gopal Pradhan,Dinesh Baniya Kshatri*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出将视频（含音频）编码为单个SIREN网络的权重，并通过知识蒸馏+16bit量化+LZMA2压缩实现视频表示压缩，而非传统的像素压缩编码。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文研究的是通用视频/音频的神经表示压缩，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然使用了知识蒸馏和量化等轻量化技术，但应用场景是视频编解码而非遥感在轨目标检测，且未涉及任何部署相关指标（延迟、FLOPs、能耗等）。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，阅读优先级极低。除非对神经表示压缩（NeRF/SIREN类方法）在遥感领域的泛化应用有特殊兴趣，否则不建议投入时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频+音频的神经表示压缩与编解码；应用场景：通用视频存储/传输；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: SIREN（正弦表示网络）：空间-时间坐标→RGB值+音频振幅；包含独立的音频和视频初始化层、共享全连接隐藏层、三个输出分支（一个视频分支+两个孪生音频分支用于噪声估计与去除）。

Lightweight method: 使用了response-based knowledge distillation（教师→学生网络蒸馏）、16-bit symmetric weight quantization、lossless LZMA2 (xz) encoding。但未涉及剪枝、NAS、结构化轻量化设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅报告了压缩比（9.05 MiB→2.33 MiB，压缩比2.61）和重建质量（视频PSNR 28.72 dB, SSIM 0.75）。未提及任何边缘/星上部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 测试视频：6.08 MiB的单个测试视频（具体名称未说明）；评价指标：视频PSNR、SSIM、音频PSNR、log spectral distance；对比方法：H.264、HEVC、MP3。

Experiments: 量化位宽扫描（1-bit到32-bit）显示16-bit时重建质量饱和；整体压缩比2.61；与H.264、HEVC、MP3对比，指出方法在这些传统编解码器面前存在不足；实现了基于WebRTC的浏览器原型。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 压缩比（2.61）远低于传统视频编码标准（H.264/HEVC），实用性有限；3. 仅在单个测试视频上评估，缺乏大规模验证；4. 未报告推理速度、计算开销等关键部署指标；5. 未涉及目标检测任务，无法判断检测精度。

Ideas for my research: 虽然论文与遥感在轨目标检测无关，但以下技术点有微弱借鉴意义：1. 知识蒸馏+量化+无损编码的联合压缩管线设计思路，可迁移到遥感检测模型的轻量化流程中；2. 16-bit量化饱和点的实验发现，对遥感模型量化位宽选择有参考价值；3. 响应式蒸馏（response-based KD）方法可尝试用于遥感检测模型的轻量化。但整体借鉴价值有限，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: A conventional codec stores a video as compressed pixel data. We instead store the video, together with its audio track, as the weights of a single sinusoidal representation network (SIREN) that maps space-time coordinates to RGB values and audio amplitudes. The network uses separate audio and video initialization layers, a stack of shared fully connected hidden layers, and three output branches: one for video and two Siamese audio branches whose disagreement is used to estimate and subtract residual noise. The overfitted teacher network is then compressed by response-based knowledge distillation into a smaller student, followed by 16-bit symmetric weight quantization and lossless LZMA2 (xz) encoding. On a 6.08 MiB test video, the quantized student reaches a video PSNR of 28.72 dB with SSIM of 0.75, and an audio PSNR of 24.18 dB with a log spectral distance of 10.69 dB, while the pipeline shrinks the representation from 9.05 MiB to 2.33 MiB, an overall compression ratio of 2.61. A bit-width sweep from 1-bit to 32-bit quantization shows that reconstruction quality saturates at 16 bits. We compare against H.264, HEVC, and MP3, report where the approach falls short of them, and describe a browser-based prototype that trains, transfers, and decodes these models over WebRTC.

</details>


### [79] [MSTF-Net: A UAV-Oriented Multi-Spectral Video Segmentation Method via Modality-Robust, Scale-Adaptive, and Consistent Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15628)
*Chenwei Wang,Zhida Wang,Zelin Li,Elif Ozden-Yenigun,Houde Liu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出MSTF-Net，一种面向无人机多光谱视频分割的模态鲁棒尺度自适应融合网络，通过跨模态注意力和时序一致性模块提升RGB-T融合分割性能。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文涉及无人机（UAV）平台和RGB-T多光谱数据，与遥感/地球观测有一定关联，但任务为视频分割（video segmentation）而非目标检测（object detection），且完全不涉及轻量化、模型压缩、星上部署等方向。属于泛视觉方法，对在轨遥感目标检测轻量化课题的借鉴价值有限。

Reading priority: 2分——弱相关。论文聚焦多光谱视频分割，非目标检测，且无任何轻量化内容。仅跨模态融合模块设计思路有微弱借鉴意义，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多光谱视频分割（multi-spectral video segmentation）；应用场景：无人机城市测绘、土地利用监测、交通监控、人群估计；传感器类型：RGB+热红外（thermal）；目标类别：摘要未说明具体类别（涉及小目标、遮挡等挑战场景）。

Model architecture: MSTF-Net包含两个核心模块：(1) Modality Spatial Complementary Suppression and Enhancement (MSCSE)模块——通过跨模态注意力生成统一实例查询，利用残差引导的差异过滤和一致性约束抑制模态特定噪声；(2) Multi-scale Temporal Cross-modality Semantic Consistency (MTCSC)模块——基于帧距离自适应调整时序感受野，捕获粗粒度全局上下文和细粒度局部结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。无法判断星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MVSeg数据集、CART数据集（均为公开RGB-T数据集）；评价指标：mIoU（mean Intersection over Union），在MVSeg上达到56.42%，在CART上达到51.80%。

Experiments: 在MVSeg和CART两个公开RGB-T数据集上进行了大量消融实验，达到SOTA分割性能，尤其在挑战性场景（小目标、遮挡、模态退化）下表现突出。摘要未提供与其他方法的详细对比表格。

Limitations: (1) 任务为视频分割而非目标检测，与课题核心任务不匹配；(2) 完全不涉及轻量化、模型压缩或加速；(3) 未报告模型复杂度（参数量、FLOPs等），无法评估部署可行性；(4) 仅针对无人机平台，未涉及卫星在轨场景；(5) 仅使用RGB-T双模态，未涉及SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感常用模态。

Ideas for my research: (1) MSCSE模块中的跨模态注意力机制和残差引导差异过滤思想，可借鉴用于遥感多模态（如光学+SAR）目标检测中的特征融合；(2) MTCSC模块中基于帧距离自适应调整时序感受野的思路，可用于遥感视频流目标检测中的时序建模；(3) 但整体而言，该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向匹配度低，不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: Multi-spectral video segmentation is essential for robust scene understanding in unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) applications such as city planning, land use monitoring, traffic monitoring, and crowd estimation. While the fusion of RGB and thermal modalities offers complementary information for perception under varying lighting and visibility conditions, two fundamental challenges remain: (1) the modal fusion dilemma, arising from significant discrepancies between RGB and thermal features that obscure complementary cues, and (2) temporal variation, induced by rapid motion and viewpoint changes on UAV platforms, which leads to appearance inconsistency and misalignment across frames. To address these issues, this study proposed MSTF-Net, a modality-robust scale-adaptive fusion framework for multi-spectral video segmentation that effectively models cross-modal fusion and temporal consistency. The Modality Spatial Complementary Suppression and Enhancement (MSCSE) module generates unified instance queries via cross-modal attention and suppresses modality-specific noise using residual-guided discrepancy filtering and consistency constraints. To model temporal dynamics, the Multi-scale Temporal Cross-modality Semantic Consistency (MTCSC) module adaptively adjusts the temporal receptive field based on frame distance, capturing both coarse global context and fine local structure across time. Extensive ablation experiments on public RGB-T datasets demonstrate that MSTF-Net achieves state-of-the-art segmentation performance, especially under challenging conditions such as small targets, occlusion, and modality degradation. Specifically, reached 56.42\% mIoU on the MVSeg dataset and 51.80\% mIoU on the CART dataset, respectively.

</details>


### [80] [Robust Monitoring of Arc Welding Processes: A Generalizable Framework with DVAE and Particle Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16013)
*Yue Cao,Hai Lin,YuMing Zhang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合无监督深度潜表示学习（DVAE）与贝叶斯滤波（粒子滤波）的弧焊过程实时监测框架，用于焊接质量监控。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦工业制造中的弧焊过程监测，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、航空图像、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Model architecture: DVAE（Dynamic Variational Autoencoder）：CNN-based encoder-decoder提取图像潜特征 + LSTM-based transition model建模时序演化；推理阶段使用Particle Filter（PF）传播潜变量和LSTM隐状态。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：GTAW（Gas Tungsten Arc Welding）和GMAW（Gas Metal Arc Welding）焊接过程数据；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标（如准确率、F1等）。

Experiments: 在GTAW和GMAW两种焊接工艺上验证，无需工艺特定调参（process-specific tuning），证明框架的泛化性和鲁棒性。摘要未给出定量对比结果。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化，与课题方向无直接关联；2. 摘要未报告模型复杂度、推理速度等部署关键指标；3. 仅针对焊接慢速惯性动力学设计，不适用于遥感在轨快速推理场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可参考的点：DVAE+粒子滤波的时序建模思路可能对遥感视频流中的目标跟踪或变化检测有启发，但与本课题"在轨目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向不匹配。

Abstract: Arc welding processes are essential for continuous fabrication but prone to disturbances that impair weld quality, making real-time monitoring critical yet difficult due to complex visual patterns and nonlinear, time-varying dynamics. Deep learning shows promise but faces scalability limits because of its dependence on large labeled datasets and application-specific tuning. We explore whether a unified approach can characterize major arc welding processes across applications and improve scalability through consistent state monitoring. This paper introduces a robust and generalizable monitoring framework for arc welding. It combines unsupervised deep latent representation learning, which extracts compact features from weld pool images, with Bayesian filtering to handle persistent and fluctuating disturbances such as arc radiation and specular reflections. Specifically, a Dynamic Variational Autoencoder (DVAE), consisting of a CNN-based encoder-decoder and an LSTM-based transition model, jointly learns latent representations and their evolution under control inputs. For robust real-time inference, a specialized Particle Filter (PF) propagates the latent and LSTM hidden states, preserving process history while suppressing sensor noise. This design is well suited to welding's slow and inertial dynamics. Validation on GTAW and GMAW without process-specific tuning demonstrates the framework's generalizability and robustness.

</details>

### [81] [GraphDx: A Cost-Aware Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Agent Framework for Sequential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15280)
*Shaoting Tan,Ning Liu,Yuntao Du,Shuyue Wei,Wu Shuai,Qian Li,Yanyu Xu,Wei Zhang,Lizhen Cui,Haitao Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GraphDx框架，利用LLM构建医学诊断知识图谱(MDKG)，通过多智能体协作实现成本敏感的序贯诊断，在MedQA和MIMIC-IV上提升诊断成功率并降低测试成本。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于**医学临床诊断(clinical diagnosis)**中的序贯决策问题，完全不涉及遥感(remote sensing)、卫星(satellite)、地球观测(Earth observation)、航空图像(aerial image)、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素；不涉及目标检测(object detection)、小目标检测、旋转框检测等遥感视觉任务；不涉及模型轻量化(lightweight)、剪枝(pruning)、量化(quantization)、知识蒸馏(knowledge distillation)、NAS、边缘/星上部署等压缩加速技术。虽然文中提到"quantized typicality"和"cost-sensitive"，但这里的"quantized"是指知识图谱中典型性的量化表示，而非模型量化压缩；"cost"是指医疗测试成本，而非计算/存储/延迟等部署成本。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：序贯诊断(sequential diagnosis)、医学临床决策；应用场景：自动化临床诊断；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：疾病诊断。

Model architecture: GraphDx框架包含：(1) 自动化流水线利用LLM构建医学诊断知识图谱(MDKG)，具有量化典型性(quantized typicality)、以行动为中心的拓扑(action-centric topology)、双目标属性(诊断相关性和成本敏感性)；(2) 三个协作智能体：感知智能体(Perception Agent)、推理智能体(Reasoning Agent)、决策智能体(Decision Agent)。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文中的"quantized"指知识图谱中典型性的量化表示，与模型量化压缩无关。未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何与星上/边缘部署相关的指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MedQA、MIMIC-IV；评价指标：诊断成功率(diagnostic success rate)、测试成本(test cost)；LLM骨干：DeepSeek-V3、Kimi-k2、Llama-3.3。

Experiments: GraphDx在三个LLM骨干上将诊断成功率从50-68%提升至79-93%，同时降低测试成本20-54%。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未说明在遥感场景下的任何适用性。

Ideas for my research: 虽然论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化无关，但其"知识图谱+多智能体协作"的框架设计思路在方法论层面有一定启发：(1) 可考虑将遥感场景知识(如目标空间分布规律、传感器成像特性)构建为知识图谱，辅助在轨检测模型的推理；(2) 多智能体协作中"感知-推理-决策"的分工模式，可类比于在轨检测中的"特征提取-目标定位-后处理"流水线；(3) 成本敏感决策的思想可迁移到在轨场景中，即"计算资源成本-检测精度"的权衡。但这些仅为泛化思路，不直接相关。

Abstract: Sequential diagnosis requires balancing diagnostic accuracy against resource costs through iterative information gathering. Existing Large Language Model (LLM) approaches exhibit a critical knowledge-reasoning gap: despite encoding extensive medical knowledge, they struggle to reason systematically under cost constraints, often resorting to excessive testing. We propose GraphDx, a knowledge-enhanced framework with two core innovations. First, we design an automated pipeline that leverages LLMs to construct Medical Diagnosis Knowledge Graphs (MDKGs) with quantized typicality, action-centric topology, and dual-objective attributes for both diagnostic relevance and cost-sensitivity. Second, we introduce three collaborative agents (Perception, Reasoning, and Decision) where the Perception and Decision Agents handle language understanding and generation, while the Reasoning Agent performs deterministic evidence scoring and cost-aware planning on the MDKG. Experiments on MedQA and MIMIC-IV across three LLM backbones (DeepSeek-V3, Kimi-k2, Llama-3.3) show that GraphDx improves diagnostic success rates from 50--68% to 79--93% while reducing test costs by 20--54%, providing a robust, economical, and interpretable solution for automated clinical diagnosis.

</details>


### [82] [Causal-Audit: Explicit and Auditable Graph-based Reasoning via Target-Aware Causal Chain Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15281)
*Su Lan,Xuefei Yin,Yanming Zhu,Alan Wee-Chung Liew*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于显式因果图的干预问答框架，通过目标感知的因果图构建和路径级因果证据聚合，提升LLM在因果推理任务中的可解释性和鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于大语言模型的因果推理与可解释问答，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：因果推理与干预问答（causal reasoning, intervention-based QA）；应用场景：大语言模型推理增强；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 显式因果图构建（target-aware causal graph construction）+ 路径级因果证据聚合（path-level causal evidence aggregation），四阶段模块化推理框架。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向LLM推理，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 三个基准数据集（benchmarks），具体名称摘要未说明；评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在三个基准上，所提框架一致优于现有基于LLM的方法，并提供可解释和可审计的因果推理轨迹。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无直接借鉴点。该论文属于NLP/LLM因果推理领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型压缩加速技术。

Abstract: Causal and intervention-based question answering is fundamental to advancing large language models (LLMs) toward reasoning beyond surface-level correlations and understanding underlying causal mechanisms. However, existing LLM-based methods often rely on implicit language-level reasoning, resulting in opaque causal assumptions, unverifiable reasoning paths, and fragile predictions under complex interventions, particularly in context-free settings. In this paper, we propose an explicit and auditable causal reasoning framework for context-free intervention-based question answering. Our method formulates causal inference as structured reasoning over an explicit causal graph through four modular stages, rather than implicit end-to-end prediction. A key innovation is a target-aware causal graph construction strategy that treats the target variable as a core constraint during graph expansion, effectively suppressing irrelevant variables, spurious causal relations, and reasoning noise. We further introduce a path-level causal evidence aggregation mechanism that combines multiple causal paths while modeling both reinforcing and counteracting effects, enabling robust decision-making beyond single-chain reasoning. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms existing LLM-based methods while providing interpretable and auditable causal reasoning traces.

</details>


### [83] [Cura 1T: Specialized Model for Agentic Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15314)
*actAVA AI,:,Haolin Chen,Leon Qi,Steve Brown,Deon Metelski,Tao Xia,Joonyul Lee,Qixuan Wang,Kevin Riley,Frank Wang,Weiran Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Cura 1T，一个通过人类门控自进化循环训练的医疗专用大语言模型，覆盖患者咨询、临床推理、交互诊断和电子病历工具使用等多种医疗场景。

Abstract: Healthcare spans high-stakes communication, expert reasoning, and workflow execution, yet specialized LLMs that cover these use cases together remain limited. A healthcare model must handle patient consultation, clinical reasoning over text and images, interactive diagnosis, and electronic health record (EHR) tool use. These capabilities fail in different ways, and a narrow update for one task can degrade another. We present Cura 1T, a healthcare-specialized LLM trained through a human-gated self-evolution loop. In each evolution round, a training agent plans a target capability, trains the model, evaluates benchmark trajectories, and refines the data mixture from observed failures. This data-centered loop improves the model through targeted synthetic and curated examples rather than a single generic medical-data update. Across the healthcare evaluation suite, Cura 1T ranks at or near the top among frontier baselines, while remaining competitive on out-of-domain reasoning and agentic benchmarks.

</details>

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1. "adaptive recovery loop"和"speculative execution"思想可用于星上推理的容错设计；2. 多智能体编排的模块化思路可用于星上多任务调度；3. 但整体而言，该论文的研究场景和技术栈与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化差异过大。

Abstract: Desktop voice assistants are still dominated by cloud pipelines that ship raw audio off the machine and expose a fixed set of skills. We describe AnovaX, a small local-first assistant that runs entirely on the user's computer and treats the desktop itself as its action surface. A single Python process wires together a wake-word gate, a speech pipeline, an LLM planner (Gemini) that emits a JSON plan of tool calls, a whitelist-and-denylist safety layer, a multi-agent orchestrator that translates each plan into typed child agents on a bounded thread pool, and an adaptive recovery loop that takes over whenever a core step fails. Every tool corresponds to a specialized agent class (AppAgent, TypingAgent, BrowserAgent and six others) with its own timeout, retry policy, and shared-resource locks. A recursive MetaAgent lets the planner delegate a sub-goal back to itself, capped at two levels of nesting. The recovery loop uses a compact ReAct-style prompt and hides Gemini's latency behind speculative execution of read-only tools. A companion Flask server exposes a phone-friendly remote over the local WiFi, mirrors every agent lifecycle event to the phone in real time, and streams the laptop's screen back over MJPEG so the user can watch remote commands land as they run. The point of the project is less to compete with Siri or Alexa than to show that a legible, few-thousand-line assistant is enough to open apps, type into them, run searches, coordinate concurrent actions, recover from single-step failures, and be driven entirely from a phone in another room -- without the LLM ever touching the keyboard.

</details>


### [85] [Precise but Uncoupled: Reviewer Precision Does Not Guarantee Critique Uptake in Multi-Agent Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15388)
*Chih-Hsuan Yang,Jingyan Jiang,Vikram Vasudevan,Cheng-Hau Yang,Huihuo Zheng,Le Chen,Eliu A. Huerta,Venkatram Vishwanath,Ian T. Foster,Rajeev Thakur*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为多智能体协作推理（multi-agent collaborative reasoning），场景为数学/科学问题求解（Omni-MATH problems），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测等。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明传统视觉模型架构。涉及gpt-oss-120b作为actor模型，以及planner-executor-reviewer pipeline (PER)和broadcast-style peer discussion两种协作架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Omni-MATH（4,181个verifier-grounded数学问题）。评价指标：final accuracy（最终准确率）、reviewer precision（评审精度）、useful critique rate（有用评审率）、critique uptake（评审采纳率）。

Experiments: 实验对比了broadcast-style peer discussion与planner-executor-reviewer pipeline (PER)两种协作方式。发现广播式讨论在困难问题上优于PER流水线；PER的评审精度更高（0.861 vs 0.644），但评审采纳率更低，导致修复效果差。强制显式确认会降低准确率，将评审指导嵌入求解器工作上下文可部分改善。

Limitations: 与遥感目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。论文本身局限：仅在数学推理任务上验证，未涉及视觉或多模态任务；仅使用单一模型系列（gpt-oss-120b），泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但可抽象借鉴的思路：1）在轻量化模型的知识蒸馏或剪枝中，可类比"评审质量"与"评审采纳"的分离现象——教师模型能力强但学生模型未必能有效吸收知识，需要设计更好的知识传递机制；2）"将指导直接嵌入工作上下文"的思路可类比将教师特征直接融入学生网络中间层（如feature-based distillation），而非仅在输出层做soft label匹配。但整体而言，该论文对遥感在轨目标检测轻量化的直接参考价值很低。

Abstract: Many math- and science-oriented agent systems use hierarchical designs with specialized reviewer roles, assuming that a dedicated review stage should help turn wrong candidates into correct ones. We test this assumption on 4,181 verifier-grounded Omni-MATH problems using matched gpt-oss-120b actors. Collaboration adds little on the easiest tiers, but from tier 4 onward the gains open sharply; in this harder regime, broadcast-style peer discussion reaches higher final accuracy than a planner-executor-reviewer pipeline (PER). We ask whether this gap is explained by reviewer quality or by whether critique changes the next answer the protocol carries forward. It is not explained by reviewer precision alone: PER's reviewer is more precise than broadcast's (0.861 vs. 0.644), yet evaluator-verified useful critique is much less likely to change the next candidate and produces lower reviewer-guided repair. These results show that reviewer detection quality and critique uptake are empirically separable. Within matched PER interventions, forcing explicit acknowledgment lowers final accuracy, while embedding reviewer guidance directly in the solver's working context partially improves follow-through without closing the gap. Overall, reviewer-centric evaluation can overstate system quality: a protocol may spot errors well yet still fail to solve more problems if it does not act on those critiques.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce DrawingVQA, the first benchmark designed to evaluate multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on real-world construction drawings -- a core media in architecture, civil, and many other engineering practices. Unlike natural images or schematic floor plans, construction drawings fuse abstract geometry, symbolic notation, tabular data, annotations, and domain-specific text, forming a uniquely complex visual-textual domain core to engineering workflows. DrawingVQA bridges this gap with 33 "Issued for Construction" drawings and 92 expertly curated question-answer pairs, spanning three reasoning depths: perceptual understanding, contextual interpretation, and domain-expert reasoning. To evaluate model capabilities, we present a dual categorization framework to jointly analyze performance across seven construction-engineering and four MLLM capability dimensions -- the first to explicitly map engineering workflows to AI reasoning competencies. Evaluations of state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal a substantial gap between model and expert performance, particularly at higher reasoning depths. This benchmark lays a foundation for domain-specialized multimodal reasoning to allow for advancement on integration of AI-driven understanding and real-world engineering workflows.

</details>

Model architecture: 四种嵌套的Codex-based agents：文本基线智能体、灵活接口可执行世界模型（无回放验证）、带调度简化的可执行世界模型、固定接口验证智能体（保留简化+精确回放）。使用gpt-5.4、gpt-5.5、gpt-5.6-sol等大语言模型作为推理引擎。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ARC-AGI-3公开游戏集。评价指标：RHAE（约99%）、动作数（少于人类基线的一半）。

Experiments: 主要实验：在gpt-5.4/gpt-5.5的高和极高推理努力度下评估四种智能体变体；在gpt-5.6-sol下进行探索性实验。结果：所有变体随模型增强和推理努力度增加而提升；变体间差异小于预期；完整验证变体在所有设置中排名第一但资源消耗更大；文本变体在某些设置中优于可执行变体。

Limitations: 模型发布时间晚于游戏数据，公开集结果可能饱和，未见数据性能未测试。与遥感目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。论文研究的是抽象推理游戏的归因分析，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务场景、技术方法、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Our previous ARC-AGI-3 agent bundled executable world modeling, scheduled simplification, and exact replay verification, leaving unclear which idea accounted for its performance. We address this attribution question with four nested Codex-based agents: a textual baseline; a flexible-interface executable world model without replay verification; the same executable model with scheduled simplification; and a fixed-interface verification treatment that retains simplification and requires exact reproduction of recorded observations. The main study evaluates all four agents with gpt-5.4 and gpt-5.5 at high and xhigh reasoning effort on the public ARC-AGI-3 games. Exploratory follow-ups evaluate the textual and verification variants with gpt-5.6-sol at xhigh and max. The most robust result is that every agent variant improves with a stronger model and with greater reasoning effort. Within each model-effort setting, differences among variants are smaller than anticipated, while the effects of individual components vary across settings. Requiring a persistent executable deliverable is not universally beneficial: the textual variant outperforms the flexible-interface executable variant in both gpt-5.5 settings. Simplification improves performance in three of the four model-effort settings, with the weakest setting as the only exception. The complete verification treatment ranks first in all four settings, although it uses substantially more resources. In the gpt-5.6-sol follow-up, the verification variant fully solves every public game at both reasoning efforts, achieves about 99% RHAE, and uses fewer than half the total actions of the human baseline. Because the model postdates these games and held-out performance remains untested, this result should be interpreted as saturation of the public set only.

</details>

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该工作面向通用GPU环境下的社交媒体分析，不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：PrideMM、Memotion。评价指标：准确率(accuracy)。幽默检测准确率80.3%，仇恨检测准确率75.9%。同时使用人工评估和GPT-4评估解释质量。

Experiments: 在PrideMM和Memotion数据集上，MAR-12在幽默检测和仇恨检测任务上均超越现有最先进方法(SOTA)。消融实验和可解释性评估表明结构化视角和注意力机制有效。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或部署优化；3. 依赖大规模VLM，计算开销大，不适合资源受限场景；4. 仅针对社交媒体迷因数据，领域迁移性差。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点极少。唯一可能相关的是"角色感知软门控注意力"这一多分支特征融合机制的设计思路，但需要大幅改造才能用于遥感多模态(如光学+SAR)目标检测中的特征融合。整体而言，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的参考价值非常有限。

Abstract: Internet memes intertwine visual cues, textual content, and cultural context, making them particularly challenging to interpret in scenarios where humor, sarcasm, and harmful intent coexist. These complexities highlight the need for explainable meme understanding systems that can provide reliable and structured reasoning to support both accurate classification and human interpretability. However, existing multimodal classifiers either overlook these interdependencies or provide only limited interpretability. In this paper, we introduce MAR-12, a novel framework that leverages Vision Language Models (VLMs) for meme detection and understanding in settings where humorous and hateful elements may coexist. The framework first interprets each meme through twelve structured perspectives derived from humor and hate theories. It then applies a role-aware soft-gated attention mechanism to learn how much each perspective should contribute, followed by a prototype-based classifier for the final prediction. Finally, explanations are synthesized using both perspective-specific reasoning and learned attention weights, ensuring transparent and context-grounded justifications. We evaluate MAR-12 on the PrideMM and Memotion datasets, where it achieves up to 80.3% accuracy for humor detection and 75.9% accuracy for hate detection, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches. Furthermore, both human and GPT-4-based evaluations confirm that MAR-12 produces coherent and persuasive explanations, particularly for memes in which humorous and harmful cues co-occur.

</details>


### [89] [From Black Box to Executable Logic: Explainable Reinforcement Learning through Prolog Expert Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15459)
*Eduardo C. Garrido-Merchán*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将深度强化学习策略（PPO）转化为可解释的Prolog逻辑程序的三阶段后处理方法，并给出理论保证和实验验证。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于深度强化学习策略的可解释性（将黑盒策略转化为逻辑程序），不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心问题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：深度强化学习策略的可解释性转换（policy distillation to logic program）。场景：强化学习控制任务（two-room key-and-door, Acrobot, CartPole, LunarLander）。传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器。目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 三阶段后处理框架：1) 提取冻结的PPO教师策略；2) 从决策中归纳有序规则列表（经典关系学习方式）；3) 输出为Prolog程序，由现成逻辑引擎执行。后续扩展阶段可编辑规则库，仅当策略评估认证回报提升时才接受编辑。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文中的"distillation"是策略到逻辑规则的知识蒸馏，目的是可解释性而非模型压缩或加速。未涉及剪枝、量化、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注的是可解释性而非部署效率。逻辑程序的可执行性不面向星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 实验环境：two-room key-and-door任务（16,944个可达状态）、Acrobot、CartPole、LunarLander连续控制任务。评价指标：回报（return）、与教师策略的回报差距、规则数量（clauses）。

Experiments: 在two-room key-and-door任务上，扩展后的Prolog程序在每个种子下均达到精确最优回报；在预算受限情况下，10/10种子超过随机教师策略的精确回报。在Acrobot上以11条规则匹配神经教师（噪声范围内），CartPole上恢复约97%回报，LunarLander上仅部分恢复（受指数下界限制）。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感卫星目标检测完全无关；2) 方法针对强化学习策略，不适用于监督学习的目标检测模型；3) 连续观测空间下转换成本随观测维度指数增长（理论下界）；4) LunarLander等精细控制任务恢复效果有限；5) 未讨论任何遥感图像或目标检测场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。可借鉴点有限：1) 规则蒸馏的思想或许可用于遥感目标检测模型的可解释性分析，但与本课题"轻量化"方向不直接相关；2) 论文中"策略评估认证"的编辑-验证循环思路，在模型剪枝/量化后性能恢复场景中可能有启发，但需要大幅改造。总体而言，不建议作为本课题的核心参考。

Abstract: A trained deep reinforcement learning policy is a black box, and we ask whether it can be made explainable by rewriting it as an executable logic program that reproduces its behaviour and that a person can read, a logic engine can run, and an optimizer can edit. We present a three-stage post-hoc transformation that extracts a frozen proximal policy optimization teacher, induces an ordered rule list from its decisions in the manner of classical relational learning, and emits the result as a Prolog program whose every decision is executed by an off-the-shelf logic engine; a subsequent expansion stage edits the rule base and accepts an edit only when policy evaluation certifies a return increase. We prove four guarantees. A return-loss bound makes the distilled program a machine-checkable certificate in a finite Markov decision process, and the expansion loop improves monotonically and terminates. For the continuous-observation setting we answer whether the conversion is possible at all: the propositional threshold instantiation converts the network to arbitrary fidelity as the resolution B grows, with disagreement O(1/B) and a return gap that closes at the same rate, and a matching lower bound shows the cost is exponential in the observation dimension for an oblique decision boundary. Empirically, on a two-room key-and-door task with 16,944 reachable states the expanded Prolog program attains exact optimal return in every seed and, in a budget-capped regime, exceeds the stochastic teacher on exact return in ten of ten seeds. On three continuous-control tasks the emitted program substitutes the network, matching the neural teacher within noise on Acrobot with eleven clauses and recovering about 97% of its return on CartPole, while on the finer-control LunarLander it recovers only partially, exactly the ceiling the exponential lower bound predicts.

</details>


### [90] [A Critical Analysis of Trustworthy AI Tools, Mark Frameworks, and the Implementation Chasms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15480)
*Michael Papademas,Xenia Ziouvelou,Kostas Karpouzis,Vangelis Karkaletsis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判性分析了可信AI（TAI）的工具和信任标记框架，发现伦理关注、生命周期覆盖、利益相关者定位和工具类型存在显著不对称，强调公平性、透明性和鲁棒性，而可解释性、数字安全和环境可持续性关注不足。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) systems increasingly impact society, ensuring their ethical and trustworthy deployment has become a global priority. While a myriad of high-level ethical guidelines have emerged, criticism persists that these frameworks remain abstract and lack concrete mechanisms for implementation. This paper conducts a critical analysis of tools and trust mark frameworks intended to operationalize trustworthy AI (TAI), drawing on a comprehensive dataset from the OECD. Through empirical mapping and descriptive comparative analysis, we identify significant asymmetries in ethical focus, lifecycle coverage, stakeholder targeting, and tool typology. Our findings show a strong emphasis on fairness, transparency, and robustness, with comparatively little attention paid to explainability, digital security, and environmental sustainability. Moreover, most tools and certifications concentrate on post-development stages, with limited guidance for early design or data collection phases. Educational initiatives and policy engagement are notably underdeveloped, suggesting that current TAI efforts are dominated by technical and procedural measures within industry contexts. We argue that bridging the persistent chasm between AI principles and practice requires expanding ethical objectives, embedding ethics across the AI lifecycle, and fostering broader multi-stakeholder participation. This study provides both a diagnosis of existing implementation gaps and actionable recommendations for advancing more holistic, inclusive, and enforceable AI governance

</details>

Abstract: Logic and optimization can, in combination, make valuable contributions to rule-based AI. Logic is the obvious medium for encoding a rule base and drawing inferences from it, while optimization provides a powerful technology for computing inferences. Their combination has taken on new relevance amid a growing concern for transparency in AI. which is important for reproducibility, explainability, trustworthiness, and fairness. Rule-based AI provides a natural solution to transparency that is becoming increasingly practical due to today's highly advanced optimization methods. This article surveys several areas of logic-optimization partnership, including probabilistic logic, Bayesian logic, belief logics and Dempster-Shafer theory, nonmonotonic (default) logic, many-valued logics, and inference of logical formulas from noisy data based on Boolean regression. It shows how to compute projections, the fundamental problem of both logic and optimization, using decision diagrams and logic-based Benders decomposition. It describes the use of postoptimality analysis to explain how conclusions are reached, further enhancing transparency, as well as the role of optimization in answer set programming modulo theories. The paper concludes by suggesting possible future research directions.

</details>


### [92] [SeerGuard: A Safety Framework for Mobile GUI Agents via World Model Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15550)
*Xue Yu,Bo Yuan,Pengshuai Yang,Kailin Zhao,Hong Hu,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SeerGuard，一个面向移动GUI智能体的后果感知安全框架，通过指令级筛选和动作级风险评估来预防执行前的错误操作。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是移动GUI智能体的安全框架，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Model architecture: SeerGuard框架包含指令级筛选和动作级风险评估；核心组件为Safety-Augmented World Model (SAWM)，通过多任务学习实现语义下一状态预测与安全风险评估的联合建模。

Datasets and metrics: 评估指标：safety-utility score（安全-效用分数，ω=0.8时从0.191提升至0.596）、risk-cost score（风险-成本分数，α=0.8时从0.347降至0.130）；数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上验证SeerGuard有效性；消融实验验证了指令级筛选、动作风险评估和下一状态预测三个模块的有效性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何相关借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于移动GUI智能体安全领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Mobile graphical user interface (GUI) agents have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in automating complex tasks, yet they introduce critical safety risks where a single erroneous action can lead to irreversible consequences. Existing safety mechanisms are primarily reactive, lacking the ability to assess risks before execution. In this paper, we introduce SeerGuard, a consequence-aware safety framework designed to mitigate these risks through pre-execution instruction-level screening and action-level risk assessment. Specifically, the action-level assessment analyzes agent-proposed actions within current GUI states, anticipating likely outcomes to identify risks before they are executed. To enable these capabilities, we construct a unified safety-augmented world model (SAWM) via multi-task learning, integrating semantic next-state prediction with safety risk assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SeerGuard generalizes effectively across diverse mobile GUI agents. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, it increases the safety-utility score from $0.191$ to $0.596$ at $ω=0.8$ and reduces the risk-cost score from $0.347$ to $0.130$ at $α=0.8$. Further analyses on our SAWM validate the effectiveness of the instruction-level screening, alongside the capability of action risk assessment and next-state prediction.

</details>


### [93] [MGDT: MLLM-Guided Diffusion Transformer with Relation-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15592)
*Xu Hou,Meiyu Liang,Wei Huang,Yawen Li,Zhe Xue,Wu Liu,Guanhua Ye,Lei Shi,Kangkang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MGDT框架，用MLLM引导的扩散Transformer和关系自适应MoE模块完成多模态知识图谱补全，核心是"先对齐再扩散"范式。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是多模态知识图谱补全（MKGC），属于知识图谱与自然语言处理领域，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。虽然使用了多模态（文本+视觉）和扩散模型，但应用场景和目标完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多模态知识图谱补全（MKGC）；场景：知识图谱推理；传感器/数据：结构化的知识图谱三元组、文本描述、视觉图像（非遥感图像）；目标类别：知识图谱中的实体和关系。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: MGDT框架包含三个核心模块：(1) Relation-Adaptive Semantic Routing Mixture-of-Experts (RASR-MoE) 用于选择关系相关的多模态语义变换路径；(2) 冻结的Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) 作为语义锚点进行跨模态对齐；(3) Knowledge Graph Diffusion Transformer (KGDT) 在对齐空间中进行图条件去噪生成。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。从模型设计（MLLM+Diffusion Transformer）来看，计算开销极大，不适合星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 三个基准数据集（摘要未说明具体名称）；评价指标摘要未说明具体名称。

Experiments: 在三个基准数据集上，MGDT持续优于强基线方法。具体对比方法和消融实验结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；(2) 模型依赖MLLM和扩散Transformer，计算开销巨大，不具备轻量化特性；(3) 未讨论推理速度、参数量等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点极少。唯一可能相关的思路是：其"Relation-Adaptive Semantic Routing MoE"模块中"抑制无关模态干扰"的思想，在遥感多模态（如光学+SAR）目标检测中，或许可借鉴类似的门控机制来选择对当前检测任务最有用的模态特征。但整体方向差异过大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Multimodal Knowledge Graph Completion (MKGC) requires inferring missing entities from structural, textual, and visual cues. Existing diffusion-based MKGC methods usually denoise directly on raw multimodal features. Such a design forces the denoiser to simultaneously perform relation-dependent cue selection, cross-modal semantic alignment, and structure-aware entity generation, which introduces noisy and semantically inconsistent conditions for diffusion and consequently leads to suboptimal completion performance. To address this limitation, we propose MGDT: MLLM-Guided Diffusion Transformer with Relation-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts (MGDT), a novel MKGC framework built on an align-then-diffuse paradigm. MGDT first employs a Relation-Adaptive Semantic Routing Mixture-of-Experts (RASR-MoE) module to select relation-relevant multimodal semantic transformation paths and suppress irrelevant modality interference. MGDT then uses a frozen Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a semantic anchor to align the routed multimodal representations into a unified latent space and reduce cross-modal semantic heterogeneity. Finally, a Knowledge Graph Diffusion Transformer (KGDT) performs graph-conditioned denoising generation in the aligned space to produce the missing entity representation. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that MGDT consistently outperforms strong baselines.

</details>


### [94] [Neuro-Symbolic AI for LEED compliance: Document-Centric Benchmarking, Deterministic Numeric Checking, and When Multimodal Hurts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15647)
*Aritro De,Juliana Felkner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号管道，使用本地部署的4B参数小语言模型（gemma3:4b）对LEED v4.1 BD+C建筑认证文档进行合规性筛查，结合确定性数值检查器处理定量阈值，在484份PDF上达到67.3%准确率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是建筑认证（LEED）文档的合规性筛查，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化模型在遥感场景下的部署。虽然使用了小模型（4B参数）进行本地部署，但这是NLP文档处理任务，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化无直接关系。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：LEED v4.1 BD+C建筑认证文档合规性筛查；场景：建筑认证审核；传感器类型：PDF文档、低分辨率图纸图像（150-300 dpi）；目标类别：LEED信用项（如EA-p2等）。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 神经符号管道（neuro-symbolic pipeline）：(1) 文档对齐模块（PDF对齐到LEED信用章节）；(2) 信用感知关键词签名检索模块；(3) 本地部署的4B参数语言模型（gemma3:4b）作为核心验证器；(4) LEED特定数值检查器（确定性符号组件）。对比模型包括llama3.1:8b。

Lightweight method: 使用了小参数模型（4B参数）进行本地部署，但这不是传统意义上的模型轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等），而是直接选用参数量较小的预训练语言模型。摘要未说明任何剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏或NAS技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量（仅提及4B和8B参数规模）、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。虽然模型是"locally deployed"，但这是指本地服务器部署，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：4栋大学建筑的484份PDF文档，153个信用级别决策。评价指标：准确率（accuracy）。

Experiments: gemma3:4b在文本核心验证任务上达到67.3%准确率，优于llama3.1:8b（更大模型反而更差）。确定性数值检查器将EA-p2从50%提升到100%准确率。完整神经符号管道达到61.6%准确率，低于纯文本基线。消融实验表明：低分辨率图纸图像（150-300 dpi）加入后持续降低准确率；提示词有效性取决于建筑的PASS率。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴。论文本身局限：整体准确率仅61.6%，低于纯文本基线；提取失败和保守行为导致性能下降；图像信息反而降低准确率。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点：(1) 小模型（4B参数）在某些任务上可以超越大模型（8B参数）的现象，提示在遥感轻量化中并非模型越小越好，需要任务匹配；(2) 神经符号混合架构的思路——确定性规则组件可以纠正模型错误，这在遥感目标检测中可类比为后处理规则修正检测结果；(3) 消融实验的设计思路（逐步移除组件观察影响）。但整体借鉴价值很低。

Abstract: LEED v4.1 BD+C certification remains a document-intensive process that requires reviewers to read hundreds of pages of project evidence and apply credit-specific threshold logic by hand. This paper investigates whether small, locally deployed language models can perform meaningful screening of LEED documentation and how deterministic symbolic components should share that work. A neuro-symbolic pipeline is introduced that aligns project PDFs to LEED credit sections, retrieves evidence with credit-aware keyword signatures, verifies compliance with a locally hosted 4-billion-parameter language model, and applies a LEED-specific numeric checker to quantitative thresholds. Experiments on four university buildings (484 PDFs, 153 credit-level decisions) show that a 4-billion-parameter model (gemma3:4b) is the strongest text-only core verifier, achieving 67.3% accuracy and outperforming a larger 8-billion-parameter model (llama3.1:8b) in this task. The deterministic numeric checker corrects arithmetic errors on key quantitative credits, moving EA-p2 from 50% to 100% accuracy and improving several other credits when required values are reliably extracted. At the same time, the full neuro-symbolic configuration achieves 61.6% overall accuracy, trailing the best text-only baseline due to extraction failures and conservative behavior on qualitative categories. Systematic ablations show that adding low-resolution drawing images (150-300 dpi) consistently reduces accuracy, and that prompt effectiveness depends on the building's ground-truth PASS rate: rubric prompts perform best on documentation-rich projects, while chain-of-thought prompts perform best on documentation-lean projects. Within the specific scope of LEED v4.1 BD+C compliance verification over raw project documentation, this pipeline and its baselines provide an initial reproducible reference point for both accuracy and failure modes.

</details>


### [95] [ToolVerse: Unlocking Massive Environments and Long-Horizon Tasks for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15660)
*Shuaiyu Zhou,Fengpeng Yue,Zengjie Hu,Yuanzhe Shen,Chenyang Zhang,feng hong,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ToolVerse框架，通过近400个真实MCP协议和约4500个工具构建大规模智能体强化学习环境，解决LLM在长时序工具集成推理中的鲁棒性问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦LLM agent在工具集成推理（TIR）任务中的强化学习训练，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM agent工具集成推理（Tool-Integrated Reasoning）；应用场景：通用智能体环境；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于LLM的agent框架，包含ToolVerse环境构建、GUST数据集、Turn-Aware Relative Advantage算法。不涉及视觉/遥感模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：GUST（Graph Unlocking Sampling Tasks）；评价指标：agentic benchmarks上的性能表现；不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 在多个agentic benchmark上进行了实验，结果表明框架显著提升了LLM在长时序工具使用中的能力。未与遥感目标检测方法对比。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM agent领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: While LLM agents demonstrate strong reasoning abilities in compact and well-defined scenarios, they struggle to maintain robustness and effectiveness when faced with large-scale, diverse, and dynamic real-world environments that demand seamless tool integration. To address this gap, we introduce ToolVerse, a comprehensive framework that scales up agentic RL environments and enables agents to perform complex long-horizon reasoning in Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) tasks. First, ToolVerse automatically builds the massive executable agent training environments from nearly 400 real-world Model Context Protocols (MCPs) that contain about 4500 tools. Second, we propose a task design strategy based on a tool dependency graph, utilizing Dynamic Unlocking Sampling Algorithm to generate long-horizon tasks, and produce GUST (Graph Unlocking Sampling Tasks) dataset. Third, to alleviate the credit assigment problem in long-horizon agentic RL, we propose a fine-grained Turn-Aware Relative Advantage algorithm. We conduct extensive Agentic RL training using ToolVerse and evaluate our framework on serveral agentic benchmarks. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework significantly strengthens LLMs' capabilities in long-horizon tool use, achieving a marked performance boost and showcasing robust reasoning within dynamic environments.

</details>


### [96] [S1-Omni: A Unified Multimodal Reasoning Model for Scientific Understanding, Prediction, and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15686)
*Jiahao Zhao,Junyi Liu,Lifeng Xu,Nan Xu,Qingli Wang,Qingxiao Li,Tianle Chen,Xiaoyu Wu,Yawen Zheng,Zikai Wang,Guanming Liu,Hequn Zhou,Jingyi Wang,Jingyuan Shu,Keqi Wang,Li He,Songyang Diao,Wenhui Xu,Xinyu Ren,Yaqin Fan,Yujin Zhou,Zhanao Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: S1-Omni是一个面向科学理解、预测和生成的统一多模态推理模型，将异构科学数据、科学规律和专家知识整合到单一模型中，在200个科学任务上训练并超越GPT-5.5等通用模型。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于AI for Science（AI4S）领域的通用科学推理模型，涉及化学（CIF、SMILES）、蛋白质序列、光谱等科学数据，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感相关内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：科学多模态推理（property prediction, spectrum-to-molecular generation, protein site/structure prediction, scientific image generation）。应用场景：通用科学AI。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 三组件架构：(1) 统一科学数据表示（将自然语言指令与CIF、SMILES、蛋白质序列、光谱、科学图像映射到共享表示空间）；(2) 自然-世界知识对齐（融入科学规律和专家知识）；(3) 领域特定任务解码。训练数据：S1-Omni-Corpus（200个科学任务，数百万推理样本）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。模型规模可能较大（需与GPT-5.5和Gemini-3.1-Pro对比），不适用于星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 训练集：S1-Omni-Corpus（200个科学任务，数百万推理样本）。评估：超过60个科学基准。指标：与GPT-5.5、Gemini-3.1-Pro及领域专用模型对比的benchmark性能。

Experiments: 在大多数基准上超越GPT-5.5和Gemini-3.1-Pro，在若干基准上匹配或超越领域专用模型。

Limitations: (1) 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/地球观测领域；(2) 未讨论模型轻量化或边缘/星上部署；(3) 未涉及目标检测任务；(4) 模型规模和推理效率未知，可能不适合资源受限场景。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接关联。唯一可抽象借鉴的点是"多模态统一表示"的思路，但遥感领域已有类似工作（如遥感视觉-语言模型），且该论文未涉及轻量化，参考价值有限。

Abstract: We present S1-Omni, a unified multimodal reasoning model for scientific understanding, prediction, and generation. AI for Science (AI4S) has advanced significantly through domain-specific models, tool-augmented LLMs, and scientific language models. However, model capabilities remain highly fragmented, limiting the joint modeling of heterogeneous data, scientific laws, and expert knowledge. S1-Omni addresses this gap by consolidating these capabilities into a single, coherent scientific reasoning model. The architecture of S1-Omni is built upon three core components: unified representation of scientific data, natural-world knowledge alignment, and decoding for domain-specific tasks. First, S1-Omni maps natural-language instructions and scientific objects, including CIF, SMILES, protein sequences, spectra, and scientific images, into a shared representation space. Second, it incorporates scientific laws and expert knowledge into data construction and training, enabling the model to reason from scientific evidence. Third, it performs task-specific decoding to support a broad range of applications, including property prediction, spectrum-to-molecular generation, protein site and structure prediction, and scientific image generation and editing. S1-Omni is trained on S1-Omni-Corpus, which covers 200 scientific tasks and contains millions of reasoning samples, and is evaluated on over 60 scientific benchmarks. It outperforms GPT-5.5 and Gemini-3.1-Pro on most benchmarks and matches or surpasses domain-specific models on several benchmarks. Overall, S1-Omni provides a practical path toward unified scientific modeling.

</details>


### [97] [Behavioral Controllability of Agentic Models for Information Extraction: From Fixed Workflows to Reflective Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15715)
*Lujia Zhang,Xingzhou Chen,Hongwei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究的是基于大语言模型(LLM)代理的学术论文数据集信息提取任务，比较固定工作流与反射式代理变体的性能差异。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于NLP领域的LLM agent在信息提取任务中的行为分析，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：学术论文中的数据集信息提取（NLP/信息抽取任务）；应用场景：学术文献处理；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: LLM agent架构，包含固定工作流基线、反射式代理变体、优化代理条件(S2)等组件，涉及reflection、memory、tool selection等机制。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：学术会议论文PDF（conference-paper dataset）；评价指标：过程级行为（工具执行、重试、反射、内存使用、运行时、故障恢复），提取覆盖率和字段完整性作为次要指标。

Experiments: 比较固定工作流基线与反射式代理变体，以及优化代理条件(S2)的表现，重点分析agent机制何时改变系统行为、是否改进任务完成度、故障模式如何驱动优化设计。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感、检测或轻量化内容。
