Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used for complex information-extraction tasks, yet it remains unclear whether agentic components such as reflection and memory lead to observable and controllable improvements over fixed LLM workflows. We study this question through conference-paper dataset extraction, where a system must identify datasets mentioned in scholarly PDFs and produce structured records. We compare a fixed workflow baseline with reflective agent variants and specify an optimized agent condition (S2) that extends the same task with richer PDF tools and dynamic tool selection. Our evaluation emphasizes process-level behavior--including tool execution, retries, reflection, memory use, runtime, and failure recovery--while treating extraction coverage and field completeness as secondary outcome measures. The paper characterizes when agentic mechanisms change system behavior, whether these changes improve task completion, and how the observed failure modes motivate an optimized agent design under the same evaluation harness.

</details>


### [98] [NeurOWL: An LLM-Based Neural-symbolic Framework for Incomplete OWL Ontology Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15776)
*Hui Yang,Jiaoyan Chen,Yiping Song,Renate Schmidt,Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NeurOWL，一个面向不完整本体（ontology）的包含关系推理与溯因的神经符号框架，利用大语言模型和本体嵌入进行语义验证与缺失公理生成。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于本体工程（ontology engineering）和语义推理（semantic reasoning），属于知识表示与推理领域，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：本体包含关系推理（subsumption reasoning）与溯因（abduction）；场景：医疗、生物信息学等领域的本体补全；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: NeurOWL神经符号框架，结合大语言模型（LLM）和本体嵌入（ontology embeddings）进行端到端推理。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 使用多个领域的真实本体（real-world ontologies）进行评测，具体数据集名称和评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在多个领域的真实本体上验证，表现出强且鲁棒的跨领域性能，但具体数值和对比方法摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。本体推理任务的计算开销可能较大，但摘要未讨论效率问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于知识图谱/本体推理方向，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无交叉。

Abstract: OWL ontologies provide a formal knowledge representation framework that enables semantic reasoning, and have been widely adopted across domains such as healthcare and bioinformatics. In practice, however, real-world ontologies are often incomplete, which pose challenges for reasoning. In this work, we focus on a fundamental subsumption reasoning problem: given an incomplete ontology and a candidate (non-entailed) subsumption, determine whether the subsumption is semantically plausible and, if so, providing a logically sound explanation containing potential missing axioms. This task unifies subsumption verification with ontology abduction, and generalizes the latter by removing the need for a predefined candidate set of missing axioms. To address this subsumption reasoning problem, we propose NeurOWL, an end-to-end neuro-symbolic framework that jointly performs verification and abduction, leveraging both formally defined semantics and textual semantics through Large Language Models and ontology embeddings. We evaluate NeurOWL on real-world ontologies across multiple domains, demonstrating strong and robust performance across different domains.

</details>


### [99] [AgentFAIR: A Multi-Agent Collaborative Framework for FAIRness Evaluation of Geospatial Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15781)
*Ming Chen,Pranav Pai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentFAIR多智能体框架，利用LLM评估地理空间数据集的FAIR（可发现、可访问、可互操作、可重用）合规性，而非遥感目标检测或模型轻量化方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦地理空间数据集的FAIR合规性评估，不涉及遥感图像目标检测、模型轻量化、星上部署等核心问题。虽然提及"geospatial datasets"与遥感数据有一定关联，但研究目标、方法和技术路线完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：数据集的FAIR合规性评估；应用场景：地理空间数据管理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明任何遥感目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: AgentFAIR多智能体框架：包含结构化元数据提取模块 + 13个子原则专用LLM评估器 + 批评者（critic）模块。每个评估器输出0-3分成熟度评分、引用证据和建议；批评者检查证据和一致性，可触发针对性重新评估。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未说明任何剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。仅提及API成本约0.054美元/数据集，但这与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：来自10个数据仓库的50个地理空间数据集（其中15个用于专家研究）；评价指标：标准化分数标准差（平均15.0pp，最高30.3pp）、Findability/Accessibility/Interoperability/Reusability得分（79.7%/70.4%/45.3%/72.0%）、秩相关系数（0.31-0.61）、子原则一致性（89% vs 71%）、Fleiss' kappa（0.71）、专家一致性（82%）。

Experiments: 与4种基线工具对比，秩相关系数0.31-0.61；10数据集重复运行子原则一致性89%（有批评者）vs 71%（无批评者）；15数据集专家研究Fleiss' kappa=0.71，与专家共识对齐82%。

Limitations: 基准测试有限、消融实验不完整、仅使用单模型家族验证，限制了准确性和泛化性的结论。与遥感目标检测轻量化方向完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文不涉及遥感目标检测或模型轻量化，无法为"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题提供技术参考。

Abstract: Geospatial datasets support applications from urban planning to climate modeling, yet consistent assessment of FAIR compliance is difficult. Existing evaluators use different rubrics and evidence sources and may fail on JavaScript-rendered pages or repository-specific identifiers. For 50 datasets from 10 repositories, the standard deviation of normalized scores across available tools averages 15.0 percentage points and reaches 30.3 for one dataset. Because these outputs are not equivalent measurements, we use them to characterize disagreement and failure modes, not comparative accuracy. We present AgentFAIR, a multi-agent framework combining structured metadata extraction with 13 sub-principle-specific LLM evaluators. Each produces a 0-3 maturity score, cited evidence, and recommendations; a critic checks evidence and consistency and can request targeted re-evaluation. Mean Findability, Accessibility, Interoperability, and Reusability scores are 79.7%, 70.4%, 45.3%, and 72.0%. Rank correlations with four baseline tools range from 0.31 to 0.61; the FAIR-enough comparison is not statistically significant. On a 10-dataset repeated-run subset, sub-principle agreement averages 89% (standard deviation: 3 percentage points), versus 71% without the critic. A preliminary 15-dataset expert study yields Fleiss' kappa of 0.71 and 82% alignment with expert consensus. API cost is approximately USD 0.054 per dataset. These results support auditability and feasibility, while the limited benchmark, incomplete ablations, and single-model-family validation constrain claims about accuracy and generalization.

</details>


### [100] [Knowledge-Centric Agents for Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15845)
*Zhendong Li,Lei Sun,Ruibo Ming,He Zhang,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种知识驱动的框架，通过知识反转、注入和推理来生成ComfyUI视觉创作系统中的工作流，而非直接文本到JSON的生成。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于视觉创作系统（如ComfyUI）中的工作流生成，属于AIGC/多模态LLM应用领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：工作流生成（workflow generation）；场景：视觉创作系统（ComfyUI）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 知识驱动框架，包含知识反转（knowledge inversion）、知识注入（knowledge injection）和可逆推理（reversible reasoning），基于LLM进行分层推理。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：大规模真实工作流集合（large collections of real-world workflows）；评价指标：节点多样性（node diversity）、结构连贯性（structural coherence）、执行成功率（execution success rate）。

Experiments: 实验表明该方法相比现有系统生成的工作流具有更丰富的节点多样性、更连贯的结构和更高的执行成功率。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。与遥感目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术细节。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。如果强行关联，知识蒸馏中的"知识反转"思想（从大规模数据中提取分层表示）在概念层面与知识蒸馏有微弱相似性，但方法本身面向的是文本到结构化输出的生成任务，而非模型压缩或遥感检测，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Workflow generation in visual creation systems such as ComfyUI demands not only syntactic accuracy but also expert-level reasoning over modular compositions. Existing large language model (LLM) approaches often treat this as a direct text-to-JSON generation task, struggling with structural brittleness and lacking the experiential knowledge required for effective design. We argue that successful workflow generation requires modeling knowledge itself, including its structure, hierarchy, and reasoning dynamics. To this end, we propose a knowledge-centric framework that learns to invert, inject, and infer with knowledge across multiple abstraction levels. We first perform knowledge inversion to distill hierarchical representations, ranging from full pseudo-codes and skeletons to high-level strategies, from large collections of real-world workflows. We then conduct knowledge injection through supervised fine-tuning, teaching the model to reason from task descriptions to strategies and from strategies to executable structures. During inference, the model performs reversible reasoning to synthesize executable workflows, augmented by self-refinement for structural coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces workflows with richer node diversity, more coherent structures, and higher execution success rates than existing systems, establishing a new foundation for knowledge-driven, agentic workflow generation.

</details>


### [101] [DSWorld: A Data Science World Model for Efficient Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15901)
*Zherui Yang,Fan Liu,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出数据科学世界模型DSWorld，通过预测数据科学操作的环境状态转移来加速自动化数据科学流程，而非直接面向遥感或目标检测任务。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关系。论文聚焦于自动化数据科学（AutoDS）领域的执行效率优化，属于泛机器学习/数据科学工作流优化方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：数据科学工作流的状态转移预测与加速；应用场景：自动化数据科学代理（AutoDS agent）的训练与推理加速；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: DSWorld框架包含：结构化状态构建（structured state construction）、成本感知路由（cost-aware routing）、轻量级真实执行（lightweight real execution）、基于LLM的模拟器（LLM-based simulator）。核心是数据科学世界模型（Data Science World Model），用于预测环境状态转移。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的"lightweight real execution"是DSWorld框架的一个组件，用于对低成本操作进行真实执行，而对昂贵操作使用LLM模拟器替代。但这并非模型压缩/剪枝/量化/蒸馏等传统意义上的轻量化方法，而是数据科学工作流层面的执行优化策略。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注的是数据科学代理的训练和推理加速（RL训练加速约14倍，搜索推理加速约3-6倍），而非边缘/星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自构建的8K规模转移轨迹数据集（8K-scale transition trajectory dataset）；评价指标：转移预测任务上的性能（比最强LLM基线高35.6%），以及RL训练加速比和搜索推理加速比。

Experiments: DSWorld加速RL-based agent训练约14倍，加速search-based inference约3-6倍，同时保持竞争性能；在转移预测任务上比最强LLM基线高35.6%。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 摘要未说明模型参数量、计算开销等轻量化核心指标；3. 依赖LLM模拟器，本身计算开销可能较大，不适合资源受限的星上场景。

Ideas for my research: 1. "World Model"思想——预测操作效果后再执行——在遥感在轨场景中可类比为：预测轻量化模型在不同压缩策略下的精度/速度权衡，避免穷举搜索；2. "cost-aware routing"思路可借鉴到星上多模型调度：根据当前资源状态（功耗、算力余量）动态选择不同轻量化版本的目标检测模型；3. 但整体而言，该论文与课题方向差距较大，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Despite strong capabilities in data understanding and decision-making, autonomous data science agents still heavily rely on trial-and-error workflows that involve expensive computation. This bottleneck motivates models that can anticipate the effects of data science operations before real execution. In this paper, we introduce the concept of Data Science World Model, which model the data science execution environment by predicting environment state transitions conditioned on current workflow states and candidate operations. We further propose DSWorld, a practical framework that combines structured state construction, cost-aware routing, lightweight real execution, and an LLM-based simulator for expensive operations. To support training, we construct an 8K-scale transition trajectory dataset and introduce Reflective World Model Optimization, an error-aware reinforcement learning strategy for improving transition prediction. Experiments show that DSWorld accelerates RL-based agent training by approximately $14\times$ and search-based inference by approximately $3$-$6\times$ while maintaining competitive performance, and outperforms the strongest LLM baseline by 35.6% on transition prediction tasks. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DSWorld.

</details>


### [102] [A Formally Grounded ODRL Evaluator: Implementation and Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15987)
*Jaime Osvaldo Salas,Paolo Pareti,Adeel Aslam,Christopher Maidens,George Konstantinidis*

Abstract: The ODRL policy language is emerging as the de-facto standard for policy modelling data access and usage preferences, AI governance policies and data workflows in European dataspaces. The current standard has no mathematical formal semantics to describe how a system should implement policy evaluation. This has resulted in a variety of systems and tools that implement their own interpretation of the language, which limits interoperability and cannot guarantee consistent results. Based on an existing semantic model of ODRL, we formalise the problems of ODRL evaluation for the access control and monitoring scenarios, in both static and streaming settings, and we provide a novel, efficient algorithm and implementation. We present the first ODRL Evaluator with transparent formal semantics and supporting all rule types. We experimentally measure its performance, analysing different scalability dimensions related to policy complexity and size of the data on which a policy is evaluated. We compare our system with the state-of-the-art by providing a comparative review of existing ODRL evaluators, which highlights the differences in supported ODRL features and evaluation modes.

</details>


### [103] [Closing the AI Trust Gap: The Case for Independent Certification for Trustworthy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15992)
*Trisevgeni Papakonstantinou,Cansu Canca,Farah Nanji,Waheedullah Pardess,Jen Weedon,Jasmijn Remmers,Eliza Krigman,Matthew Ball,Yalda Daryani,Kiran Iqbal,Francielle Vargas,María Llorente Sánchez,Joe Humphreys,Fendi Tsim,Kelly Fitzpatrick,Jeff Dunn,Catherine Feldman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文讨论负责任AI与可信AI之间的信任差距，提出以结果为导向的独立认证机制来弥补这一差距，属于AI治理与伦理领域，与遥感、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文属于AI治理、负责任AI（Responsible AI）与可信AI（Trustworthy AI）的社会技术研究领域，讨论的是AI系统的认证、监管与市场信号机制。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等任何技术内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型结构或网络设计。

Abstract: Over the past decade, responsible AI (RAI) has produced a substantial body of practice for identifying and mitigating the risks AI poses in high-stakes settings. Yet this work has not produced a market that rewards trustworthiness. Firms that invest seriously in safety, fairness, and oversight cannot consistently prove to consumers, regulators, and shareholders that their systems go beyond the bare minimum of compliance. What is missing is a way for society to recognize or compare the difference. The result is a trust gap: a structural condition in which responsible development efforts happen inside organizations but produce no external, independently recognized and verifiable signal of trustworthy outcomes. We argue this gap is sustained in part because of a focus on responsible AI (a matter of internal process) as opposed to trustworthy AI (a matter of independently verifiable real-world outcomes), and that it persists because of three compounding failures: (1) the market cannot distinguish trustworthy systems from their imitations; (2) evaluation targets models and outputs rather than deployed sociotechnical systems and their outcomes; (3) the measurement ecosystem is oriented toward avoiding harm rather than demonstrating benefit. Reviewing existing AI governance instruments and comparing them to certification regimes in healthcare, sustainability, and security, we show that none integrate a governance baseline, independently verified positive-outcome evidence, and market signaling in a single framework. We propose independent, outcome-oriented certification as the connective layer that can close the trust gap, complementing regulation and internal governance by making trustworthiness measurable, comparable, and commercially rewarded.

</details>


### [104] [SciForge: An AI-Native, Multimodal Workbench for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16038)
*SciForge Team,Zhangyang Gao,Minghao Fang,Yifei Liu,Hanhui Yang,Xinyu Gu,Shixiang Tang,Siqi Sun,Lei Bai,Cheng Tan,Mengdi Liu,Hao Wu,Shuizhou Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciForge是一个面向科学研究的AI工作台，用于管理论文、代码、数据集等多模态研究工件，支持智能体驱动的搜索、解析、工作流执行等功能，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向无任何交集。

Abstract: Scientific work increasingly spans heterogeneous artifacts -- papers, code, datasets, scientific file formats, model outputs, figures, manuscripts, and team decisions -- yet general-purpose AI assistants rarely preserve these objects as a coherent, auditable research state. We present SciForge, a multimodal research-native AI workbench that reserves the graphical interface for human judgment while search, parsing, model routing, workflow execution, plotting, writing, and presentation generation run as modular agent-accessible services. SciForge is built around five pillars: (i) \emph{goal-scoped scientific decision governance} for \textbf{goal-oriented} research, with review gates and shared review surfaces; (ii) \emph{translate-then-reason} for \textbf{multimodal} input, routing scientific objects through domain translators before the agent reasons; (iii) \emph{evidence governance} for \textbf{auditable} traceability, linking claims to provenance chains and audit findings; (iv) \emph{collaborative team science} for \textbf{collaborative} research, enabling multi-role decision governance, with shared team workspaces planned for future releases; and (v) \emph{real-world application scenarios} for \textbf{practical} impact, demonstrated through eight end-to-end user cases, with flagship demonstrations including multi-day agentic research sprints for gene discovery, AI-guided de novo protein design, molecular optimization, and genome-to-BGC discovery. The system combines a thin interaction layer, contextual research capability patterns, an Agent Runtime and Workflow Engine, an Evidence-DAG audit sidecar and a Scientific Model Router. SciForge currently runs as a desktop application, with mobile supervision support; future releases will deepen team collaboration. The system is open-source and available at https://github.com/AGI4Sci/SciForge

</details>

Abstract: Frontier AI companies have published capability thresholds that differ substantially, making it difficult for third parties to verify whether a threshold has been crossed or to compare requirements across companies. Moreover, without common minimum thresholds, risk mitigation may be inconsistent, creating a potential race to the bottom in safety standards. We develop a methodology for deriving harmonized thresholds across three risk domains. For misuse risks (cyber and biological), we take expected harm as the key primitive and use an explicit risk-modeling approach that accounts for risk channels and model release conditions. For automated AI R&D, we base our proposed threshold on the observed rate of AI progress rather than expected harm. Our analysis expands upon prior work and highlights existing empirical gaps and limitations.

</details>

Experiments: 在四个开源模型上，将CRAFT与EvalTree聚类和随机生成基线进行对比。CRAFT在金融领域四个模型均取得最强平均性能；在法律领域四个模型中有三个最强，另一个在基线解码方差范围内。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对大语言模型，不涉及视觉或遥感任务；2. 未讨论计算开销和部署效率；3. 仅验证了金融和法律两个专业领域，泛化性有限；4. 摘要未说明诊断过程的计算成本。

Ideas for my research: 虽然论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化无关，但其"能力诊断→针对性改进"的思路有一定借鉴意义：1. 可类比思考如何诊断轻量化模型在遥感目标检测中的薄弱环节（如小目标、密集场景、旋转目标等），从而有针对性地设计轻量化策略或数据增强方案；2. 层次化分析思路可用于分析不同轻量化方法在不同遥感场景下的性能退化模式。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Evaluations should do more than measure a models current performance. They should tell us what to fix for the next model iteration and provide a way to generate targeted post training data. Most evaluation pipelines identify weak examples, topics, or categories, but they leave the underlying capability failure implicit: they say where a model fails, not why. We introduce CRAFT, a method that converts any rubric based evaluation dataset into a model specific diagnosis of weak capabilities. CRAFT treats each grading criterion as a capability probe: it extracts a capability description from every prompt rubric pair, clusters these descriptions into a hierarchical capability tree, scores the target model at every node, and selects low performing nodes dynamically across tree levels, at the granularity where each failure is clearest. The selected weak capabilities then direct the generation of targeted supervised finetuning data. Holding the data generation, finetuning, and evaluation setup fixed, we compare CRAFT against prompt level EvalTree clustering and untargeted random generation on four open source models, two professional domains (finance and legal), and 13 held out benchmarks disjoint from the diagnostic data. CRAFT achieves the strongest finance domain average for all four models under repeated temperature decoding; on legal domain, it is strongest for three of four models and remains within the decoding variance bands of the best baseline on the fourth. Diagnosing weaknesses at the level of rubric criteria, rather than prompts or categories, thus yields both a sharper picture of what a model cannot do and measurably better models after finetuning on that diagnosis.

</details>


### [107] [Beyond a Single Direction: Chain-of-Thought Disrupts Simple Steering of Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26772)
*Kia-Jüng Yang,Dominik Meier,Jiachen Zhao,Terry Ruas,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究DeepSeek-R1推理大模型（LRM）中拒绝机制与思维链（CoT）的关系，发现拒绝行为由残差流激活和CoT共同编码，CoT可独立携带并重建顺从信号，使LRM对激活层干预更鲁棒。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是大语言模型（LLM/LRM）的拒绝机制（refusal）与思维链（CoT）的关系，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型安全对齐（refusal mechanism analysis）；应用场景：LLM安全控制；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B（基于LLaMA-8B蒸馏的推理模型），涉及残差流（residual stream）激活和思维链（CoT）机制。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然模型名称包含"Distill"，但论文研究的是蒸馏后模型的拒绝机制，而非蒸馏方法本身。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 实验发现：激活干预（activation steering）在CoT固定时仅39%逆转拒绝；移除CoT后提升至70%；两阶段干预（先激活干预再重新生成CoT）可达94%；单独CoT在移除干预后仍保留48%效果。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设计。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM安全对齐领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化方向无任何交集。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) generate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before producing final outputs, introducing a dynamic internal state that may complicate control mechanisms such as refusal. Unlike instruction-tuned LLMs, where refusal is mediated by a single directional subspace, refusal in large reasoning models (LRMs) additionally depends on the CoT. In DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B, activation steering reverses refusal in only 39% of cases when the CoT is kept fixed, but removing the CoT entirely increases this to 70%, indicating that the CoT actively reinforces refusal. In a two-stage intervention where the model regenerates its CoT under activation steering, refusal is reversed in 94% of cases, while the resulting CoT alone retains 48% of this effect even after steering is removed. This suggests that the CoT can carry and reconstruct the compliance signal independently. These findings indicate that refusal in LRMs is jointly encoded in residual stream activations and CoT. This joint activation makes LRM more robust against activation-level interventions alone, but exposes CoT to a possible alternative surface attack.

</details>

### [108] [Design-Based Supervised Learning with Noisy Human Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15455)
*Robert Chew,Matthew R. Williams*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出PA-DSL方法，利用仲裁标签纠正人工审计标签中的噪声，再基于纠正后的审计信息对自动标注偏差进行校正，适用于带噪声人工审计的统计学习场景。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于统计学习中的标签噪声校正与偏差消除方法，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化或边缘部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：自动标注数据的偏差校正与统计推断；应用场景：通用统计学习/自然语言处理（Wikipedia Detox实验）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构。PA-DSL是一种基于概率抽样的统计估计方法，而非深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署相关内容。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 实验使用合成数据集和Wikipedia Detox半合成数据集；评价指标为覆盖率（coverage）和RMSE（均方根误差）。

Experiments: 在合成和Wikipedia Detox实验中，PA-DSL相比仅使用仲裁标签的方法，在保持名义覆盖率的同时将RMSE降低了10-17%。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感图像或目标检测任务；2. 无任何模型轻量化或部署相关内容；3. 方法依赖已知的审计和仲裁概率，实际应用中该假设可能不成立；4. 仅在文本数据上验证，未在视觉任务上测试。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果在遥感在轨目标检测中涉及人工标注噪声校正问题，其标签纠偏思想或许有参考价值，但与本课题核心方向（轻量化检测模型）无直接关联。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use automated classifiers to label unstructured data for statistical analysis. Existing rectification methods can correct errors in these automated labels using a probability-sampled audit set, but they usually treat the audit labels as correct. In practice, human audit labels are often noisy, and only some audited items are reviewed by an expert or adjudicator. We propose Partially Adjudicated Design-Based Supervised Learning (PA-DSL), a method for this setting. It uses adjudicated cases to correct noisy human labels and then uses the corrected audit information to debias analyses based on the full set of automated labels. The estimator is valid for a broad class of downstream analyses when the audit and adjudication probabilities are known. In synthetic and Wikipedia Detox semi-synthetic experiments, PA-DSL maintains nominal coverage and reduces RMSE by 10-17% relative to using only adjudicated labels when noisy human labels contain recoverable signal.

</details>


### [109] [Retraining Seeks Stable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15623)
*Moritz Hardt*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出"稳定信号原理"来解释重复风险最小化（重训练）在表演性预测中的收敛行为，证明当存在非零稳定信号时，适当正则化的重训练会几何收敛到该信号方向，即使模型对数据的影响很大。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是表演性预测（performative prediction）中重训练过程的收敛性理论，属于机器学习理论/因果推断方向，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文是纯理论分析，未涉及具体任务、应用场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文未提出具体模型架构，而是从理论上分析重复风险最小化（repeated risk minimization）这一通用训练过程的收敛性质。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。仅提到正则化（regularization）作为控制表演性的手段，而非用于模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文为纯理论工作，未使用任何数据集或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文未进行实验验证，仅提供理论证明，并提及该视角可应用于语言建模中的数据反馈循环。

Limitations: 论文是纯理论分析，未提供实验验证；其结论是否适用于遥感卫星在轨场景中的模型重训练（如模型更新、增量学习）完全未知；稳定信号假设（目标存在模型无关的成分）在遥感目标检测中是否成立需要额外论证。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。可借鉴点非常有限：论文关于重训练收敛性的理论分析，如果未来考虑在轨模型的持续学习/增量更新场景，其中"稳定信号"的概念（目标中模型无关的固有成分）可能对设计鲁棒的重训练策略有启发，但需要大量额外工作将其映射到遥感目标检测的具体问题中。

Abstract: Predictive models deployed at scale influence future data, a phenomenon called performativity. And there is always one way to cope: Train the model on new data, deploy it again, and repeat. This process, called retraining or repeated risk minimization, creates a feedback loop between model and data that real-world learning systems can't avoid.
  Results on performative prediction shed light on this dynamic: If the model's influence on the data is small, retraining reaches a fixed point. What remains open is why fixed points should naturally exist, and what governs retraining when the model's influence is strong. In this work we develop a new perspective on retraining -- the stable signal principle -- that addresses these questions. We start from the assumption that the prediction target has at least some small model-independent component, a stable signal, such as the intrinsic quality of an item.
  We prove that when a nonzero stable signal exists, repeated risk minimization, suitably regularized, converges geometrically to the direction of this stable signal. This is true even if the model's influence on the target is arbitrarily large relative to the stable signal. Regularization emerges naturally as a force to control performativity, rather than to promote generalization, revealing a new facet of an old concept. We extend the analysis to a broad family of affine retraining operators under arbitrary model-induced feature changes, heterogeneous time-varying effects, and nonlinear responses. The stable signal perspective also applies to data feedback loops in language modeling, providing new explanations for the stability of language model training from model-generated data.

</details>


### [110] [Which Hyperparameters Matter? A Game-Theoretic Framework for Interpretable Hyperparameter Sensitivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15884)
*Nyi Nyi Aung,Heepeom Shin,Abigail Lawlor,Adrian Stein*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一个基于博弈论（Shapley Effects + Pareto前沿）的可解释超参数-目标交互分析框架，用于多目标优化场景下的超参数敏感性分析和早期模型评估，而非提出新的优化算法或检测模型。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化方法，而是聚焦于通用的超参数-目标交互可解释性分析框架，属于机器学习元研究（meta-learning / hyperparameter optimization）范畴。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明具体任务和应用场景。仅提及在三种不同神经网络架构和不同问题域的多目标设置下验证，未涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构。仅提及使用了三种不同的神经网络架构进行验证，但未给出架构名称或设计细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。该框架用于超参数分析，不涉及模型压缩或加速。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 未提供任何实验数据支撑其有效性声明；3. 框架的实际计算开销（Shapley Effects计算复杂度高）未讨论；4. 未与现有超参数分析方法进行定量对比。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果在轻量化模型的多目标优化（如精度 vs 速度 vs 参数量）中需要分析超参数敏感性，该框架的思路（Shapley Effects分析各超参数对多个目标的贡献度）可辅助搜索空间缩减。但该方向已有更成熟的工具（如贝叶斯优化、NAS中的帕累托前沿分析），且该论文本身未提供可直接复现的算法或代码。

Abstract: This work presents a game-theoretic framework for interpretable hyperparameter-objective interaction analysis rather than proposing a new optimization algorithm. In the proposed framework, Shapley Effects are employed for global sensitivity analysis, while Pareto front sets are utilized to identify effective hyperparameter configurations and support early-stage model evaluation. The resulting analysis reveals which players (hyperparameters) are most influential with respect to different objectives in a given game (application). Consequently, the proposed framework provides interpretable insights into objective-aware hyperparameter interactions, enabling practitioners to guide subsequent optimization, reduce the search space, and perform early-stage model evaluation. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is demonstrated using three distinct neural network architectures across different problem domains under multi-objective settings.

</details>


### [111] [Deep and Probabilistic Models for Gene Regulatory Network Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16053)
*Claudia Skok Gibbs*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出PMF-GRN和GLM-Prior两个框架用于基因调控网络重建，属于生物信息学领域，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是基因调控网络（GRN）重建，属于计算生物学/生物信息学领域。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未明确说明具体数据集名称和评价指标，仅提及跨物种泛化（yeast, mouse, human）。不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 摘要未提供具体实验结果、对比方法或消融实验数据。

Limitations: 该论文与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供有意义的局限性分析。摘要本身提到GRN重建面临的实际和方法论约束、不完整的参考网络、点估计输出缺乏不确定性、先验知识依赖等问题。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于生物信息学领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题定义、数据模态、任务目标、部署约束等方面均无交集。

Abstract: Gene regulatory networks (GRNs) link transcription factor (TF) proteins to their target genes, yet reconstructing these networks from genome-wide data remains challenging under practical and methodological constraints. Many methods couple modeling assumptions to a specific inference procedure and rely on heuristic model selection, while evaluation is constrained by incomplete reference networks and point-estimate outputs that lack uncertainty. GRN reconstruction also depends on prior knowledge to constrain TF-gene interactions, yet available priors are often assay-dependent and difficult to transfer across species and less-characterized systems. In this thesis, we develop two complementary frameworks that address these limitations. In the first, PMF-GRN casts GRN inference as a probabilistic graphical model optimized by variational inference, enabling principled model selection and uncertainty-aware edge estimates. In the second, GLM-Prior addresses the prior bottleneck by fine-tuning the pretrained Nucleotide Transformer to predict TF-target gene interactions directly from nucleotide sequence, while generalizing across yeast, mouse, and human settings. Together, PMF-GRN and GLM-Prior motivate a dual-stage view of GRN reconstruction in which sequence-derived priors provide a transferable starting scaffold and probabilistic inference refines regulatory estimates with quantified uncertainty under incomplete evaluation resources.

</details>


### [112] [Cluster-Aware Matching via Laplacian Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16178)
*Gabriel Samberg,YoonHaeng Hur,Yuehaw Khoo,Nir Sharon*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于拉普拉斯最优传输（LapOT）的聚类感知点云匹配方法，通过二次拉普拉斯正则化项鼓励最优传输保持点云的聚类结构，并引入RSC方法实现跨点云的一致聚类。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于点云匹配（point cloud matching）和最优传输（optimal transport），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：点云匹配（point cloud matching）、聚类感知对齐（cluster-aware alignment）。场景：通用点云匹配应用，非遥感场景。传感器类型：未说明。目标类别：未说明。

Model architecture: 核心方法：Laplacian Optimal Transport (LapOT) —— 在最优传输问题中加入二次拉普拉斯正则项；Refined Simultaneous Clustering (RSC) —— 利用LapOT得到的聚类感知耦合矩阵实现跨点云的一致聚类分割。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及通过理论分析和实验验证有效性。

Experiments: 摘要未提供定量实验结果、对比方法或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 未讨论计算效率或实时性；3. 未说明在大规模点云上的可扩展性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果未来研究涉及遥感点云（如LiDAR点云）的配准或聚类任务，LapOT中的拉普拉斯正则化思想可能对保持点云结构的一致性有参考意义，但与在轨目标检测模型轻量化的核心课题方向不匹配。

Abstract: In many applications of matching, the point clouds to be matched are not merely unstructured sets of points but rather samples from distributions with an intrinsic cluster structure. In such cases, as individual points are often interchangeable within a coherent region, finding a robust region-to-region alignment is more desirable than establishing a precise point-to-point correspondence. To this end, we propose a novel approach for cluster-aware matching based on Laplacian Optimal Transport (LapOT). The key idea is to regularize the optimal transport problem with quadratic Laplacian terms constructed from similarity graphs of the point clouds, which encourages the optimal coupling to respect the cluster structure of both point sets. We also introduce Refined Simultaneous Clustering (RSC), a method that leverages the cluster-aware coupling obtained from LapOT to produce consistent partitions across the point sets, which can overcome the limitations of independent clustering and yield more stable and interpretable results. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach through theoretical analysis and empirical experiments, showing that LapOT indeed produces cluster-aware matching that leads to more consistent and meaningful alignments between point clouds.

</details>

### [113] [Low Complexity Neural Network Digital Predistortion of Wideband Power Amplifiers through Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15441)
*Cel Thys,Rodney Martinez Alonso,Ali H Alsarraf,Dominique Schreurs,Sofie Pollin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于LASSO和MRMR特征选择的低复杂度神经网络数字预失真(DPD)架构，在保持线性化性能的同时降低30%计算复杂度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦通信领域的数字预失真(DPD)算法，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。虽然涉及神经网络复杂度降低，但应用场景和问题域完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：数字预失真(DPD)线性化；场景：通信功率放大器非线性补偿；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于特征选择的NN DPD架构，使用Phase-Normalized NN结构，通过LASSO和MRMR进行离线特征工程构建紧凑输入表示。

Lightweight method: 特征选择（Feature Selection）：使用LASSO（最小绝对收缩和选择算子）和MRMR（最小冗余最大相关性）算法进行离线特征工程，降低输入维度从而减少计算复杂度。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及计算复杂度降低30%，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自测宽带FR3功率放大器数据集（已公开）；评价指标：线性化性能（具体指标摘要未说明）、计算复杂度（降低30%）。

Experiments: 在实测宽带FR3功率放大器数据集上，所提方法相比基线NN DPD方法降低30%计算复杂度，同时保持可比的线性化性能。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等主流轻量化方法；4. 未提供参数量、FLOPs等可迁移的部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可参考的点是：LASSO+MRMR特征选择思路可用于遥感目标检测模型输入通道或特征图的冗余筛选，但需要大幅改造以适应检测任务。

Abstract: Due to the continuous increase in communication bandwidth and the use of highly efficient yet nonlinear power amplifiers, Digital Predistortion (DPD) algorithms are becoming increasingly complex. In particular, neural network (NN) based DPD approaches using Phase-Normalized NN architectures often incur substantially higher computational costs than widely deployed polynomial-based methods, such as the Memory Polynomial (MP) and Generalized Memory Polynomial (GMP) models. To bridge this gap between research performance and practical implementation, we propose a low-complexity Feature Selection NN DPD architecture. The proposed method employs an offline feature-engineering pipeline based on the Least Absolute Shrinkage and Selection Operator (LASSO) and the Minimum Redundancy Maximum Relevance (MRMR) algorithm to construct a compact and informative input representation. Using measured wideband FR3 power amplifier datasets that are publicly released with this work, we demonstrate up to 30% reduction in computational complexity while maintaining comparable linearization performance.

</details>


### [114] [Pinching Antenna-Assisted ISAC with Waveguide Mode Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15547)
*Ruotong Zhao,Yijia Zhang,Shaokang Hu,Derrick Wing Kwan Ng*

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究内容与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题在问题定义、技术路线、应用场景上均无交集。

Abstract: Conventional pinching antenna (PA)-assisted integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) architectures typically assume static receiver locations or predetermined receive waveguides, thereby underutilizing the inherent spatial degrees of freedom. This paper proposes a novel mode-selectable PA-assisted ISAC framework to maximize the post-combining sensing signal-to-noise ratio while satisfying multi-user quality-of-service constraints by jointly optimizing the waveguide mode selection, transmit beamforming, and transmit/receive PA positions. To tackle the resulting mixed-integer nonconvex optimization problem, we develop a low-complexity block-coordinate descent algorithm that leverages a penalty-based majorization-minimization method to achieve high-quality suboptimal solutions. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed design significantly outperforms both traditional PA and fixed-antenna benchmarks by synergistically harnessing spatial adaptability and modal reconfigurability. In particular, the mode-selectable design enables the coordinated optimization of transmit/receive operations and sensing-communication resource allocation, thereby maintaining sensing robustness under stringent communication requirements.

</details>

Abstract: We propose an integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) framework that supports chirp signal transmission in CP-OFDM-based multiple access communication systems, enabling efficient coexistence of communication and sensing capabilities. Our framework employs the discrete Fourier transform phase rotated and permuted frequency division multiple access (DFT-p-FDMA) waveform to transmit chirp signals using a portion of the frequency resources, while ensuring interference-free concurrent CP-OFDM data transmissions on other bands. We analyze the effective channel behavior under the DFT-p-FDMA waveform, characterizing how delays and Doppler shifts impact radar target echoes. We also show how processing multiple received symbols improves Doppler resolution in practical scenarios. Our framework allows flexible adjustment of range-Doppler resolution through optimized time-frequency resource allocation, offering a versatile solution for ISAC applications. Simulation results validate the framework's performance in delay and Doppler estimation, highlighting its potential to support ISAC in next-generation wireless networks.

</details>


### [116] [An Open-Source, Autonomous Platform for High-Resolution Energy Monitoring in Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15594)
*Vignesh Selvaraj,Aditya Nagaraj,Shengyuan Zhang,Sina Sadeghian,Sangkee Min*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一个开源、低成本、模块化的工业能源监测平台AEMS，用于高分辨率三相电压/电流采集，不涉及遥感或目标检测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦工业能源监测（Industry 4.0场景），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：工业能源监测（能量数据采集与分析）；场景：三轴CNC加工中心；传感器：三相电压/电流传感器（24-bit ADC）；目标类别：主轴、进给驱动、快速移动、材料去除等能量状态。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Datasets and metrics: 验证平台为三轴CNC加工中心，无标准遥感数据集。评价指标为可分辨的能量状态和进给速率变化检测灵敏度（50 mm/min），与目标检测mAP、FPS等指标无关。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测模型或轻量化方法。该论文是硬件平台论文，非算法论文。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心要素。

Abstract: High-resolution energy data is increasingly central to Industry 4.0, where electrical signals such as three-phase voltage and current carry rich information about machine condition, tool wear, and process dynamics. Capturing this information in practice remains difficult: commercial power analysis are largely proprietary, offer limited or no access to high-sampling rate data for transient analysis, restrict access to raw waveform data, and offer no customization, while general-purpose open hardware lacks the front-end accuracy, isolation, and robustness required for industrial measurement. This paper presents Autonomous Energy Monitoring System (AEMS), an open-source, low-cost, and modular platform supported by a host, edge-gateway, and optional cloud software stack that enables autonomous, long-duration acquisition independent of a continuously connected host and thereby closes this gap by combining research-grade fidelity with industrial deployability. The system acquires three-phase voltage and current through an isolated front-end and a 24-bit, simultaneously sampling analog-to-digital converter, managed by a dual-core architecture that separates deterministic acquisition and on-board logging from host communication and control. Industrial interfaces (Ethernet, RS-485/Modbus, and BLE) together with hardware-level synchronization enable scalable, time-aligned acquisition across multiple machines, supported by a complete host, edge-gateway, and optional cloud software stack. We validate the platform on a three-axis CNC machining center, where it resolves spindle, feed-drive, rapid-traverse, and material-removal energy states and detects feed-rate changes as small as 50 mm/min. By releasing the full hardware and firmware openly, this work aims to democratize access to high-fidelity energy monitoring for both researchers and small and medium-sized manufacturers.

</details>

Abstract: Graph signal sampling and reconstruction are commonly formulated in the graph Fourier transform (GFT) domain. However, the reconstruction performance may be limited when practical graph signals are not sufficiently concentrated in the GFT spectrum. To address this issue, this paper proposes a graph signal sampling and reconstruction framework based on the graph fractional Fourier transform (GFRFT) domain. The fractional order is introduced as an adjustable spectral domain parameter, and the optimal order is selected to provide a more suitable representation domain for a given graph signal and sampling model. Under a unified sampling reconstruction formulation, subspace, smoothness, and stochastic priors are incorporated, and both unconstrained and predefined reconstruction mechanisms are considered, leading to several fractional domain sampling and reconstruction methods. Furthermore, the theoretical analysis shows that the optimal GFRFT domain can provide a more suitable low-dimensional spectral representation by improving energy concentration and reducing projection residual. The effects of residual leakage and noise amplification are further considered to explain how this representation advantage is translated into reconstruction error reduction. Experimental results show that, GFRFT domain sampling and reconstruction generally achieve better recovery performance than GFT domain methods.

</details>


### [118] [FSIM: Fluid Element Stacked Intelligent Metasurface for Multiuser Downlink Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15651)
*Tsung-Yu Wei,Li-Hsiang Shen,Kai-Ten Feng,Lie-Liang Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种流体元辅助堆叠智能超表面(FSIM)架构用于MISO通信系统，通过优化元原子位置、波束成形和相移来提升和速率性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是无线通信系统中的智能超表面(FSIM)架构优化，属于通信物理层领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化等核心主题。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向通信系统而非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 评价指标：和速率(sum rate)；数据集：仿真数据，未使用任何遥感或目标检测标准数据集。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无直接关联，不提供可借鉴点。

Abstract: A fluid element (FE)-aided stacked intelligent metasurface (FSIM) for multiple-input single-output (MISO) communication system is investigated, where a multi-antenna base station (BS) serves multiple single-antenna users through FSIM. Unlike conventional SIM with fixed meta-atom deployment, the architecture allows the meta-atoms in each layer to move within a predefined fluidic region to further increase the spatial diversity. By jointly optimizing the two-dimensional meta-atom positions, BS transmit beamforming, and FSIM phase-shifts, the cascaded BS-FSIM-user channels can be flexibly reconfigured to enhance the desired signals and suppress multiuser interference. The proposed sum-rate maximization problem is highly non-convex and nonlinear due to the coupled solutions of element position, beamforming, and phase-shift. To address this challenge, an alternating optimization (AO) algorithm is developed to iteratively update these variables. The beamforming and FSIM phase-shift subproblems are transformed into semi-definite programming problems and solved by using successive convex approximation (SCA), first-order Taylor approximation, and penalty-based rank-one relaxation, whilst the FE position subproblem is handled through a projected gradient-based update. Simulation results reveal that a compact FSIM with a small inter-layer thickness is preferable, as increasing the thickness weakens inter-layer coupling and degrades the achievable sum rate. Results also demonstrate that the proposed FSIM significantly outperforms conventional SIMs with fixed positions, patch-based structures, partial fluidity, restricted fluid regions, and existing flexible intelligent metasurfaces. Furthermore, the proposed AO-based algorithm achieves superior rate performance compared to sub-schemes, metaheuristic methods, and conventional beamforming benchmarks.

</details>


### [119] [Energy-Efficient Resource Allocation for Six-Dimensional Movable Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15653)
*Ziyun Zhang,Ruotong Zhao,Shaokang Hu,Derrick Wing Kwan Ng*

TL;DR: 研究六维可移动天线(6DMA)多用户无线网络的能效最大化问题，提出BCD优化框架联合优化天线3D位置和朝向。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点：论文中"能效增益随机械能耗增加而递减"的trade-off思想，可类比思考星上模型轻量化中"精度提升与计算/能耗开销"之间的权衡关系。但整体技术路线无直接借鉴价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates the energy-efficiency (EE) maximization problem for a multiuser wireless network equipped with six-dimensional movable antennas (6DMAs), where the three-dimensional (3D) positions and orientations of the antennas are jointly optimized to fully exploit the additional spatial degrees of freedom offered by dynamic channel reconfiguration. However, the practical operation of 6DMAs incurs non-negligible mechanical energy consumption. Moreover, orientation-dependent phase variations, together with the strong coupling among antenna positions, rotation angles, transmit beamforming, and time allocation, render the resulting problem highly non-convex and analytically challenging. To address this issue, we develop a block coordinate descent (BCD) optimization framework that integrates Dinkelbach's transformation with the majorization-minimization (MM) approach to efficiently obtain a high quality suboptimal solution with guaranteed convergence. Simulation results unveil that the proposed design achieves significant EE improvements over conventional benchmarks, thereby highlighting the critical importance of accounting for practical mechanical energy costs in 6DMA enabled systems. Furthermore, our results reveal a fundamental trade-off between throughput enhancement and mechanical overhead: although larger antenna reconfigurations can improve channel conditions, their EE gains gradually diminish due to the increased mechanical energy consumption.

</details>

*Li-Hsiang Shen*

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化方法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于无线通信物理层优化问题，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: This paper investigates an energy-efficient active stacked intelligent metasurfaces (ASIM)-assisted downlink transmission framework, where a multi-antenna base station (BS) serves multiple users through a multi-layer metasurface architecture. Unlike conventional passive intelligent surfaces, the considered ASIM employs active amplification and multiple transmissive layers to enhance electromagnetic wave manipulation. We aim to maximize the system energy efficiency (EE) by jointly optimizing the BS beamforming and ASIM configurations under user quality-of-service and amplification constraints. The resulting problem is highly coupled and non-convex due to the cascaded near-field channel and multi-layer metasurface structure. To address this challenge, we first transform the original problem through epigraph, Lagrangian dual, and quadratic transformations. An alternative optimization framework is then developed, where the BS beamforming subproblem is solved via successive convex approximation (SCA), while the ASIM configuration is optimized using Bayesian optimization based on a Gaussian-process surrogate model. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed scheme significantly improves the achievable EE compared to conventional passive SIM and heuristic benchmark methods. Furthermore, the impacts of amplification capability, number of metasurface layers, and inter-layer spacing on system performance are investigated, providing useful design insights for future active metasurface-assisted wireless networks.

</details>


### [121] [Dual-Security for Indoor OFDM-ISAC Systems via Temporal Artificial Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15710)
*Yinchao Yang,Yathreb Bouazizi,Prabhat Raj Gautam,Michael Breza,Julie A. McCann*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究OFDM-ISAC系统中的双安全（dual-security）问题，通过时域人工噪声同时保护通信和感知功能安全，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文主题是ISAC（集成感知与通信）系统的安全机制，属于无线通信物理层安全领域。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、遥感场景理解；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何与模型压缩或部署相关的内容。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及深度学习模型或神经网络架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等内容。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴内容。该论文属于无线通信安全领域，研究范式、技术路线、评价体系均与目标检测模型轻量化完全不同。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题在问题定义、技术方法、应用场景上均无交集。

Abstract: With the rapid development of integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) as a key enabler for future wireless networks, ensuring the security of both communication and sensing functions has become increasingly important. Current secure ISAC studies focus restrictively either on the communication or the sensing security, but not both. To bridge this gap, this paper investigates security for both, i.e., dual-security, in indoor orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) based ISAC systems. Specifically, we consider a scenario in which a sensing user (SU) is authorised for sensing but may eavesdrop on communication data, while a communication user (CU) is authorised for communication but may perform unauthorised sensing. We chose this scenario as the pathological case where an authorised eavesdropper has more information and is more effective than an unauthorised one. To address this case, we propose the use of temporal artificial noise (AN) to prevent malicious CU sensing by enlarging its time-domain sensing error, and simultaneously degrade SU data eavesdropping by reducing its frequency-domain signal-to-noise-plus-interference ratio (SINR) with standard OFDM receiver processing. Meanwhile, our proposed scheme guarantees the sensing performance of the SU and the communication performance of the CU. We present numerical results that demonstrate AN can effectively provide dual protection for sensing and communication in OFDM-ISAC systems while guaranteeing the performance of legitimate users.

</details>


### [122] [Map as a Prompt: Learning Multi-Modal Spatial-Signal Foundation Models for Cross-scenario Wireless Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15713)
*Yong Chu,Xun Zhou,Zenglin Xu,Hui Wang,Yue Yu*

Main category: eess.SP

Lightweight method: 仅提到"lightweight soft prompts"用于跨场景适应，但这不是模型轻量化方法（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS等），而是提示调优技术。摘要未说明任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接参考的是"map-as-prompt"的提示调优思想，但场景差异过大（无线定位 vs 遥感目标检测），且不涉及轻量化核心问题。

Abstract: Accurate and robust wireless localization is a critical enabler for emerging 5G/6G applications, including autonomous driving, extended reality, and smart manufacturing. Despite its importance, achieving precise localization across diverse environments remains challenging due to the complex nature of wireless signals and their sensitivity to environmental changes. Existing data-driven approaches often suffer from limited generalization capability, requiring extensive labeled data and struggling to adapt to new scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose SigMap, a multimodal foundation model that introduces two key innovations: (1) A cycle-adaptive masking strategy that dynamically adjusts masking patterns based on channel periodicity characteristics to learn robust wireless representations; (2) A novel "map-as-prompt" framework that integrates 3D geographic information through lightweight soft prompts for effective cross-scenario adaptation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our model achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple localization tasks while exhibiting strong zero-shot generalization in unseen environments, significantly outperforming both supervised and self-supervised baselines by considerable margins.

</details>


### [123] [Energy-Efficient Target-Aware Hybrid Beamforming for THz Near-Field ISAC with Sparse Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15716)
*Nusaibah A. Alshorman,Chong Han,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用太赫兹ISAC系统中稀疏阵列栅瓣实现扩展目标单次全孔径感知的混合波束赋形架构，重点解决波束扫描效率与能耗问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦太赫兹通信感知一体化（ISAC）的波束赋形与阵列设计，属于无线通信/雷达感知领域，不涉及遥感卫星、光学/SAR/高光谱图像、目标检测模型或轻量化部署。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 稀疏连接混合波束赋形架构（sparse-connected hybrid beamforming architecture），包含开关控制的稀疏RF网络、数字预编码器、量化移相器、天线-RF开关矩阵。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。文中提到的"硬件与能耗节省"源于稀疏阵列架构本身，而非深度学习模型压缩。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真实验（非遥感数据集）；评价指标：Cramer-Rao感知精度、通信性能（低秩THz信道）、收敛速度、硬件与能耗节省。

Experiments: 140 GHz仿真实验，展示了接近全数字Cramer-Rao感知精度、竞争性通信性能、快速收敛及硬件能耗节省。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。摘要未说明任何与遥感图像、目标检测模型、模型压缩/加速相关的内容。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于通信/雷达信号处理领域，不涉及遥感图像目标检测或深度学习模型轻量化，不建议作为参考。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) at terahertz (THz) frequencies enables ultra-high-resolution perception while facing a key limitation: highly directional THz beams cannot illuminate extended targets within a single beam. Conventional solutions rely on sequential beam scanning, reducing sensing accuracy and increasing energy consumption. Moreover, in conventional sparse-array, grating lobes are generally treated as undesirable artifacts that should be suppressed to avoid ambiguity and interference. In contrast, this paper adopts a reverse design philosophy by intentionally engineering sparsity-induced grating lobes as controllable auxiliary illumination beams for extended-target sensing. This paper exploits grating lobes and proposes a sparse-connected hybrid beamforming architecture that intentionally engineers and exploits grating lobes to enable single-shot, full-aperture illumination of extended targets while supporting multi-user downlink communication. A switch-controlled sparse RF network preserves the array aperture and generates a dominant main lobe with structured secondary lobes covering the entire target extent. A covariance-driven alternating-minimization framework jointly optimizes digital precoders, quantized phase shifters, and antenna-RF switching. Simulations at 140 GHz demonstrate near fully-digital Cramer-Rao sensing accuracy, competitive communication performance in low-rank THz channels, rapid convergence, and significant hardware and energy savings, establishing structured sparse connectivity as a scalable and energy-efficient solution for extended-target THz ISAC.

</details>


### [124] [Multibit Quantized Precoding for MU-mMIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15959)
*Getuar Rexhepi,Shreesal Shrestha,Christoph Studer,Giuseppe Thadeu Freitas de Abreu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种面向多用户大规模MIMO下行链路的多比特量化预编码方法(MQP)，通过l0范数惩罚和分数规划实现有限字母约束下的预编码，并引入高斯置信传播降低计算复杂度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是无线通信领域的massive MIMO下行链路量化预编码问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: MQP方法基于l0范数惩罚（平滑代理函数近似）+ 分数规划凸化 + 离散步进非凸延续(graduated non-convexity continuation) + 高斯置信传播(GaBP)加速最小二乘更新。

Lightweight method: 文中提到的"降低计算复杂度"是通过GaBP将最小二乘更新变为线性时间复杂度，但这属于通信信号处理中的算法加速，与深度学习模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真系统（不同规模），误码率(error-rate performance)作为评价指标。未使用遥感或目标检测相关数据集。

Experiments: 在不同系统规模和信道条件下进行仿真，MQP（含/不含GaBP）与现有量化预编码算法对比，达到有竞争力或更优的误码率性能。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关。该论文面向通信物理层问题，不涉及任何视觉感知、遥感图像处理或深度学习模型压缩内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文讨论的是MIMO通信系统的量化预编码，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题域、方法体系和应用场景上均无交集。

Abstract: We propose a novel multibit quantized precoding method for the downlink of multi-user massive MIMO systems with low-resolution digital-to-analog converters. The new method, termed multibit quantized precoding (MQP), enforces the finite-alphabet constraint through an l0-norm penalty, approximated by a smooth surrogate so as to yield a reformulated problem, which is then convexized via fractional programming, ultimately extending quantized precoding beyond 1-bit alphabets. The regularization parameter of the proposed method is selected via a discrepancy principle integrated with graduated non-convexity continuation, resulting in a principled and reproducible hyperparameter tuning method and an efficient iterative algorithm with a closed-form, least-squares-type update per iteration. In order to further reduce the computational complexity of the method, we include a Gaussian belief propagation (GaBP) step for turning the least-squares update in linear-time. Simulations performed for systems with different sizes demonstrate that both methods, namely the MQP with and without GaBP, achieve competitive or superior error-rate performance compared to state-of-the-art quantized precoding algorithms under various channel conditions.

</details>


### [125] [Inertial Human Motion Capture: From Biomechanics to Recent Sensor Fusion Methods and Back](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16000)
*Manon Kok,Ive Weygers,Hassan Osman,Daniel Weber,Ruiyuan Li,Thomas Seel,Ajay Seth*

TL;DR: 一篇关于IMU人体运动捕捉的综述性教程，聚焦运动学估计的四个关键方面和三大挑战，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是IMU人体运动捕捉（human motion capture），属于生物力学/传感器融合领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：人体运动学估计（kinematics estimation）；场景：实验室外人体运动捕捉；传感器：IMU（惯性测量单元）；目标类别：人体关节角度。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提及传感器融合方法、运动学链约束等，但未给出网络结构细节。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务、场景、方法上均无交集。

Abstract: Inertial measurement units (IMUs) are a promising means to capture human motion, yet obtaining meaningful biomechanical quantities from IMU measurements remains non-trivial. This tutorial-style review focuses on kinematics and introduces four key aspects (inertial human motion capture objective, environmental conditions, subject & attributes, and motion characteristics) to determine how to translate biomechanical problems into adequate formulations for the fusion of inertial sensor measurements. We identify three fundamental challenges for kinematics estimation from IMUs: IMUs do not provide direct information about the joint angle, IMUs do not measure their own orientation, and real-world environments and dynamics compromise sensor reliability. Though there exist widely-used methods to overcome these challenges, they suffer from severe limitations in real-life applications, e.g., the need for sensor-to-segment calibration, and the fact that magnetic field disturbances degrade joint angle accuracy. The full potential for many use-cases hence remains untapped in terms of accuracy and reliability. We share insights into recently proposed methods, e.g. exploiting the human body's kinematic chain constraints, having the potential to overcome these limitations. We also present guiding questions related to the four key aspects and illustrate their use for navigating the methodological landscape for the use-case of lower-extremity joint angle estimation, for which we share open-access code and compare the traditional workflow with three alternatives. Our aim is to bridge the gap between the sensor fusion community developing methods for human motion capture and the biomechanics community in need of accurate, easy-to-use, and reliable methods to study human motion outside of the laboratory.

</details>


### [126] [A Kalman Filter-Assisted Data-Predictive SAR ADC With Reduced Switching Energy for Low-Power Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16139)
*Xiyuan Feng,Yuxiang Zhao,Jie Xiong,Dian Lin,Yunlei Zhong,Wei Liu,Zhongheng Ji,Ruiyu Tian,Chenhao Zhuo,Yue Yin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种卡尔曼滤波辅助的数据预测型SAR ADC，通过预测4个MSB来减少CDAC切换次数和量化周期，在180nm CMOS工艺下实现50.3%的功耗降低。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是模拟集成电路设计领域的ADC电路论文，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然"轻量化"在广义上可理解为低功耗设计，但本文讨论的是硬件电路层面的ADC能效优化，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型的轻量化（模型压缩、剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）属于完全不同的技术领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：超低功耗模数转换器(ADC)设计；应用场景：IoT设备、可穿戴健康监测器、无线传感器网络；传感器类型：SAR ADC；目标类别：不适用。与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: Kalman filter-assisted data-predictive SAR ADC架构，包含卡尔曼滤波器递归状态估计器、4位MSB并行切换方案、双模式操作（常规模式/Kalman预测模式）。采用180-nm CMOS工艺实现。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及模型轻量化方法。本文的"轻量化"指电路级功耗优化，通过Kalman滤波预测MSB减少CDAC切换能耗和量化周期，属于硬件电路设计层面的能效优化，与深度学习模型压缩无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关内容。本文讨论的是ADC芯片本身（采样率20 MS/s，1.8V供电，功耗0.975 mW），不涉及任何深度学习模型或目标检测算法的部署。

Datasets and metrics: 无数据集。评价指标：功耗（从1.96 mW降至0.975 mW，降低50.3%）、SNR 57.88 dB、SFDR 74.51 dB（@504 kHz）。

Experiments: 在180-nm CMOS工艺下流片验证，对比常规模式与Kalman预测模式：功耗降低50.3%，量化周期缩短4个时钟周期，切换能耗降低约50%。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法评估其在遥感目标检测轻量化方面的局限性。从电路角度看，180-nm工艺较老，且Kalman预测模式在输入信号剧烈变化时可能影响鲁棒性（论文通过双模式切换缓解）。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于模拟IC设计领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化（深度学习模型压缩与加速）无技术交集。唯一的间接启发是：Kalman滤波作为一种状态估计方法，在遥感目标跟踪任务中可能有应用，但本文讨论的是ADC量化层面的Kalman预测，与目标检测无关。

Abstract: The proliferation of Internet of Things (IoT) devices and wearable health monitors has created an urgent demand for ultra-low-power analog-to-digital converters (ADCs). Successive approximation register (SAR) ADCs are widely used in such applications, yet their energy efficiency remains constrained by the sequential bit-by-bit switching of the capacitive DAC (CDAC). The high-weight most significant bit (MSB) transitions dominate the total switching energy, and the rigid N -cycle conversion flow imposes a hard lower bound on latency per sample.This paper presents a Kalman filter-assisted data-predictive SAR ADC that replaces the first four comparator-driven decisions with a recursive state estimator. The Kalman filter predicts the 4 MSBs from the complete conversion history before each cycle begins, enabling simultaneous parallel switching of the MSB capacitors. This eliminates redundant CDAC transitions, shortens the quantization cycle by four clock periods, and reduces switching energy by approximately 50%. An optimized 4-bit MSB switching scheme further suppresses residual switching at the hardware level. The ADC, designed in a 180-nm CMOS process, supports configurable dual-mode operation, toggling between a conventional mode and the Kalman-driven predictive mode for robustness under erratic inputs. At 20 MS/s and a 1.8-V supply, the predictive mode reduces total power consumption by 50.3% (from 1.96 mW to 0.975 mW), with a measured SNR/SFDR of 57.88/74.51 dB at 504 kHz, confirming its suitability for energy-constrained wireless sensor networks.

</details>

### [127] [Structure of the Circular-Dyadic Convolution Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15293)
*Ben Fauber,Alireza Moradzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从数学上分析了用Hadamard变换替代FFT计算卷积时产生的代数误差，给出了误差抵消位置、误差算子秩和期望误差的闭式表达。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文是纯数学/信号处理理论分析，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化或任何部署相关技术。

Reading priority: 1分。纯数学理论分析，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向无直接关联，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。讨论的是Hadamard变换与FFT/DFT在卷积计算中的误差分析，不涉及任何深度学习模型结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。虽然Hadamard变换理论上比FFT计算更轻量（实数符号翻转），但论文并未将其作为模型压缩或加速方法讨论，而是纯误差分析。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。无实验数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。仅有理论推导，无实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 论文仅从理论角度分析误差特性，未讨论该误差分析如何应用于实际深度学习模型或遥感任务中，也未给出任何缓解误差的实用方案。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。如果课题涉及在星上使用Hadamard变换替代FFT加速卷积运算（例如在FPGA上实现轻量卷积），本文的误差分析可作为理论基础，但需要自行推导如何将该误差分析转化为实际模型设计或部署策略。直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Dyadic and circular convolution can both be computed in $O(N\log N)$ time using the Hadamard transform and the FFT-computed discrete Fourier transform (DFT), respectively. The Hadamard transform is preferable for its real-valued sign flips, yet its substitution for the DFT introduces algebraic error. We present three complementary results that characterize this error. First, we identify exact error cancellation: two input and two output positions are universally error-free, and no reordering of the output can eliminate this error. Second, the error operator is nearly full rank, while its null space has only logarithmic dimension. Third, the expected error is governed by a single alignment scalar, with a closed-form expression obtained by averaging over random filters. In general, the substitution error asymptotically doubles the output energy, except for filters in the universal zero-error subspace, which incur no error. Collectively, these results show that the substitution error is structured, predictable, and governed by alignment.

</details>


### [128] [Position: Quantum Program Generation Must Prioritize Validity Over Probabilistic Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15313)
*Junhao Song,Yu Zhou,William Knottenbelt,Yudong Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文主张将概率性缩放假设应用于量子电路合成是方向性错误，提出验证感知的量子程序生成架构。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是量子电路合成（quantum circuit synthesis）和量子程序生成，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。
