Model architecture: 摘要未说明传统视觉模型结构。论文提出验证感知架构（verifier-centric agents），包含层次化约束（hierarchical constraints）、拓扑掩码（topological masks）和符号代理（symbolic proxies），但这些是针对量子程序生成的设计，与遥感目标检测模型无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文为position paper（立场论文），未提及具体数据集或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文为观点论述性文章，未提供实验对比或消融实验结果。

Limitations: 论文本身是立场论述，未提供实验验证。与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文讨论的是量子计算领域的生成方法，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: The scaling hypothesis assumes that increasing model parameters yields emergent reasoning capabilities. This position paper argues that applying this probabilistic paradigm to generic quantum circuit synthesis is a directional error. Unlike natural languages, quantum circuits require strict adherence to mathematical constraints that manifest a significant syntax-semantics gap. Training on unverified quantum programs means that models learn syntax but fail to capture the physical semantics of the Hilbert space. Since the valid subset of circuit designs decays exponentially with the number of qubits, post-hoc filtering is mathematically intractable. We propose a pivot from human-centric copilots to verifier-centric agents. We integrate hierarchical constraints, topological masks, and symbolic proxies directly into generation. Our analysis suggests that scale alone cannot bridge the validity gap. Verification-aware architectures offer a viable path for modular quantum program generation. These considerations point toward generation methods that encode task-specific rules of quantum information, rather than relying on imitation alone.

</details>


### [129] [DebrisTracer: Reliable Tracking in Hypervelocity Impact Fast Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15986)
*Théophane Loloum,Fabien Vivodtzev,David Hébert,Baptiste Reynier,Michel Arrigoni,Julien Tierny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DebrisTracer框架，用于超高速撞击成像中碎片轨迹的可靠追踪，通过扩展基于关键点提取与匹配的拓扑追踪框架融入领域知识，实现碎片质量和速度分布的准确估计。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于超高速撞击碎片的轨迹追踪（debris tracking in hypervelocity impact imaging），属于航天材料/空间碎片物理分析领域，不涉及遥感图像、卫星在轨目标检测、模型轻量化或部署。虽然涉及"space"和"aerospace"，但核心任务是物理实验中的碎片追踪，而非遥感卫星对地观测目标检测。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：碎片轨迹追踪（debris tracking）；应用场景：超高速撞击实验成像分析；传感器类型：高速成像（fast imaging），非遥感卫星传感器；目标类别：撞击碎片（debris fragments）。与遥感目标检测（舰船/飞机/车辆等）无关。

Model architecture: 基于关键点提取与匹配的拓扑追踪框架（topology tracking framework based on critical point extraction and matching），扩展融入领域知识和物理假设。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时推理等部署相关信息。实现语言为C++。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：超高速撞击实验成像数据（hypervelocity impact fast imaging），具体数据集名称摘要未说明；评价指标：物理验证指标，包括实验抛射质量（ejected mass）和弹坑深度剖面（crater depth profiles）的预测精度。

Experiments: 在多个不同撞击角度和物理条件的用例上进行了实验，与领域专家使用的已有工具进行对比，在物理验证指标上展示了精度提升。消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 未讨论模型效率、实时性、轻量化等部署关键问题；3. 针对的是地面实验数据分析，而非星上在轨处理场景。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的间接启发是：该工作展示了如何将领域知识（domain knowledge）和物理假设融入追踪框架，类似思路可用于遥感目标检测中融入遥感物理先验（如地物光谱特性、几何约束等），但整体关联度非常弱。

Abstract: This application paper presents DebrisTracer, a framework for the reliable tracking of debris in hypervelocity impact fast imaging. These noisy and highly specific datasets capture the ejection of a large number of debris fragments after the impact of a projectile launched at hypervelocity into a target material. The reliable estimation of debris mass and speed distributions is of major importance in aerospace applications. We document how to extend an off-the-shelf topology tracking framework based on critical point extraction and matching, in order to incorporate domain knowledge and physical assumptions. Our approach automatically produces an accurate and reliable debris tracking, enabling an interpretable visual analysis of this complex space-time phenomenon. Extensive experiments demonstrate the accuracy improvements provided by our approach over established tools used by domain experts in terms of physical validation, specifically via the prediction of the experimental ejected mass and crater depth profiles. We illustrate the utility of our approach across several use cases (with varying impact angles and physics). We show that our statistical summaries enable the visual identification of distinct regimes within the debris population, corroborating and refining prior expectations of domain experts. Our database and our C++ implementation are available at this address: https://github.com/tloloum/DebrisTracer.

</details>


### [130] [A Transportable Threshold-Based Framework for Interpretable Classification of Medical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15394)
*Antony Garcia,Adrian Noriega,Gabrielle Britton,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于伯努利朴素贝叶斯的可解释临床分类框架，通过χ²统计二值化处理连续变量，在三个医疗数据集上取得有竞争力的AUC分数。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦医疗临床分类的可解释性，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：临床分类（疾病诊断）；应用场景：医疗健康；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：糖尿病、乳腺癌、心力衰竭预测。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: Bernoulli Naïve Bayes (BNB) 模型，结合χ²统计引导的统计二值化方法将连续变量转化为二值特征。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。模型本身是朴素贝叶斯，计算开销低，但论文并未从轻量化角度讨论。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然BNB模型本身轻量，但论文未讨论星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Pima Indians Diabetes、Wisconsin Breast Cancer、Heart Failure Prediction（三个医疗公开数据集）。评价指标：AUC、Brier score、校准截距/斜率、后验beta校准。

Experiments: Pima数据集AUC 0.800，Wisconsin Breast Cancer AUC 0.984，Heart Failure Prediction AUC 0.919。进行了概率校准分析（Brier score、校准曲线等）。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 不涉及模型轻量化或部署优化；4. 仅在三个小规模医疗数据集上验证，未在遥感数据上测试。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的启发是：如果未来在遥感在轨场景中需要可解释性决策（如星上紧急判断），可参考其统计二值化+朴素贝叶斯的思路，但与本课题核心方向（遥感目标检测+轻量化）基本无关。

Abstract: Black-box models limit the adoption of artificial intelligence in medicine due to their lack of interpretability and reproducibility. We introduce a statistically grounded framework that provides fully interpretable, rule-based clinical classification using the Bernoulli Naïve Bayes (BNB) model. The method applies supervised $χ^2$-guided statistical binarization to continuous variables, identifying thresholds that maximize association with clinical outcomes within the training data. This transformation allows BNB to operate effectively on continuous medical data without sacrificing its inherent transparency. The approach was evaluated on three benchmark datasets, Pima Indians Diabetes, Wisconsin Breast Cancer, and Heart Failure Prediction, achieving area-under-the-curve (AUC) scores of 0.800 for the Pima analysis, 0.984 for Wisconsin Breast Cancer, and 0.919 for Heart Failure Prediction. In addition to discrimination, probabilistic reliability was assessed using leakage-safe cross-validated calibration analysis including Brier score, calibration intercept/slope, and post-hoc beta calibration, which improved probability calibration across datasets. These results suggest that a statistically interpretable framework can achieve performance comparable to more complex models while providing explicit, clinically meaningful decision rules and calibrated risk estimates. To illustrate this transparency concretely, a complete worked example demonstrates that model inference can be reproduced using only a reference table and basic arithmetic, without access to software or proprietary tools. This work offers a practical approach to supporting trustworthy and generalizable AI in real-world healthcare settings.

</details>


### [131] [Physics-Based Deep Spatiotemporal Hyperlocal Radar Nowcasting with a Multi-Variable U-Net for High-Resolution Precipitation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16080)
*Akshay Sunil,Muhammed Rashid,Raja Sekhar Sivaraju,Sushma Nair,Subimal Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种紧凑型雷达-only降水临近预报框架，基于U-Net编码器-解码器结构，融合多仰角反射率、多普勒径向速度和径向速度梯度代理特征，用于孟买地区未来90分钟内的降水预报。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于气象降水临近预报（precipitation nowcasting），使用地基多普勒雷达数据，不涉及遥感卫星、光学/SAR/高光谱遥感图像、空间在轨智能或目标检测任务。虽然使用了U-Net和轻量级设计，但应用场景、传感器类型和任务目标与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议作为核心参考文献。仅当需要了解气象领域的紧凑型U-Net设计或注意力机制时，可作为泛化参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：降水临近预报（precipitation nowcasting）；应用场景：城市洪水管理、实时决策（孟买地区）；传感器类型：地基多普勒雷达（Doppler radar）；目标类别：降水回波（composite reflectivity），无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 编码器-解码器U-Net结构；输入为多仰角反射率、多普勒径向速度及径向速度梯度代理特征（速度幅值、散度类、方向切变类、涡度类通道）；高反射率注意力模块（high-reflectivity attention module）增强对流核心敏感性。

Lightweight method: 摘要提及"compact radar-only nowcasting framework"，但未具体说明轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。模型设计紧凑，但轻量化技术细节未给出。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及"runs on a standard computer, generating nowcasts within seconds for real-time use"，表明推理速度较快，但未量化。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：2023年5月至8月孟买多普勒雷达观测数据；评价指标：Critical Success Index (CSI) 在≥10、≥20、≥30 dBZ阈值下分别为0.437、0.332、0.193（90分钟预报）；RMSE和空间相关性（与persistence基线对比）。

Experiments: 在时间独立事件上评估；与persistence方法对比，在较长预报时间下RMSE更低、空间相关性更高；高反射率注意力模块的消融效果摘要未详细说明；物理引导归因分析验证了模型学习到的敏感性的气象意义。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 轻量化方法不具体，无参数量/FLOPs等量化指标；4. 仅针对孟买地区单一雷达站点，泛化性未知；5. 无星上部署相关讨论。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1) 高反射率注意力模块的设计思路可类比遥感目标检测中的注意力机制；2) 紧凑型U-Net编码器-解码器结构可用于遥感图像分割或检测的轻量骨干网络设计；3) 物理引导归因分析思路可用于遥感模型的可解释性验证。但整体与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化方向弱相关。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting over the immediate 10-90 min period is important for flood management and real-time decision-making in urban regions. Conventional short-range forecasting with high-resolution numerical weather prediction requires frequent data assimilation, model initialization, and spin-up, introducing computational latency. Machine learning provides an alternative by learning storm evolution directly from high-frequency observations and producing forecasts quickly after training. This is particularly relevant for Mumbai, India, where monsoon convection, land-sea interactions, and localized intense rainfall make short-term prediction difficult. Here, we develop a compact radar-only nowcasting framework that combines multi-elevation reflectivity, Doppler radial velocity, and radial-velocity-gradient proxy features within an encoder-decoder U-Net. Using the most recent radar volume scan, the model predicts 12 future composite reflectivity fields at 7.5-min intervals up to 90 min lead time. The derived velocity magnitude, divergence-like, directional-shear, and vorticity-like channels represent kinematic signatures associated with convergence and boundary interactions without requiring full wind-field retrieval. A high-reflectivity attention module improves sensitivity to convective cores, and physics-guided attribution examines whether the learned sensitivities are meteorologically meaningful. The model is trained using Mumbai Doppler radar observations from May to August 2023 and evaluated on temporally independent events. At 90 min lead time, Critical Success Index values are 0.437, 0.332, and 0.193 for $\geq$10, $\geq$20, and $\geq$30 dBZ thresholds, respectively. Compared with persistence, the model gives lower RMSE and higher spatial correlation at longer lead times. Once trained, it runs on a standard computer, generating nowcasts within seconds for real-time use.

</details>


### [132] [Regularity-Aware Stochastic MGDA with Adaptive Conflict-Avoidant Update Direction Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15412)
*Chentong Huang,Lisha Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种随机多目标正则感知方法（MoRe），通过利用冲突避免方向的Lipschitz连续性改进随机多梯度下降的收敛速率，从$\widetilde{\mathcal O}(T^{-1/4})$提升至$\widetilde{\mathcal O}(T^{-1/2})$。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是纯机器学习理论工作，聚焦多目标优化的收敛性分析，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化任何具体应用场景。

Reading priority: 1分。纯优化理论论文，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向无直接关联，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文为纯优化理论，未涉及任何具体任务、场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何神经网络模型结构设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未提及任何具体数据集名称或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要仅提及"实验证明了在多任务性能上的有效性并验证了收敛行为与理论速率一致"，但未给出任何具体数值结果、对比方法或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 纯理论工作，缺乏实际应用验证；2. 未说明在深度学习大规模模型上的实际加速效果；3. 未讨论与遥感/目标检测任务的结合方式；4. 收敛性分析基于非凸假设，但未验证在具体视觉任务上的表现。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但可间接借鉴：1. 多目标优化（如检测精度 vs 模型大小 vs 推理速度）在轻量化场景中常见，MoRe方法中根据梯度冲突程度自适应切换更新策略的思路可用于多目标NAS或剪枝中的多目标搜索策略设计；2. 但该工作停留在理论层面，直接迁移到遥感目标检测轻量化需要大量适配工作，优先级低。

Abstract: Multi-objective learning (MOL) aims to optimize multiple objectives simultaneously. The multi-gradient descent algorithm (MGDA) is a workhorse that iteratively updates along a common descent or conflict-avoidant (CA) direction across objectives. In stochastic settings, however, the vanilla stochastic MGDA method, SMG, lacks a fast convergence rate because mini-batch sampling introduces noise in the gradients. This causes bias in the update direction, which is controlled by the CA direction continuity. In this paper, we show that the CA direction is $1/2$-Holder continuous with respect to the Jacobian matrix, and the exponent $1/2$ cannot be improved in the worst case. This leads to a suboptimal convergence rate for vanilla stochastic MGDA in prior works. Nevertheless, under additional regularity conditions, we show this can be improved to Lipschitz continuity. Based on this insight, we propose a stochastic multi-objective regularity-aware (MoRe) method that exploits the Lipschitz continuity of the CA direction when the subproblem is regular, and switches to a fixed scalarization weight otherwise. Intuitively, the proposed algorithm employs CA direction update when the gradient conflict is large, and linear scalarization update otherwise. Theoretically, our method improves the convergence rate of SMG in the nonconvex setting from $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(T^{-1/4})$ to $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(T^{-1/2})$, where $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\cdot)$ hides logarithmic factors. Meanwhile, we also establish the per-iterate conflict-avoidance guarantees. Empirically, experiments demonstrate its effectiveness in multi-task performance and verify convergence behavior consistent with the established theoretical rate.

</details>


### [133] [AI Trading: Evaluating Large Language Models for Technical Market Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15414)
*Geofrey Ntale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种大语言模型（GPT-4 Turbo、Claude 3 Opus、Gemini 1.5 Pro、Llama 3 70B、FinGPT）在金融市场技术分析中的表现，包括K线模式识别、交易信号生成、回测和财报理解。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦金融市场的LLM技术分析，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：金融技术分析（candlestick pattern recognition, directional signal generation, backtesting, financial report comprehension）；应用场景：金融市场；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: GPT-4 Turbo, Claude 3 Opus, Gemini 1.5 Pro, Llama 3 70B, FinGPT（均为通用或金融领域大语言模型，非视觉检测模型）

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。且金融场景与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：OHLCV金融数据、财报文本；评价指标：Sharpe ratio, maximum drawdown, Sortino ratio, information coefficient, F1-score, BLEU score

Experiments: 模拟回测实验，GPT-4 Turbo在通用模型中表现最佳，FinGPT因领域微调在风险调整收益上有竞争力，两者均跑赢S&P 500基准。所有模型存在数值幻觉、上下文窗口限制、横盘市场表现不一致等问题。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术方法或实验设计。摘要未涉及视觉检测、模型压缩、边缘部署等核心内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于金融NLP领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何技术交集。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for processing the heterogeneous information environments of modern financial markets. This paper presents a systematic, comparative evaluation of five prominent LLMs: GPT-4 Turbo, Claude 3 Opus, Gemini 1.5 Pro, Llama 3 70B, and the domain-specialized FinGPT, with respect to their capacity for technical market analysis. The evaluation spans four structured tasks: candlestick pattern recognition from OHLCV data, directional signal generation (BUY/SELL/HOLD), backtesting of signal quality through a simulated execution pipeline, and financial report comprehension. Our experimental framework employs rigorous quantitative metrics, including Sharpe ratio, maximum drawdown, Sortino ratio, information coefficient, F1-score, and BLEU score. Findings from simulated backtesting indicate that GPT-4 Turbo achieves the highest annualized return and Sharpe ratio among general-purpose models, while FinGPT demonstrates competitive risk-adjusted performance due to domain-specific fine-tuning. Both models outperform a passive S&P 500 benchmark under the tested conditions. The study identifies persistent failure modes across all evaluated models, including numerical hallucination, context-window limitations, and inconsistent performance in sideways market regimes. We conclude that while LLMs hold genuine promise within AI trading systems, robust deployment requires careful task decomposition, rigorous backtesting protocols, and domain-aware fine-tuning strategies.

</details>


### [134] [Diffusion models recover accurate mixture weights despite score function insensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15485)
*Andrew Dennehy,Ramchandran Muthukumar,Rebecca Willett,Nisha Chandramoorthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散模型（score-based generative models）在多模态分布中混合权重（mixture weights）恢复的精度问题，提出扩散分数敏感性指数（DSSI）来解释模式放大现象。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是生成式扩散模型的理论行为（混合权重估计与模式放大），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：生成式建模的理论分析（混合权重估计）；应用场景：无；传感器类型：无；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: Score-based generative models（基于分数的生成模型），扩散模型（diffusion models），具体架构细节摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等均未提及）。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：benchmark data distributions（基准数据分布），具体名称摘要未说明；评价指标：DSM loss（扩散分数匹配损失）、DSSI（扩散分数敏感性指数）、mixture weight estimation errors（混合权重估计误差）。

Experiments: 理论证明：对任意维度的高斯混合模型，证明了混合权重估计误差与DSM损失同阶；实验验证：在基准数据分布上展示了噪声过程中敏感性的涌现，以及敏感性值可预测模型恢复混合权重的能力；还展示了噪声调度选择可降低扩散敏感性从而导致模式放大。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 仅关注生成模型的混合权重恢复问题，不涉及判别式检测任务；3. 未讨论任何实际部署场景。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于生成式模型的理论分析，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化（判别式任务+模型压缩加速）的研究方向几乎没有交集。

Abstract: Score-based generative models exhibit a puzzling behavior: they often appear to cover all modes of a target multimodal distribution and yet may fail to learn the correct relative mode amplitudes, which can be interpreted as mixture weights. We resolve this apparent paradox by relating the diffusion score matching (DSM) loss to the error in estimating mixture weights from generated samples. We show that, even when the target score is insensitive to mixture weights, generated samples can recover the weights accurately if the scores at intermediate noise levels are informative about the weights. Accordingly, we define the diffusion score sensitivity index (DSSI) as the variation in the DSM loss relative to changes in a parameter. We then show that the DSSI governs the accuracy with which the parameter of the target distribution can be estimated from generated samples. For Gaussian mixtures in arbitrary dimensions, we prove that the mixture weight estimation errors are on the same order as the DSM loss under mild conditions. Empirically, we show the emergence of sensitivity during the noising process of benchmark data distributions under typical noise schedules, and that these sensitivity values predict how well a well-trained model recovers mixture weights. Furthermore, we show that the choice of noise schedule can reduce diffusion sensitivity, leading to mode amplification. Although we focus on mixture weights, the proposed sensitivity framework governs the recovery of any qualitative parameter of the target distribution.

</details>


### [135] [qZACH-ViT: Quantization-Aware Intrinsic Explanations with Recursive Attribution-Stabilized Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15421)
*Athanasios Angelakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种量化感知的紧凑型可解释ViT模型qZACH-ViT，结合混合精度INT8量化和递归属性稳定优化RASO，在医学图像分类上实现模型压缩与可解释性的统一。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦医学图像分类（MedMNIST数据集），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景；不涉及目标检测（仅做分类）；虽涉及量化（INT8）和模型压缩，但应用场景为医学影像而非遥感在轨部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于医学图像分类+可解释性+量化的交叉工作。仅INT8量化和ONNX部署流程有微弱借鉴意义，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像分类（非目标检测）；应用场景：医学影像分析；传感器类型：摘要未说明（MedMNIST包含多种医学图像模态）；目标类别：医学疾病类别。

Model architecture: ZACH-ViT：零token（无CLS token）、无位置编码的ViT变体，具有递归内在补丁级类别证据；qZACH-ViT：量化感知扩展版本。

Lightweight method: 混合精度INT8量化（ONNX INT8图，包含16个有符号INT8 MatMulInteger投影+INT32累加）；模型压缩（ONNX产物比原始checkpoint小70%）；CPU端加速（1线程1.41×，4线程2.39×端到端加速）。

Onboard deployability: 参数量：摘要未说明；FLOPs：摘要未说明；延迟：CPU端1线程和4线程端到端加速比1.41×和2.39×；显存：摘要未说明；能耗：摘要未说明；实时性：摘要未说明；部署格式：ONNX INT8图；部署平台：CPU（非FPGA/NPU/TensorRT/星上部署）。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：7个MedMNIST数据集（医学图像），每类50张训练图像，10个固定种子，共280次运行；评价指标：数据集特定的主要指标（primary metric），预测一致性（99.9751%），指标变化绝对值（均值0.000133，最大0.004386），内在图余弦相似度（0.999955），秩相关（0.9944），top-10%重叠（0.9692），充分性误差，输入噪声稳定性。

Experiments: 210个qZACH-ViT checkpoint全部转为混合精度INT8 ONNX；qZACH-ViT+Adam在所有7个数据集上优于FP32 ZACH-ViT基线，平均增益0.0313；qZACH-ViT+RASO平均增益0.0368；964,920次源到INT8测试比较中预测一致性99.9751%；RASO显著降低充分性误差并改善输入噪声稳定性。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对医学图像分类，非遥感目标检测场景；2. 仅做INT8量化，未涉及剪枝、蒸馏、NAS等其他轻量化方法；3. 仅部署在CPU，未涉及GPU/FPGA/NPU/星上硬件；4. RASO在部分预测或XAI指标上并不全面占优；5. 未报告参数量、FLOPs等关键效率指标。

Ideas for my research: 1. 量化感知训练（QAT）策略可借鉴：将INT8量化与模型训练联合优化，在遥感目标检测模型中尝试混合精度量化；2. ONNX INT8部署流程可参考：将遥感检测模型转为ONNX INT8格式以提升推理速度；3. 属性稳定优化（RASO）的思想可迁移：在遥感检测中同时优化分类/回归损失与特征归因一致性，提升模型可解释性；4. 零token设计（无CLS token）思路可用于轻量遥感ViT设计，减少冗余计算。

Abstract: Compact medical-image classifiers need efficiency and interpretable evidence, yet these goals are often addressed separately. We introduce qZACH-ViT, a quantization-aware extension of the zero-token (CLS-token-free), position-free ZACH-ViT backbone with recursive intrinsic patch-level class evidence. We also introduce Recursive Attribution-Stabilized Optimization (RASO), which norm-matches classification and attribution gradients and removes attribution components that conflict with classification. We evaluate four controlled conditions on seven MedMNIST datasets using 50 training images per class and ten fixed seeds, completing 280 runs. All 210 qZACH-ViT checkpoints are converted to executable mixed-precision ONNX INT8 graphs containing 16 signed INT8 MatMulInteger projections with INT32 accumulation. Deployed mixed-precision INT8 qZACH-ViT with Adam improves the FP32 ZACH-ViT baseline mean on all seven datasets, with a mean paired gain of 0.0313 in the dataset-specific primary metric; qZACH-ViT with RASO yields a mean gain of 0.0368. Across 964,920 source-to-INT8 test comparisons, prediction agreement is 99.9751\%, with a mean absolute primary-metric change of 0.000133 and a maximum of 0.004386. Across 3,600 matched intrinsic maps, mean cosine similarity is 0.999955, mean rank correlation is 0.9944, and mean top-10\% overlap is 0.9692. ONNX artifacts are 70.0\% smaller than source checkpoints and provide $1.41\times$ and $2.39\times$ end-to-end CPU speedups with one and four threads. RASO significantly reduces sufficiency error and improves input-noise stability over Adam with the same attribution loss, but does not dominate every predictive or explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) metric. These results establish qZACH-ViT as a deployable compact intrinsically explainable model and RASO as a targeted stability-oriented optimization procedure.

</details>


### [136] [Do Generative Models Keep Time? A Time-Aware Evaluation of Synthetic Sequential Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15606)
*Kiwan Kwon,Kangmin Kim,Hojin Lee,Yeseong Jung,Hyeongwoo Kong,Vamsi K. Potluru,Saerom Park,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向合成时序表格数据的时域保真度评估协议，通过数据属性分类指导评估维度选择，并发现传统静态评估与时序评估结果存在显著分歧。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文聚焦于合成时序表格数据的评估方法，属于数据质量/隐私保护领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。任务为合成时序表格数据的时域保真度评估，应用场景为隐私保护数据共享，不涉及传感器类型或目标类别。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何部署相关信息（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等）。

Datasets and metrics: 使用了13个数据集（跨6个领域），评估了8种生成模型。评价指标包括：timestamp validity（时间戳有效性）、cross-sectional structure（横截面结构）、within-entity dynamics（实体内部动态）、time-varying relational structure（时变关系结构），以及轨迹级别的效用和隐私评估。

Experiments: 实验发现：传统静态评估与时序评估下的模型排名存在显著分歧；生成模型的时序失败模式具有架构一致性而非随机性。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接或间接的借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于时序数据生成与评估领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化研究方向无任何交集。

Abstract: Synthetic sequential tabular data are increasingly used for privacy-preserving data sharing, yet a generator can reproduce every marginal and every foreign-key relationship while emitting timestamps that run backwards or repeat, and while sending entities along paths that no real entity followed. Conventional tabular evaluation, which pools records into static distributions, is blind to such failures. We present a taxonomy-guided evaluation protocol for temporal fidelity, in which the applicable measurements are determined by the data rather than fixed in advance. Each dataset is first characterized along four properties: how time is represented, whether observations are regularly sampled, whether trajectories are mutually dependent, and how the schema links entities to their histories. These properties determine which evaluation dimensions are meaningful. The protocol then measures timestamp validity, cross-sectional structure at aligned time points, within-entity dynamics, and time-varying relational structure, and recasts utility and privacy evaluation over trajectories rather than isolated rows. We apply the protocol to eight generative models across thirteen datasets spanning six domains. Rankings under conventional evaluation disagree substantially with those obtained under temporal evaluation, and the resulting failures are architecture-coherent rather than random. Temporal fidelity must therefore be measured on the time axis itself, rather than inferred from pooled record distributions.

</details>


### [137] [From hyperplanes to hyperellipsoids: characterizing the inherent interpretability of linear and single-qubit mixed-state binary classification models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15433)
*Kaitlin Gili*

TL;DR: 本文比较了标准线性模型与单量子比特混合态模型在二分类任务中的可解释性，指出后者本质上是前者的"椭球版本"，旨在为无量子背景的读者提供一条理解量子机器学习的教学路径。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Abstract: We characterize and compare the inherent interpretability offerings of a standard linear model with a single qubit mixed state model for the task of supervised binary classification. A side by side comparison reveals that a single qubit mixed state model for binary classification is just the ``ellipsoid version" of standard linear model classification. More precisely, rather than learning a hyperplane to classify data, we learn a hyperellipsoid. We discuss the consequences of the geometric inductive biases of both models, as well as how each model contains a different feature importance inductive bias. This short characterization offers an accessible route to quantum machine learning (ML) ideas for readers who have zero background in quantum and are only familiar with linear classification in ML. In support of ML pedagogy, we encourage instructors to utilize this piece to smoothly introduce quantum ML ideas into the undergraduate ML classroom.

</details>


### [138] [ASK-NN: An Asymmetric Nearest-Neighbor Test that detects Distribution Drifts in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15607)

Abstract: Hallucinations and artificial text in LLM-generated outputs often appear as distributional deviations between prompt and response hidden-state distributions. Since prompts or retrieved contexts typically serve as reference samples and responses as query samples, with major differences in length, these asymmetries motivate the use of change test statistics that treat the two samples differently. We consider an asymmetric two-sample test ASK-NN based on the directed k-nearest-neighbor graph. Our statistic counts reference points whose nearest neighbor in the pooled sample is also a reference point. Under the permutation null, it admits an exact finite-sample conditional mean and variance; we further establish asymptotic normality and consistency under fixed alternatives. ASK-NN is computationally effective and easy to implement. Empirically, it is competitive with kernel and graph-based baselines on synthetic benchmarks, artificial-text detection, and LLM hallucination detection from token-level hidden states.

</details>


### [139] [Stochastic Reset Pathfinding: Path-Level Regret for Cascading Bandits over Graph Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15440)
*Guni Sharon,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机重置寻路问题（SRP），将其建模为组合级联赌博机（CCB）框架，并设计了PathUCB和PathTS两种算法，应用于量子中继网络、闪电网络支付路由等场景。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是图上的寻路学习问题（Stochastic Reset Pathfinding），属于组合赌博机和强化学习范畴，应用场景是量子网络、支付路由和不可靠网格网络，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感相关领域，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：随机重置寻路（Stochastic Reset Pathfinding, SRP），属于组合级联赌博机（CCB）框架下的序贯决策问题。应用场景：量子中继网络中的纠缠分发、闪电网络支付路由、不可靠网格网络中的投递。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 提出Log-Dijkstra元算法框架，包含两种实例化：PathUCB（基于UCB）和PathTS（基于Thompson Sampling）。核心是路径级别的遗憾分析，通过每条路径的复杂度C(π)（结合每条边的前缀和后缀可靠性）来分解遗憾。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等均未提及）。

Datasets and metrics: 实验域：量子网络（quantum-network）、分层DAG（layered-DAG）、网格世界（grid-world）、Erdos-Renyi图。评价指标：累积遗憾（regret），以及算法收敛性。不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 在量子网络、分层DAG、网格世界和Erdos-Renyi图上进行了实验，支持理论分析。PathTS通常取得最佳经验性能。但存在对抗性实例使PathTS无法收敛，与组合Thompson Sampling在乘法奖励问题上的已知指数级障碍一致。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. PathTS存在对抗性实例不收敛的问题；3. 仅适用于已知有向图、未知边成功概率的设定，泛化性有限。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。其路径级遗憾分解方法（per-path complexity C(π)）在理论上对组合优化问题有参考价值，但与遥感目标检测的模型压缩/加速/部署问题无关。

Abstract: We introduce Stochastic Reset Pathfinding (SRP), an episodic learning problem on a known directed graph with unknown stationary edge success probabilities. In each episode, the agent commits to a source-to-goal path, and any edge failure during execution resets it to the source. SRP captures settings such as entanglement distribution in quantum repeater networks, payment routing on the Lightning Network, and delivery in unreliable mesh networks. We show that the global-reset structure makes the optimal policy open-loop, placing SRP within the combinatorial cascading bandit (CCB) framework. We propose a Log-Dijkstra meta-algorithm with UCB (PathUCB) and Thompson Sampling (PathTS) instantiations. Our main technical result is a path-level regret bound for PathUCB that decomposes regret over suboptimal paths via a per-path complexity C(pi) combining each edge's prefix and suffix reliability. The bound is complementary to the edge-level CCB bound and more informative on structured graphs with polynomially many source-to-goal paths. Experiments on quantum-network, layered-DAG, grid-world, and Erdos-Renyi domains support the theory and show that PathTS typically achieves the best empirical performance among the algorithms tested. We then exhibit an adversarial instance on which PathTS fails to converge, consistent with a known exponential obstruction for combinatorial Thompson Sampling on multiplicative-reward problems. We recommend PathTS as the practical default while cautioning that adversarial instances exist.

</details>


### [140] [Who Became Financially Vulnerable After COVID-19? A Population-Level Machine Learning Analysis Using MEPS Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15446)
*Alexey Kresin,Zien Cheng,Ammar Ahad,Ebiyomare Kelvin,Manish Sivaratri,Prabhjeet Singh,Omar Aljawfi,Olabisi Ojo,Nawar Shara*

Main category: cs.LG

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关领域；也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等视觉检测任务；更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等模型压缩或部署技术。论文属于医疗健康领域的统计建模与机器学习应用研究。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。实际任务为医疗财务脆弱性预测，应用场景为美国人口健康调查，数据为MEPS调查数据，目标为预测高医疗财务负担（家庭自付医疗支出超过收入10%）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明遥感模型架构。实际使用了逻辑回归（logistic regression）、随机森林（random forest）、梯度提升模型（gradient boosting）进行预测。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明遥感数据集。实际使用Medical Expenditure Panel Survey (MEPS) 2019和2021年数据，评价指标为调整后的比值比（adjusted odds ratios）和预测性能（predictive performance）。

Experiments: 摘要未说明遥感相关实验。实际实验包括：调查加权亚组分析、逻辑回归估计调整比值比、随机森林和梯度提升评估预测性能、时间泛化测试（疫情前模型对疫情后数据的预测能力）。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无任何直接或间接的可借鉴点。论文属于医疗健康领域，不涉及计算机视觉、遥感图像处理、模型压缩或边缘部署等任何相关技术。

Abstract: The cost of healthcare remains a concern in the United States and may have been influenced by disruptions associated with the COVID-19 pandemic. This study examines healthcare financial vulnerability before and after the pandemic using Medical Expenditure Panel Survey (MEPS) data from 2019 and 2021. High financial burden was defined as out-of-pocket healthcare expenditures exceeding 10% of family income. Survey-weighted subgroup analyses were performed to obtain nationally representative estimates across demographic and socioeconomic groups.
  Descriptive analyses were complemented by interpretable logistic regression and machine learning models. Logistic regression was used to estimate adjusted odds ratios, while random forest and gradient boosting models were used to evaluate predictive performance. Temporal generalization assessed whether models trained on pre-pandemic data remained predictive when applied to post-pandemic observations.
  Financial vulnerability was strongly associated with poverty status, insurance coverage, and prescription drug spending. Subgroup analyses indicated persistent disparities across population groups, with some evidence of increased burden among vulnerable populations in 2021. Despite these differences, models trained on pre-pandemic data exhibited only modest reductions in predictive performance when evaluated on post-pandemic data, suggesting that the principal predictors of healthcare financial vulnerability remained relatively stable over time.
  These findings provide a population-level assessment of healthcare financial vulnerability during the COVID-19 period and demonstrate the value of combining interpretable statistical modeling with machine learning for population health research. The results may support future population health surveillance, risk stratification, and healthcare policy research aimed at reducing financial barriers to care.

</details>


### [141] [LLM4EHR: Aligning Clinical Time Series with Medical Event Sequences via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15447)
*Jingteng Li,Alexander Capstick,Louise Rigny,Iona Biggart,Neil J Sebire,Payam Barnaghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM4EHR，一种基于ICU电子健康记录(EHR)的临床基础模型，通过对比学习对齐临床事件与时间序列的时序结构，用于下游临床预测任务。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于临床机器学习/ICU预测，使用EHR数据，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：临床基础模型预训练与下游预测；应用场景：ICU重症监护；传感器/数据类型：电子健康记录(EHR)中的临床事件和时间序列；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 结合领域自适应大语言模型(domain adapted LLM)与Transformer时间序列编码器(transformer TS encoder)，通过正则化对比学习目标(regularised contrastive objective)对齐EHR事件与时间序列。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用ICU EHR数据；下游任务性能指标；摘要未说明具体数据集名称和指标细节。

Experiments: 通过消融实验(ablation study)验证LLM4EHR学习到的EHR时间序列嵌入在下游临床任务上表现有竞争力；通过k-shot自适应实验证明嵌入可迁移到新队列。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感、检测或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能间接参考的是对比学习(contrastive learning)用于多模态对齐的思路，但该论文面向临床EHR数据，与遥感图像/卫星数据差异极大，且不涉及目标检测和轻量化。

Abstract: Recent research in clinical machine learning, focusing on outcome predictions in intensive care unit (ICU), has shifted from bespoke supervised models to foundation models, utilising modern representation learning methods. Here, foundation models are pre-trained on mixtures of complex clinical data modalities, useful for various downstream tasks. Existing works often utilise Electronic Health Records (EHR) to provide rich and diverse patient observations to train clinical foundation models. However, existing methods do not sufficiently explore the shared temporal structures between clinical events and time series (TS) observations recorded in EHRs. This limitation potentially leads to less robust and adaptive clinical foundation models, resulting in reduced performance on downstream tasks. To fully exploit this temporal structure, we propose LLM4EHR, a new clinical foundation model trained on ICU EHR data. Combining domain adapted large language models with a transformer TS encoder, we pre-trained LLM4EHR by temporally aligning the EHR events and TS. For this, we propose a regularised contrastive objective to learn robust EHR TS representations conditioned on EHR event embeddings produced by the domain adapted LLM. Supported by an ablation study, we find that learnt EHR TS embeddings from LLM4EHR improve performance on various downstream clinical tasks with competitive performance. Further, we empirically demonstrate that LLM4EHR learns transferable clinical TS embeddings that can be deployed to new cohorts via k-shot adaptation. These findings provide a step towards building more generalisable and performant clinical foundation models.

</details>


### [142] [Relevant and Irrelevant: A Renormalization Group Analysis of Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15449)
*Parviz Haggi-Mani,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从Wilson重整化群理论视角，将Transformer注意力机制视为对MLP残差堆栈不动点的微扰，分析注意力在不同数据相关性下的相关性强弱，发现注意力的相关性取决于数据生成过程的谱结构。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文是纯理论性的深度学习机制分析，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测任务，也不涉及任何轻量化或部署方法。虽然Transformer架构在遥感目标检测中常用，但本文完全不讨论遥感场景或检测任务。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对Transformer理论分析有特殊兴趣，否则可跳过。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：Transformer注意力机制的物理理论分析（重整化群视角）。应用场景：合成马尔可夫链序列数据。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: Transformer（注意力机制 + MLP残差堆栈）。将注意力视为对MLP不动点的微扰算子，分析其相关（relevant）、边缘（marginal）或无关（irrelevant）性质。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成马尔可夫链序列数据（控制相关长度）。评价指标：残差损失、有效秩（effective rank）、扰动衰减谱（perturbation decay spectrum）、动态范围（dynamic range）。

Experiments: 在长相关序列上，注意力是强相关的（relevant），能闭合MLP无法弥补的残差损失缺口，驱动表示空间的相变；在短相关序列上，注意力是无关的（irrelevant），Transformer收敛到与MLP相同的损失和不动点几何。第一层第一个注意力头（L0H0）贡献了超过4倍于后续头的表示偏移。长相关下Transformer选择性保留慢马尔可夫模式（衰减长度动态范围5.4倍 vs MLP的1.3倍）。

Limitations: 仅使用合成马尔可夫链数据，未在真实图像/遥感数据上验证；纯理论分析，不涉及任何实际检测任务或部署约束；未讨论注意力机制对模型大小、推理速度的影响。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。但以下理论视角可能有间接启发：(1) 注意力在不同数据相关性下的"相关性"差异，提示在遥感场景中（如不同空间分辨率、不同地物尺度），注意力机制的行为可能不同，轻量化时需考虑保留哪些注意力头；(2) 第一层第一个注意力头主导表示偏移的发现，提示剪枝时可能优先保留浅层关键头；(3) 扰动衰减谱分析方法可用于评估轻量化模型（如剪枝后）的表示能力退化程度。但这些均为间接启发，需自行设计实验验证。

Abstract: Using the language of Wilsonian renormalization group theory (RG), we treat the Transformer's attention mechanism as a perturbation of the trained MLP residual-stack fixed point and ask whether it constitutes a relevant, marginal, or irrelevant operator. We derive a fixed-point shift formula and obtain four testable predictions for the fixed-point geometry, effective rank profile, layer specificity, and perturbation decay spectrum. Testing these on synthetic Markov chain sequences with controlled correlation length, we find: (1) For large chains(long correlation), attention is strongly relevant: it closes a residual loss gap the MLP cannot bridge and drives a phase transition in representation space, with effective rank jumping above input dimensionality at layer 1 and stabilizing at a high-dimensional plateau. (2) For short chains(short correlation), attention is irrelevant: the Transformer converges to the same loss and fixed-point geometry as the MLP, though it contracts perturbations faster. (3) The transition is dominated by the first-layer head (L0H0), which accounts for more than 4 times the representational shift of any subsequent head, consistent with the prediction that the relevant operator acts before the MLP begins integrating out positional variation. (4) Perturbation decay experiments reveal a regime reversal: in the long correlation regime the Transformer selectively preserves slow Markov modes (5.4 times the dynamic range in decay length vs. 1.3 times for the MLP); in the short correlation regime it suppresses all modes faster than the MLP, with no spectral selectivity. Together, these results show that the relevance of attention is not a property of the architecture but of the spectral structure of the data-generating process, and that a first-order RG perturbation framework provides a predictive account of that difference.

</details>


### [143] [Looped Latent Attention: Cross-Loop KV Compression for Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15456)
*James O' Neill,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Looped Latent Attention (LLA)，一种针对循环权重共享Transformer（looped, weight-tied Transformers）的KV缓存后训练压缩编解码器，利用循环间KV向量的低秩轨迹特性实现高倍率缓存压缩。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）推理时的KV缓存压缩，面向的是文本生成场景（MATH-500数学推理），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关领域，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然涉及模型压缩（缓存压缩），但压缩对象是LLM的KV cache而非遥感目标检测模型的网络参数或特征图，且部署场景是H200 GPU上的文本推理，非星上/边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不推荐优先阅读。除非对循环权重共享架构或KV缓存压缩技术有跨领域兴趣，否则可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM推理时的KV缓存压缩；应用场景：文本生成/数学推理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Looped, weight-tied Transformers（循环权重共享Transformer），即重复使用同一个block的Transformer架构。核心模块：Looped Latent Attention (LLA) — 一种后训练缓存编解码器，存储紧凑的K和V latent，在attention读取时重建循环特定的K/V向量。LLA-2D变体将head维度也折叠进一个latent实现极限压缩。

Lightweight method: 后训练缓存压缩（post-training cache codec），基于SVD初始化编解码器，通过KL散度和attention-output蒸馏进行精炼。压缩对象是KV cache而非模型参数或FLOPs。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏（用于蒸馏的是attention output而非模型参数蒸馏）、NAS等传统轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。实验在H200 GPU上进行，测量了batch capacity（4k上下文下从32序列提升到768序列，21.3x压缩）。未提供参数量、FLOPs、显存（除batch capacity外）、延迟、能耗等典型部署指标。与遥感卫星在轨部署场景完全无关。

Datasets and metrics: 评估数据集：MATH-500（数学推理）；模型：Ouro-1.4B, Ouro-2.6B-Thinking, Huginn-3.5B；指标：压缩率（21.3x, 32x）、MATH-500准确率（从0.43提升到0.66）、no-answer generations减少。对比方法：head-axis MLA、cross-layer sharing、KV quantization、final-loop reuse。

Experiments: 在Ouro-1.4B上，per-head LLA在匹配缓存预算下优于head-axis MLA、跨层共享、KV量化和最终循环复用。在Ouro-2.6B-Thinking和Huginn-3.5B上验证了跨模型迁移性。SVD编解码器在32x压缩下仍接近无损（decoder-independent evaluation）。在H200上，4k上下文下batch capacity从32序列提升到768序列（21.3x压缩）。长数学推理场景下，on-policy精炼将MATH-500从0.43提升到0.66。

Limitations: 1. 完全面向LLM文本推理场景，与遥感目标检测无关；2. 压缩的是推理时的KV cache而非模型参数或计算量，不减少模型FLOPs或参数量；3. 需要额外的编解码器存储和计算开销；4. 未讨论在资源受限设备（如星上FPGA/NPU）上的可行性；5. 仅验证了数学推理任务，未涉及视觉或遥感任务。

Ideas for my research: 虽然论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向弱相关，但以下思路可能有借鉴意义：1. 循环权重共享（weight-tied）思想可用于设计超轻量遥感检测网络，通过重复使用block减少参数量；2. 利用循环/递归结构中隐藏状态的"低秩轨迹"特性进行压缩的思路，可类比到遥感视频流或时序遥感数据中的特征压缩；3. 后训练编解码器+SVD初始化的方法可迁移到遥感检测模型的特征图压缩或中间层缓存压缩场景。但整体借鉴价值有限，因为核心问题域（LLM KV cache vs. 遥感目标检测模型）差异较大。

Abstract: Looped, weight-tied Transformers reduce parameters by reusing a block, but decoding still stores a separate K/V cache for every recurrence step. We show that this loop-indexed cache is highly structured. For a fixed token, layer and head, K/V vectors trace a short low-rank trajectory across loops, while the head and layer axes remain much flatter. We introduce Looped Latent Attention (LLA), a post-training cache codec that stores compact K and V latents and reconstructs loop-specific K/V vectors only when attention reads them. The default per-head codec compresses recurrence, while LLA-2D also folds heads into one latent for the extreme-compression regime. The codec is initialized from SVD of teacher activations and refined with KL and attention-output distillation. At matched cache budget, per-head LLA outperforms head-axis MLA, cross-layer sharing, KV quantization and final-loop reuse, showing that the recurrent cache is low-rank but not safely collapsible to a single state. The same axis advantage holds on Ouro-2.6B-Thinking and transfers to Huginn-3.5B, where an SVD codec remains near-lossless to 32x in decoder-independent evaluation. The cache reduction is exact. On one H200, the latent-store path increases measured Ouro-1.4B batch capacity at 4k context from 32 to 768 sequences at 21.3x compression. For long math rollouts, on-policy refinement on student-generated prefixes raises MATH-500 at 4x from 0.43 to 0.66 and reduces no-answer generations.

</details>


### [144] [Robust Peak-cost Constrained Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15457)
*Shilpa Mukhopadhyay,Sourav Ganguly,Santosh Mohan Rajkumar,Honghao Wei,Debdipta Goswami,Arnob Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究鲁棒峰值代价约束强化学习（RP-CRL），目标是最大化期望奖励同时控制轨迹中最大代价，提出基于积分概率度量的鲁棒值估计方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于强化学习中的安全约束优化问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化任何一方面。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为鲁棒峰值代价约束强化学习，应用场景为安全关键系统（safety-critical applications），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提出了一种基于积分概率度量（integral probability metrics）的鲁棒值估计方法，以及一个替代优化框架（surrogate optimization framework）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。实验部分仅提及有效执行安全约束并保持奖励性能。

Experiments: 实验表明所提方法在动力学扰动下能有效保证安全性，同时保持较强的奖励性能。未提供与遥感目标检测相关的对比实验。

Limitations: 论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供该方向的局限性分析。摘要本身未说明该方法在视觉任务上的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文研究方向（安全强化学习中的峰值代价约束）与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: We study robust peak-cost constrained reinforcement learning (RP-CRL), where the objective is to maximize expected reward while controlling the maximum cost encountered along a trajectory. This setting is motivated by safety-critical applications in which a single large violation can be catastrophic and therefore cannot be adequately captured by the standard CMDP framework based on expected cumulative cost. Existing reachability-constrained RL methods adopt Lagrangian-based approaches, yet the underlying duality properties of peak-cost constrained MDPs remain unclear. We show that, unlike standard CMDPs, peak-cost constrained MDPs may not admit zero duality gap. We further consider a robust formulation to address simulator-to-real-world mismatch in the transition dynamics. To solve this problem, we develop a surrogate optimization framework and a robust value estimation method based on integral probability metrics. We prove that, with appropriate hyperparameter choices, the surrogate solution attains the same robust reward value as the original problem while violating the constraint by at most epsilon. Experiments show that the proposed method effectively enforces safety under dynamics perturbations while retaining strong reward performance.

</details>


### [145] [ADS-C: Antidistillation Sampling for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15467)
*Khawaja Abaid Ullah,Mohammad Javad Khojasteh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ADS-C，一种针对分类任务的抗知识蒸馏防御方法，通过输入相关的梯度扰动保护教师模型不被对手通过查询接口蒸馏复制，且保证教师模型精度零损失。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于分类模型的知识蒸馏防御（对抗蒸馏攻击），属于AI安全/隐私保护领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：分类模型的知识蒸馏防御（对抗蒸馏攻击）；应用场景：AI安全、模型知识产权保护；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：CIFAR-10/100、Tiny-ImageNet上的通用图像分类。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。涉及教师模型（teacher）和学生模型（student）的蒸馏框架，以及基于梯度扰动的防御机制。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文研究的是如何阻止知识蒸馏（反蒸馏），而非利用蒸馏进行模型压缩。知识蒸馏本身是一种轻量化手段，但本文是从防御角度研究如何防止蒸馏。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet；评价指标：教师模型精度、学生模型精度（蒸馏后）、精度损失百分比。

Experiments: 在CIFAR-100上，ADS-C保护下学生模型精度损失17.4个百分点，而教师精度零损失；未修改的防御方法教师精度损失27.5个百分点。CIFAR-10上学生损失29.6 vs 教师损失32.9；Tiny-ImageNet上学生损失13.3 vs 教师损失22.2。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对分类任务，未扩展到目标检测等更复杂任务；2. 未在遥感/卫星图像上验证；3. 未讨论计算开销和实时性；4. 与轻量化/在轨部署完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但可反向思考：如果知识蒸馏防御技术成熟，未来在轨模型可能面临被对手通过查询接口蒸馏复制的风险，ADS-C的思路（零精度损失的防御）对保护星上模型知识产权有潜在参考价值。不过这与"轻量化"核心目标相悖。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation enables an adversary to replicate a proprietary classifier by querying its prediction interface and training a surrogate on the returned probability vectors. Antidistillation sampling, proposed for large language models, counters this threat with an input-dependent, gradient-directed perturbation of the served distribution; its transfer to classification has not been studied. Adapting the defense to classification, we show its behavior is governed by the distribution of the teacher's per-input confidence margins. Because well-trained classifiers are severely overconfident, the direct transfer exhibits an inert window: below a closed-form-predictable threshold, it affects neither attacker nor defender; beyond it, the defense undergoes a phase transition and degrades the teacher faster than the attacker's student. Temperature softening rescales the transition in closed form, and every temperature configuration lies on the same unfavorable trade-off curve. Our method, ADS-C, composes the perturbation under a closed-form, per-input margin budget that provably preserves every served top-1 prediction, so the defended teacher's accuracy equals the undefended teacher's identically. Under this guarantee the distilled student still loses 17.4 percentage points on CIFAR-100, 29.6 on CIFAR-10, and 13.3 on Tiny-ImageNet; matching this degradation with the unmodified defense costs 27.5, 32.9, and 22.2 points of teacher accuracy. Because served labels are unchanged, a hard-label attacker gains nothing, while the defended soft output trains a student up to 29.7 points below that floor: the incentive to distill served probabilities is not merely removed but reversed. To our knowledge, ADS-C is the first antidistillation defense for classification whose utility cost is exactly zero.

</details>


### [146] [Deep Learning Approaches for Sleep Apnea Classification from Polysomnographic EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15477)
*Shashank Manjunath,Mukesh Cheemakurthi,Aarti Sathyanarayana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用Vision Transformer和Graph Attention Network对多通道EEG信号进行儿童睡眠呼吸暂停自动检测，最佳AUC为0.750。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是睡眠呼吸暂停的EEG信号检测，属于生物医学信号处理领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关概念，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及模型轻量化或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：睡眠呼吸暂停自动检测（二分类/异常检测）；场景：临床睡眠诊断（polysomnography/EEG）；传感器：多通道EEG；目标类别：呼吸暂停事件（非视觉目标）。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: Vision Transformer (ViT)、Graph Attention Network (GAT)；输入表示包括原始时序信号、短时傅里叶变换(STFT)频谱图、基于相干性的图、拓扑数据分析(TDA)特征。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。论文关注的是临床部署可行性（clinical deployment challenges），而非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：2410名儿童受试者（训练集）+ 575名儿童受试者（测试集），进行了年龄和性别匹配；评价指标：AUC（最佳0.750），同时进行了按人口统计学（年龄、性别、AHI严重程度）和睡眠阶段（N1、N2、N3、REM）的分层分析。

Experiments: 对比了ViT和GAT在多种信号表示上的表现，ViT+TDA特征取得最佳AUC 0.750；进行了分层分析揭示性能差异。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无法提供该方向的局限性分析。论文自身局限性：仅使用单数据集（儿童）、AUC仅0.750（临床部署仍有挑战）、未讨论模型效率。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可参考的点：（1）ViT在非视觉信号上的应用思路，但遥感目标检测已有大量ViT工作；（2）多模态/多表示融合策略（raw signal + spectrogram + graph）的思路可迁移到遥感多模态（光学+SAR+高光谱）融合检测；（3）分层分析（按demographics/stages）的思路可迁移到遥感场景下的多尺度/多场景分析。但整体而言，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴意义很小。

Abstract: Sleep apnea diagnosis via polysomnography remains resource intensive and relies on time consuming manual data analysis and scoring. Recent work has demonstrated that central nervous system effects of sleep apnea events can be detected through electroencephalogram (EEG) signals. However, most work uses a single feature type on various datasets combined with different classification algorithms. In this work, we present a comprehensive comparison of deep learning architectures and feature representations for automated sleep apnea detection from multichannel EEG on a single dataset of pediatric subjects. We evaluate Vision Transformers and Graph Attention Networks across distinct signal representations: raw temporal signals, short-time Fourier transform spectrograms, coherence based graphs, and two topological data analysis (TDA) derived features. Using age and sex matching of our train and test sets, we train on 2410 pediatric subjects and test on 575 pediatric subjects. We achieve a best test AUC of 0.750 using a vision transformer based model trained on TDA features. Stratified analysis across patient demographics (age, sex, AHI severity) and sleep stages (N1, N2, N3, REM) reveals significant performance variation. Our results demonstrate the feasibility of EEG based automated OSA screening while highlighting essential challenges for clinical deployment.

</details>


### [147] [Inpainting Insights: Elevating Visual XAI with Photorealistic Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15482)
*Josef Lindl,Mariana Chaves,Damien Garreau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文改进LIME可解释性方法，用生成式图像修复替代传统扰动策略，生成更逼真的扰动样本以提升图像模型解释质量。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于XAI（可解释AI）中的扰动方法改进，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署中的任何一个核心主题。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时推理等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比消融细节。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可借鉴的技术点。本文关注的是模型可解释性，而非检测精度或效率。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接联系是：若在轨部署的轻量模型需要可解释性分析时，本文的生成式扰动方法可能提供参考，但这并非课题核心方向。

Abstract: The increasing complexity of state-of-the-art machine learning models has made their behavior progressively harder to interpret, spurring rapid advancements in the field of eXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI). Among many methods proposed, perturbation-based approaches play a major role. By systematically altering (perturbing) input features, these approaches measure the impact on the model's predictions. For image data, traditional perturbation techniques, often involve replacing pixel values e.g., with a pre-defined color. However, such approaches, but also more refined deterministic techniques, generate unrealistic out-of-distribution samples and often leave visible artifacts, which can mislead the model and compromise explanation quality. In this work, we adjust LIME, a widely used perturbation-based method, to demonstrate how generative inpainting can improve perturbation-based explanations for images. We achieve photorealistic perturbed samples that align better with the original data distribution and enhance explanation quality.

</details>


### [148] [An Auto-Scaling Approach for Serverless Environments Based on a Multi-Expert Consensus Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15511)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Mehrdad Ashtiani,Amirhossein Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向Serverless计算的无服务器自动扩缩容框架，通过依赖图分析、多模型集成预测和成本感知控制来优化资源管理。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是Serverless computing（无服务器计算）的自动扩缩容（autoscaling），属于云计算/系统领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。任务为Serverless应用的自动扩缩容，场景为云计算平台，不涉及遥感传感器或目标类别。

Model architecture: 使用MLP、LSTM、CNN三种轻量模型进行资源需求预测，通过performance-weighted probabilistic ensemble（基于性能加权的概率集成）融合多模型输出。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的"轻量"（lightweight MLP, LSTM, CNN）是指预测模型本身的轻量化，而非深度学习模型压缩方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。与遥感模型轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 使用真实工作负载轨迹（real workload traces）进行实验。评价指标包括预测准确率（99.88% prediction accuracy）、预测误差（prediction error）、基础设施成本（infrastructure cost）。

Experiments: 实验表明：1）有监督预测方法优于无监督聚类方法；2）所提集成方法预测准确率达99.88%，优于代表性混合预测方法；3）在多种云定价模型下均能降低基础设施成本并维持性能目标。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或目标检测内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但可抽象借鉴的思路：1）多模型集成（ensemble）思想可用于轻量化模型精度补偿；2）成本感知控制策略可类比星上资源（算力/功耗）约束下的推理调度。但整体借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Serverless computing provides automatic resource management and pay-per-use execution, but effective autoscaling remains challenging because of dynamic workloads, cold-start latency, and dependencies among functions. We present a dependency-aware autoscaling framework that integrates graph-based bottleneck identification, short-term workload forecasting, multi-model consensus, and cost-aware scaling control. Serverless applications are represented as directed dependency graphs, and structurally important functions are identified using weighted degree centrality. Resource demand is predicted using lightweight MLP, LSTM, and CNN models. Their outputs are combined through a performance-weighted probabilistic ensemble inspired by Bayesian model averaging. The controller further incorporates cold-start awareness and cost comparison to select among scale-up, scale-down, and hold actions. Experiments using real workload traces show that supervised forecasting substantially outperforms unsupervised clustering for autoscaling decision generation. The proposed ensemble achieves 99.88 percent prediction accuracy and reduces prediction error compared with representative hybrid forecasting methods. Evaluations across multiple cloud pricing models also demonstrate consistent infrastructure cost reductions while maintaining performance targets. The results show that combining dependency analysis, multi-expert forecasting, and cost-aware control provides a robust and practical solution for serverless autoscaling.

</details>


### [149] [Cache-Aware Prompt Compression:A Two-Tier Cost Model for LLM API Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15516)
*Yan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出缓存感知的提示压缩方法（CAPC），通过查询无关压缩配合缓存控制策略，在LLM推理中实现比纯缓存或查询感知压缩更低的成本，但完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）推理阶段的提示缓存（prompt caching）与提示压缩（prompt compression）成本优化，属于NLP/LLM推理优化领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM推理成本优化（prompt caching + compression）；应用场景：生产级LLM部署、企业工具助手、知识图谱RAG、零售基准测试；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 不适用。论文不涉及视觉模型或检测模型架构，讨论的是LLM推理系统的缓存架构（Anthropic Sonnet 4.6 API的两层缓存架构，hot/tier阈值约3500 tokens）。

Lightweight method: 不适用。论文讨论的是prompt compression（提示压缩）而非模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然"compression"一词在轻量化领域常见，但本文的压缩对象是文本提示（tokens），而非模型参数或计算图。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。论文完全不涉及星上部署、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件部署相关内容。讨论的是云端LLM API调用成本优化。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LongBench-v2（16个配置）、tau-bench retail benchmark（50个任务）；指标：成本节省百分比、缓存命中率（rho）、压缩比（r）、质量差异（与未压缩基线差距<0.05）。

Experiments: 在Anthropic Sonnet 4.6 API上实验：CAPC在LongBench-v2的16/16配置中最便宜，比纯缓存节省49%，比查询感知压缩节省64%，比原始未压缩节省90%；在企业工具助手（94k token schema前缀）上实现51.7%成本降低（r=3）；在知识图谱RAG管道上FastAPI实现9.3倍、httpx实现2.4倍成本降低；在tau-bench上CAPC是最便宜的策略且奖励与原始相同（36/50, p=1.00），而查询感知压缩反而比原始贵40.1%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化完全无关，没有任何可借鉴的视觉检测或轻量化技术内容。论文讨论的是LLM推理的文本提示压缩，属于完全不同的研究领域。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。虽然"缓存感知"（cache-aware）的设计思路在理论上可以类比到边缘部署中的特征缓存策略，但本文不涉及任何视觉检测或模型压缩技术，对遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的参考价值极低。

Abstract: Production LLM deployments combine two cost-reduction primitives: prompt caching (a discounted rate for re-used token prefixes) and prompt compression (fewer tokens sent). The compression literature has standardized on query-aware methods that produce a different compressed prefix per query, mechanically invalidating the prefix-strict cache on every call. We characterize this cost empirically on Anthropic's Sonnet 4.6 API and find caching is far from the rho=1.0 ideal the literature assumes: Sonnet's cache has a two-tier architecture with a sharp threshold near 3,500 tokens, below which the hit rate plateaus at rho~0.83 across 30-call sessions. Our cost model predicts, and experiments confirm, that under realistic rho, query-aware compression beats naive caching at high compression ratios (r>=6). We propose Cache-Aware Prompt Compression (CAPC), pairing query-agnostic compression with explicit cache_control plus a tier-preserving ratio bound that prevents over-compression from pushing the cached prefix into the hot tier. CAPC is the cheapest strategy in 16/16 configurations on LongBench-v2, with mean savings of 49% over cache-only, 64% over query-aware compression, and 90% over vanilla, at quality within 0.05 of the uncompressed baseline. We validate CAPC on three production workloads: an enterprise tool-using assistant with a 94k-token schema prefix (51.7% cost reduction at r=3); a graphify knowledge-graph RAG pipeline across two codebases (9.3x vs cache-all on FastAPI, 2.4x on httpx); and the public tau-bench retail benchmark (50 tasks), where CAPC is the cheapest of four strategies with reward exactly equal to vanilla (both 36/50, p=1.00) while query-aware compression is the most expensive at +40.1% over vanilla -- the first production confirmation of the crossover model's negative-ROI prediction on a public benchmark.

</details>


### [150] [Recursive Harness Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15524)
*Hyunin Lee,Jinglue Xu,Jeffrey Seely,Donghyun Lee,Matei Zaharia,Yujin Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出递归式框架自改进方法（RHI），通过优化用户构建的agent框架（harness）来提升执行轨迹质量，降低推理成本，但完全不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是大语言模型agent的框架自改进（harness self-improvement），不涉及遥感图像、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化网络、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、边缘部署等任何相关主题。虽然提到了"computationally lightweight"和"reduce inference cost"，但这是在LLM agent推理成本层面，与遥感在轨目标检测的模型轻量化完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM agent框架优化（harness optimization）；应用场景：定量金融、机器人、制药等合成机器学习研究任务；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 提出Recursive Harness Self-Improvement (RHI)方法，将agent框架表示为prompt级别的agent循环规范，通过成对反馈迭代优化。不涉及任何视觉模型或检测网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的"computationally lightweight"和"reduce inference cost by up to 60%"是指通过优化harness降低LLM agent的推理成本，而非模型压缩/加速方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。与遥感模型轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的内容。未提供参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署信息。

Datasets and metrics: 30个合成机器学习研究任务，涵盖定量金融、机器人、制药领域。评价指标：agent性能（performance ceiling）、推理成本（inference cost reduction up to 60%）。不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 在30个合成任务上，少量RHI迭代即可显著提升低推理努力agent的性能上限，超过最大推理努力设置，同时降低最多60%推理成本。消融实验表明性能提升主要来自改进的上下文管理（inter-agent信息流），而非更长的推理轨迹。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关。论文聚焦于LLM agent框架优化，不涉及视觉感知、遥感图像理解、目标检测或模型轻量化等核心问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文讨论的是LLM agent框架的自改进，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、评估指标上均无交集。

Abstract: Under model--harness co-evolution, harnesses are not merely inference-time scaffolds but data-generating components whose execution traces can shape future foundation models. This motivates harness-in-the-loop learning: optimizing harnesses for both immediate agent performance and the quality of traces used for future model training. However, continually updating provider-built scaffolds is costly and labor-intensive. We therefore investigate whether optimizing user-constructed harnesses in a task-specific manner can improve execution-trace quality while remaining computationally lightweight and requiring only a few update iterations. To this end, we introduce Recursive Harness Self-Improvement (RHI), which represents the harness as a prompt-level specification of the agent loop and iteratively refines it using pairwise feedback over its own revision history. Across 30 synthetic machine-learning research tasks spanning quantitative finance, robotics, and pharmacy, a few RHI iterations suffice to substantially raise the performance ceiling of low-reasoning-effort agents, exceeding the corresponding maximum-reasoning-effort setting while reducing inference cost by up to 60%. We show that these gains arise primarily from improved task-specific context management through more effective inter-agent information flow rather than longer reasoning traces. Finally, we formalize this behavior as an information-theoretic hypothesis for RHI's implicit optimization objective, suggesting RHI as a practical algorithm for continual learning within the paradigm of model--harness co-evolution.

</details>


### [151] [Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15525)
*Felippe Alves,Renato Vicente*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了KAN网络在语言模型中的可审计性和作为MLP替代方案的效果，发现小基KAN可用于审计但无一致性能优势。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于自然语言处理领域的KAN网络审计与MLP替代评估，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更未讨论任何轻量化部署相关技术（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等）。虽然文中提到了pruning（剪枝），但这是针对KAN网络内部边激活的审计性剪枝，与模型轻量化部署无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言模型（Language Model）中的KAN网络审计与MLP替代评估。应用场景：自然语言处理（BabyLM、Wikitext-103、BLiMP等NLP基准）。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: KAN (Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks)，B-spline KAN，MLP，SwiGLU，grouped Chebyshev，rational GR-KAN，MLPEdge，ClimbMix。核心是用一维可学习边函数替代固定节点激活。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到了pruning（剪枝），但这是针对KAN网络内部边激活的审计性剪枝（pruning the lowest-activity 20-25%），目的是分析网络行为而非模型压缩加速。未涉及量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等部署指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何星上/边缘部署相关信息。未提供参数量（除10M和286M两个规模外）、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等指标。虽然提到了10M参数和286M参数规模，但这是语言模型规模，与遥感目标检测模型无关。

Datasets and metrics: BabyLM（语言模型预训练数据集）、Wikitext-103（语言建模基准）、BLiMP（语法判断基准，59,875对）、EWoK（常识推理基准）。评价指标：validation loss、accuracy（BLiMP上62.4-63.1%）、NLS（非线性得分）、fPCA压缩覆盖率、closed-form-fit覆盖率。

Experiments: 主要发现：(1) 6层10M参数B-spline KAN中87.8%边超过NLS>0.1，0.4%不活跃；(2) 剪枝最低20-25%激活边损失可忽略；(3) 近全fPCA压缩和高闭式拟合覆盖率是低容量grid-2基的性质，非KAN通用行为；(4) KAN族和门控变体在BabyLM上优于GELU MLP，但该排序不迁移到标准化基准；(5) 参数匹配的MLPEdge在Wikitext-103上不如MLP；(6) 286M参数GR-KAN仍低于SwiGLU ClimbMix基线。

Limitations: (1) 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测领域；(2) 不涉及模型轻量化部署（剪枝仅用于审计而非压缩）；(3) 实验仅在NLP任务上，结论无法直接迁移到视觉/遥感任务；(4) 未提供任何推理速度、参数量效率、FLOPs等部署相关指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：(1) KAN网络作为一种替代MLP的结构，未来若在遥感目标检测的检测头或特征融合网络中使用，可关注其参数量效率；(2) 文中对边激活的审计方法（pruning low-activity edges）可类比为结构化剪枝思路，但需重新设计适用于遥感检测任务的剪枝策略；(3) 文中指出小基KAN的可审计性优势，但性能无一致提升，提示在遥感轻量化中不应盲目替换MLP为KAN而不做针对性优化。

Abstract: Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) replace fixed node activations with learned one-dimensional edge functions, offering an explicit interface for interpretation and a possible alternative to transformer feed-forward networks. We test these claims separately. In a six-layer, 10M-parameter B-spline KAN, we reconstruct all 884,736 feed-forward edges: 87.8\% exceed (NLS>0.1) and 0.4\% are inactive. Pruning the lowest-activity 20--25\% causes negligible loss increase, although structured MLP neuron pruning tolerates comparable sparsity. The audit replicates on BabyLM, but grid-size sweeps show that near-total fPCA compression and high closed-form-fit coverage are properties of the low-capacity grid-2 basis, not universal KAN behavior. For replacement, we evaluate MLP, SwiGLU, grouped Chebyshev, and rational GR-KAN networks on BabyLM. The KAN-family and gated variants improve validation loss over the GELU MLP, but this ordering does not transfer to standardized benchmarks: across ten seeds and 59,875 BLiMP pairs, accuracies span 62.4--63.1\%, EWoK remains at chance, and a (+0.7)-point GR-KAN effect on BLiMP reverses on the supplement. Larger tests are also cautionary: parameter-matched MLPEdge underperforms the MLP on Wikitext-103, and 286M-parameter GR-KAN remains below a SwiGLU ClimbMix baseline after stabilization. Thus, small-basis KANs provide a practical, corpus-transferable interface for auditing learned scalar transformations, but the tested replacements show no consistent benchmark, quality, or latency advantage over strong MLP baselines.

</details>


### [152] [Publicly-Verifiable Certificates for Statistical Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15528)
*Michael Ngo,Michael P. Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非交互式学习证明的概念，定义并构造了公开可验证的统计有效性证书（pvCSV），用于自适应统计查询（SQ）算法的学习结果验证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文属于机器学习理论（学习证明、统计查询模型）范畴，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何目标检测或轻量化网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何遥感或目标检测数据集及评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。无实验对比或消融研究。

Limitations: 该论文是纯理论工作，讨论的是学习算法的可验证性问题（proof of learning），与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，不具备任何直接或间接的可迁移性。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于计算学习理论方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Following Goldwasser, Rothblum, Shafer, and Yehudayoff, who defined a framework for interactive proofs of learning [ITCS'21], we initiate the study of non-interactive proofs of learning. We define and study a new notion: Publicly-Verifiable Certificates of Statistical Validity (pvCSVs), which allow for public, distributionally-robust certification that the result of a learning algorithm is valid. In a pvCSV, a learner publishes a hypothesis $h$ and corresponding certificate $π$; then, any user, who holds a user-specific distribution, can read the pair $(h,π)$ and determine efficiently whether the hypothesis is valid according to the user-specific distribution.
  We construct pvCSVs in the context of Adaptive Statistical Query (SQ) Algorithms. To certify SQ algorithms that makes $k$ adaptive queries, we construct pvCSVs where the sample complexity scales with $O(\log k)$, whereas the sample complexity of the best learning algorithms scale with $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{k})$. More generally, we study proof systems for learning in the SQ model, demonstrating the model's strengths as well as its limitations.

</details>


### [153] [From Feasibility to Desirability: Plan, Learn, Adapt (PLA) Framework for Personalized On-Device Itinerary Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15552)
*Himel Dev,Tanmoy Sen,Madhusudan Basak,Bashima Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PLA三阶段框架（Plan-Learn-Adapt），用于移动端个性化旅行行程生成，结合轻量规划器集成、Bradley-Terry奖励模型和可行性保持的本地精调，在保证100%可行性的同时提升行程完成率91%。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是**个性化旅行行程生成**（personalized trip itinerary generation），属于推荐系统/组合优化/移动端部署领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关概念，也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等检测任务。虽然涉及轻量规划器（lightweight planners）和移动端部署（on-device, latency），但其应用场景和任务类型与遥感在轨目标检测完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。仅当对"可行性保持的轻量部署框架设计"这一通用方法论感兴趣时可泛读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：个性化旅行行程生成（personalized trip itinerary generation）；应用场景：移动端旅游规划（FlyEnJoy App）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 三阶段框架PLA：Plan阶段（异构轻量规划器集成，生成结构多样的可行候选方案）→ Learn阶段（Bradley-Terry奖励模型，从成对比较中学习行程偏好）→ Adapt阶段（可行性保持的本地精调，在设备感知的计算预算内优化）。

Lightweight method: 涉及轻量规划器（lightweight planners）的异构集成，以及设备感知的计算预算（device-aware compute budget）控制。但这不是模型压缩意义上的轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS），而是指规划算法本身的轻量设计以适配移动端部署。

Onboard deployability: 移动端部署（on-device），报告了平均设备端延迟109.9 ms。但未报告参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等指标。注意：这是手机端部署，不是星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：2,519对人工成对比较（pairwise human comparisons），覆盖超过100个美国城市；评价指标：胜率（win rate）、可行性（feasibility）、留一城市交叉验证准确率（leave-one-city-out accuracy）、行程完成率提升（itinerary completion rate increase）。

Experiments: 奖励引导的集成规划器达到67.8%胜率，比最佳单一规划器高11.2个百分点，100%可行性。GPT-5、Claude Opus 4.5、Gemini 3 Pro在相同约束下可行性为0%。奖励模型在留出城市上平均留一城市交叉验证准确率67.6%。在FlyEnJoy生产部署中，行程完成率提升91%，平均设备端延迟109.9 ms。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 未涉及任何遥感图像处理、目标检测模型或轻量化压缩技术；3. 移动端部署与星上部署（资源约束、辐射环境、实时性要求）差异巨大；4. 未报告模型参数量、FLOPs等轻量化核心指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下思路可间接参考：1. "可行性保持"（feasibility-preserving）的设计理念——在轨检测中可类比为"保证每个中间状态都满足星上资源约束"的设计思路；2. 异构轻量规划器集成（heterogeneous ensemble of lightweight planners）的思想，可类比为多个轻量检测模型的集成策略；3. 设备感知计算预算（device-aware compute budget）的概念，可迁移到星上资源感知的模型调度策略。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Generating personalized trip itineraries is a complex planning task and involves a tension between hard combinatorial feasibility and soft latent desirability. Classical optimization enforces constraints but fails to capture subjective traveler preferences. While learning-based approaches model preferences, they cannot guarantee feasibility. Mobile deployment imposes additional resource constraints on both. To address this, we propose Plan, Learn, Adapt (PLA), a three-stage framework for personalized on-device itinerary generation. The Plan stage builds a heterogeneous ensemble of lightweight planners that produces structurally diverse feasible candidates. From pairwise itinerary comparisons, Learn fits a compact Bradley-Terry reward model that captures emergent schedule properties such as pacing, geographic coherence, and day balance, which per-POI signals miss. Finally, Adapt applies feasibility-preserving local refinement within a device-aware compute budget; every intermediate state is feasible by construction. On 2,519 pairwise human comparisons across more than 100 U.S. cities, the reward-guided ensemble achieves a 67.8% win rate, 11.2 percentage points above the best single planner, with 100% feasibility. Three frontier LLMs, GPT-5, Claude Opus 4.5, and Gemini 3 Pro, achieve 0% feasibility under the same constraints. The reward model generalizes across held-out cities, with a 67.6% mean leave-one-city-out accuracy. In production deployment within FlyEnJoy, PLA increased itinerary completion rates by 91%, with 109.9 ms average on-device latency.

</details>


### [154] [Hard Rules, Soft Preferences: Bridging Reasoning, Learning, and Optimization for Personalized Packing Checklist Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15562)
*Himel Dev,Madhusudan Basak,Tanmoy Sen,Paromita Shome,Bashima Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个面向航空旅行打包清单的推理引导学习框架，包含符号引擎、偏好学习和CP-SAT优化器三个阶段，用于生成合规且个性化的行李清单。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是航空旅行打包清单生成问题，属于推荐系统/约束优化领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：旅行打包清单生成；场景：航空出行；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：行李物品。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 三阶段框架：(1) 符号引擎生成合规种子清单；(2) 两阶段偏好学习器（梯度提升树+LambdaMART）估计包含效用和优先级效用；(3) CP-SAT优化器选择合规子集。不涉及任何视觉模型或检测网络。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到部署在iOS旅行应用FlyEnJoy中，但未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。该部署为手机端应用，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 604个标注旅行场景，包含29K包含标签和343K成对比较。评价指标：召回率99.7%、rubric有效性0.96、AUC-ROC 0.943、NDCG@5 0.923、约束满足率100%。

Experiments: 符号引擎对比前沿LLM（0.78-0.81 rubric有效性）；CP-SAT对比贪心选择（28%约束满足）和随机选择（10%约束满足）。实际部署后清单完成率翻倍，编辑和完成时间减少。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。该论文的约束优化方法虽可迁移到其他领域，但摘要未提供与视觉/遥感相关的任何信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文的符号推理+约束优化范式在理论上可迁移到遥感模型部署中的资源约束优化问题（如算力、功耗、带宽约束下的模型选择），但摘要未提供任何与视觉检测或轻量化相关的技术细节。

Abstract: Packing for air travel is recurring and error-prone: the checklist must be personal and context-aware, yet feasible under safety rules, item dependencies, and luggage limits. Existing packing assistants are template-driven and generic, or recommendation-driven but unconstrained, leaving users to manually patch regulatory and capacity violations. We propose a reasoning-guided learning framework with three stages: (1) a symbolic engine that generates a regulation-aware seed checklist with explicit dependency structure, (2) a two-stage preference learner that estimates inclusion and priority utilities from user add and remove actions while mitigating survivorship bias, and (3) a CP-SAT optimizer that selects a compact, compliant subset. The architecture instantiates a general pattern for constrained personalization, applicable wherever hard feasibility coexists with sparse preference signals. On 604 labeled trip scenarios, comprising 29K inclusion labels and 343K pairwise comparisons, the symbolic engine attains 99.7% recall and 0.96 rubric validity, compared with 0.78 to 0.81 for frontier LLMs. Gradient-boosted trees and LambdaMART reach an AUC-ROC of 0.943 and an NDCG@5 of 0.923. CP-SAT attains 100% constraint satisfaction, compared with 28% for greedy selection and 10% for random selection. Deployment in FlyEnJoy, a production iOS travel app, doubled checklist completions and reduced editing and completion time.

</details>


### [155] [Information-Directed Sampling for Causal Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15577)
*Muhammad Qasim Elahi,Murat Kocaoglu,Mahsa Ghasemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究因果bandit问题中不可操控变量的信息共享机制，提出Thompson Sampling和信息导向采样的因果变体，并给出贝叶斯遗憾界分析。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是因果bandit（因果多臂赌博机）的理论问题，属于强化学习/因果推断领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：因果bandit中的决策优化；应用场景：通用因果推断/强化学习；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。采用贝叶斯框架，以观测分布的CPT（条件概率表）为未知参数，提出Thompson Sampling和IDS的因果变体。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文无任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验在多个合成因果bandit任务上进行，评价指标为累积遗憾（regret），不涉及遥感数据集。

Experiments: 在合成因果bandit任务上，所提方法优于因果和非因果基线方法，能更有效利用跨干预的信息共享。

Limitations: 1. 假设已知因果图且无潜在混淆变量，实际场景中该假设可能不成立；2. 仅针对合成数据实验，缺乏真实应用场景验证；3. 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接联系是：因果推断中的信息共享机制或许可启发遥感多任务学习中的特征共享设计，但关联非常间接，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Causal bandits exploit structural relationships among variables to share information across interventions and accelerate the identification of high-reward decisions. In many applications, however, some variables cannot be directly manipulated, even though they influence the reward and provide useful information about the underlying causal system. We study contextual causal bandits with non-manipulable variables, where context variables are observed before action selection and additional variables are observed after each intervention. Assuming a known causal graph without latent confounding, we adopt a Bayesian formulation in which the conditional probability tables of the observational distribution constitute the unknown parameter. This representation allows observations collected under one intervention to update reward estimates for other interventions through their shared causal mechanisms. We develop causal variants of Thompson Sampling and Information-Directed Sampling (IDS) for this setting. For Thompson Sampling, we establish an entropy-dependent sublinear Bayesian regret bound. For IDS, we derive an entropy-dependent regret bound that explicitly quantifies the additional error introduced by Monte Carlo approximation of the expected regret and information gain; when these quantities are available exactly, the bound recovers the standard sublinear IDS rate. We further provide high-probability confidence bounds for the Monte Carlo estimates used by the algorithm. Experiments on several synthetic causal bandit tasks show that the proposed methods outperform causal and non-causal baselines by more effectively exploiting information shared across interventions.

</details>


### [156] [Rethinking Transfer in Continual Learning: A Replay-Based Realisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15587)
*Yang Meng,Zhenya Liu,Zhuokai Zhao,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向持续学习的迁移选择性回放(TSR)方法，通过零训练任务签名选择有益回放数据来提升前向迁移，而非无差别回放旧数据。
